## 核心收录

### 2. The Window Is in the Reasoning: When LLM Agents Decide to Follow Prompt Injections

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24048)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`prompt injection`、`branching rollouts`、`decision timing`、`monitor`

👤 **作者**：Haoxuan Jia、…、Hao Peng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：运行时监视器与单次运行基准共享假设：agent 是否跟随注入在读到注入时就已定——用分支 rollout 检验之
- 🔬 **研究方法**：精确重放到读注入步再多次重采样其余回合，把结局方差分为读取时固定与之后决定两部分；四 AgentDojo 套件×有无思考×重采样推理前缀
- 📌 **结论**：Qwen3 无思考时 0.91 方差在读取时已定、开思考降到 0.62；决策随推理逐渐形成（前缀重采样可见）；廉价 CoT 监视器漏掉已决定的大部分而更强监视器与前瞻监视器接近前缀决定量；单次运行标签与重跑至多 35% 不一致——基准须重复运行（注入跟随的时序解剖）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM agents that read untrusted content can be hijacked by prompt injections. Runtime monitors that try to catch a hijack before the agent acts, and benchmarks that score each task with a single run, share an assumption: whether an agent follows an injection is settled when the injection is read. We test this assumption with branching rollouts that replay an episode exactly up to the step at which the agent reads an injection and then resample the rest of the episode many times, splitting the outcome variance into a part fixed at read time and a part decided afterwards. Across four AgentDojo suites, a Qwen3 agent without thinking is largely decided at read time (0.91 of the outcome variance lies between states); with thinking on, this share drops to 0.62 (95% CI of the drop [-0.38, -0.22]), in the same direction for three attack templates and two success labels. How much is fixed at read time is a property of the model that the same measurement reveals, and thinking lowers the attack success rate of Qwen3 but raises it for two other models. Resampling from reasoning prefixes shows that the decision forms gradually as the agent reasons. Zero-shot chain-of-thought monitors built on cheaper models miss much of what is already decided, whereas stronger monitors, and a lookahead monitor that judges simulated next actions, come close to what the prefix determines. Finally, single-run labels of thinking agents disagree with a rerun up to 35% of the time, so benchmarks need repeated runs per task.

</details>

### 3. Red-Teaming Multi-Agent Attacks for Loss-of-Control Risks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24052)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`multi-agent attacks`、`loss-of-control`、`distributed attack`、`covertness`

👤 **作者**：John Kitaoka、Thao Amelia Pham、Carlos Rafael Giudice、Jacob Merizian

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 更有能力更持久并大规模部署——更多开始协作时，AI 控制措施须在它们就有害目标隐蔽合谋时仍然有效
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MiniLab：5 个互联服务+249 良性任务+8 攻击目标的合成基础设施；16 个人工优化攻击（权重渗出/无监视 rogue 部署/权限提升等）演示分布式攻击（不同 agent 执行分离攻击片段）与序列组合
- 📌 **结论**：GPT-5.6 Sol 逐个读轨迹只标记 7% 成功分布式攻击（单 agent 同任务 87%）；个体攻击可组合成更长失控序列而无一 agent 被标记；模型自规划的隐蔽攻击最多 25% 隐蔽成功（人工 80%）——最坏情况协调失控的基准化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI agents are growing more capable and persistent, and are being deployed in ever larger numbers. As more start collaborating, AI control measures must remain effective when they covertly collude on a harmful objective. We introduce MiniLab, a synthetic series of live services and codebases (5 interconnected services, 249 benign tasks, 8 attack objectives), adapted from internal AI deployment infrastructure. We demonstrate distributed attacks, in which different agents carry out separate attack fragments, and how their covertness evades frontier monitors when reading their trajectories individually. We show how distributed attacks spread across diverse contexts and harmful objectives through 16 human-optimized attacks (weight exfiltration, unmonitored rogue deployment, permission elevation, and more). A frontier monitor, GPT-5.6 Sol, reading each agent separately flags only 7% of successful distributed attacks, compared with 87% when a single agent carries out the same side task. Individual attacks can also be composed into longer attack sequences toward a greater loss-of-control outcome, without any individual agent being flagged. Finally, we tested whether models can plan these covert attacks: even with execution and monitor feedback, the best model-planned attacks succeed covertly against a single-trajectory GPT-5.6 Sol monitor in at most 25% of executions, compared with 80% for human-crafted plans, though this may scale with model capabilities. By turning worst-case coordinated attacks into reproducible red-team cases, our work provides a foundation for preventing locally benign agent actions from accumulating into system-level loss of control.

</details>

### 4. ToolRosetta: Surface-Form Invariance of Tool-Call Guards

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24055)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`tool-call guards`、`surface form`、`shell commands`、`guard validity`

👤 **作者**：Hasan Demirkiran、Jens Ernstberger

- 🎯 **研究动机**：步级工具 guard 在整洁 JSON 工具调用上评测——部署的编码 agent 经 execute_bash/execute_code 行动而载荷是字符串：表面形式变化下的 guard 稳定性未测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ToolRosetta：把 TS-Bench 的 AgentDojo/AgentHarm 每个候选动作重渲染为 Python 调用、shell 命令与无理由形式（代码产生+往返解析验证，请求/历史/标签/效果固定只变表面）
- 📌 **结论**：前沿与强开源 guard 稳定（≤3.4 点）；五个专用 agent guard 四个不稳：shell 形式下假阳性升 21-47 点（TS-Guard 在自家基准 +38 点，挡掉三分之一到九成良性动作）；guard 的 JSON 基准精度不能预测其部署工作点（表面形式不变性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Step-level guards for tool-using agents are evaluated on tidy JSON tool calls, but deployed coding agents act through execute_bash and execute_code tools whose payloads are strings. We introduce ToolRosetta, which re-renders every candidate action in the AgentDojo and AgentHarm splits of TS-Bench as a Python call, as a shell command, and without the agent's rationale. The renderings are produced by code and verified by round-trip parsing, so the user request, the history, the label and the action's effect stay fixed while only the surface form changes. Across eleven guards with two placebo controls, frontier and strong open-weight guards are stable: no frontier error rate moves by more than 3.4 points. Four of five specialized agent guards are not: under the shell form their false-positive rates rise by 21 to 47 points on AgentDojo, and TS-Guard's by 38 points on its own home benchmark, blocking between a third and nine in ten benign actions; the fifth, StepGuard, does not move. A small open guard drifts five points more permissive. Removing the agent's rationale moves most guards whose input carries it, in guard-specific directions. A request-blind control on one guard shows that the rationale is where intent is read from. A parser that restores the canonical call in front of the guard removes the effect for these renderings, by construction. Benchmark accuracy on JSON calls does not predict a guard's operating point behind a code-acting agent.

</details>

### 5. Under the Skin: Backdoor Infection in Multimodal Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24058)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`cross-modal backdoor`、`infection`、`shared latent space`、`MLLM`

👤 **作者**：Jiawen Wang、Ruo Yang、Haoran Dai、Binghui Wang、Ning Luo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MLLM 已在一模态被注入后门——能否不改不毒另一模态地构造其触发器（经共享潜空间激活恶意行为）？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：后门感染：从已感染模态的后门出发在另一『被感染模态』构造对应触发器（训练期不修改不投毒该模态）；轻量约束感知优化生成触发器
- 📌 **结论**：LLaVA/InternVL/Qwen-VL/Gemma 上跨模态触发器比直接优化更高效有效且更难检测——多模态系统的新威胁向量（跨模态后门迁移）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive performance in generating text conditioned on inputs from different modalities. In this work, we introduce backdoor infection, a cross-modal attack framework targeting MLLMs. Starting from a model already injected with a backdoor in one modality, our attack constructs a corresponding trigger in another modality, called the infected modality. Exploiting the model's shared latent space, the trigger in the infected modality reliably activates the malicious behavior, without modifying or poisoning the infected modality during training. We further develop a lightweight, constraint-aware optimization method for efficient and practical trigger generation. Extensive experiments across several state-of-the-art MLLMs, including LLaVA, InternVL, Qwen-VL, and Gemma, demonstrate that our approach identifies cross-modal triggers that are more efficient, effective, and less detectable than direct optimization baselines, highlighting a critical new threat vector in multimodal systems.

</details>

### 6. AM-Bench: A Unified Taxonomy and Evaluation Suite for Agentic Misalignment

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24264)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agentic misalignment`、`fraud triangle`、`benchmark`、`taxonomy`

👤 **作者**：Eric Zhang、Terry Jingchen Zhang、Chijioke Ugwuanyi、Jerick Shi、Bernhard Schölkopf、Zhijing Jin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agentic 错位=agent 选择有害行动而非失败——既有安全基准评是否服从显式有害指令，导致 agent 选择有害行动的条件理解不足
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AM-Bench：诈骗三角（威胁/机会/推理）框架的统一分类学+七行业 35 场景；威胁五类出自工具趋同论、机会七类出自 ESRR 分类、四问二元推理框架
- 📌 **结论**：十前沿模型跨场景 62 个百分点错位差距（Gemini 3 Flash/3.1 Pro 最高而 Claude Sonnet 4.6 全条件近零）——agentic 错位的理论接地下基准

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As LLM-based agents are deployed with increasing autonomy in real-world environments, a critical failure mode emerges: agentic misalignment, where agents choose harmful actions over failure. However, existing safety benchmarks primarily evaluate whether agents comply with explicitly harmful instructions, leaving the conditions that cause agents to choose harmful actions poorly understood. To address this limitation, we propose AM-Bench, a unified taxonomy and 35 scenario evaluation benchmark spanning seven industries for agentic misalignment grounded in the fraud triangle, a well-established criminological framework explaining goal-directed misconduct. We adapt this framework to agentic environments and construct our evaluation suite and detection systems along three dimensions: perceived threat, perceived opportunity, and reasoning. We operationalize each dimension into structured subcategories rooted in prior theory: five threat categories derived from the instrumental convergence thesis, seven opportunity categories drawn from the Emergent Strategic Reasoning Risks (ESRR) taxonomy, and a novel four-question binary reasoning framework synthesized from prior literature. Our evaluation of 10 frontier models reveals a 62-percentage-point gap in misalignment across scenarios, with Gemini 3 Flash and Gemini 3.1 Pro exhibiting the highest levels of agentic misalignment while Claude Sonnet 4.6 displays near-zero rates across all conditions. Our reasoning framework further reveals that models may exhibit harmful behaviours without explicit situational awareness or explanation, complicating both mitigation and detection. AM-Bench scenarios and code are available at this https URL

</details>

### 7. SPIDES: Information-Flow Control for Browser Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24277)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`information-flow control`、`browser agents`、`prompt injection`、`SOP`

👤 **作者**：Egor Zverev、…、Santiago Zanella-Béguelin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：浏览器 agent 读写任意页面内容——暴露于注入与数据渗出；恢复同源策略既不充分（同源公共汇可泄漏私有数据）也不必要（合法任务须授权跨源流）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SPIDES：以元素级 HTML 机密性/完整性标注传播的标签门控浏览器原语动作（输入/点击/导航）；不可信与机密内容从 agent 上下文掩蔽、策略阻断未授权流——安全保证独立于驱动 agent 的模型；MicroNet 28 站闭环生态配对良/对抗任务
- 📌 **结论**：设计上 0% 攻击成功率同时保持高任务完成率与自主性——信息流控制作为 web 安全到 agent 的有效扩展

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Browser agents automate complex workflows by reading and acting on arbitrary page content, but this flexibility exposes them to prompt injection attacks and unintended data exfiltration. Recent work argues that LLM-driven browser agents erode traditional web security boundaries such as the Same-Origin Policy (SOP) by acting across origins with the user's authority and no built-in isolation. We argue that restoring SOP is neither sufficient (a same-origin public sink can leak private data) nor necessary (legitimate tasks require authorized cross-origin flows) for protecting the security of users. We propose SPIDES, which instead enforces information-flow control by gating primitive browser actions such as typing, clicking and navigating on labels propagated from element-level HTML confidentiality and integrity annotations. Untrusted and confidential content is masked from the agent's context, while policies block unauthorized flows, providing security guarantees against both prompt injection and data exfiltration that hold independently of the model driving the agent. To evaluate SPIDES and baseline defenses under a common threat model, we build MicroNet, a closed ecosystem of 28 interlinked websites with element-level ground-truth labels and paired benign/adversarial tasks. SPIDES achieves 0% attack success rate by design while retaining high task completion rate and autonomy. Our findings demonstrate that information-flow control is an effective technique to extend web security to browser agents, gaining on autonomy and utility against state-of-the-art baselines. The source code and data are openly accessible at this https URL.

</details>

### 8. BanglaShield: A Script-Robust Safety Classifier and Benchmark for Bangla Native, Romanized, and Code-Mixed Prompts

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24288)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`Bangla safety`、`script robustness`、`romanization`、`guard model`

👤 **作者**：Mohsinul Kabir、Md. Tariquzzaman、Mohammad Rashidujjaman Rifat、Tharindu Ranasinghe、Nishat Raihan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：安全分类器建评于标准文字文本——孟加拉语等低资源语言常规以原生文字、拉丁罗马化与英语/印地/乌尔都混合书写
- 🔬 **研究方法**：BanglaShield：10,245 提示×四级分类×三种对齐书写形式（共享标签+组感知分割的严格配对比较）；六 LLM×36,882 分类查询+24,318 判定响应+紧凑微调编码器
- 📌 **结论**：LLM 分类器把 47.0% 不安全原生文字提示标为安全、罗马化升至 63.4%；三个多语 guard 放过 30.3-57.3%；微调编码器宏 F1 87.8/84.4 超最佳 LLM 42.8-46.1 点（参数少 ~130 倍）——书写形式鲁棒的孟加拉安全分类

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Safety classifiers for large language models (LLMs) are typically built and evaluated on standard-script text, yet Bangla, like many lower-resource languages, is routinely written in native script, in Latin-script romanization, and code-mixed with English, Hindi, or Urdu. We introduce BanglaShield, a resource and model for graded prompt-safety classification across these written forms. We consolidate 10,245 Bangla prompts from six sources under a four-level taxonomy (Safe, Offensive, Harmful, Severe) and render each prompt in three aligned forms that share labels and a group-aware split, enabling strictly paired comparisons. Benchmarking six contemporary LLMs, including a Bangla-specialized one, on 36,882 classification queries and 24,318 judged responses, we find that LLM classifiers label 47.0% of unsafe native-script prompts as Safe, rising to 63.4% under romanization, while response-level safety accuracy drops by 10.3 points. Three multilingual guard models also pass 30.3-57.3% of unsafe native-script prompts. Using a model's own classification as an input filter reduces harmful compliance by only about five points. In contrast, BanglaShield, a compact fine-tuned encoder, reaches 87.8 macro-F1 on native-script and 84.4 on romanized prompts, outperforming the best LLM on each form by 42.8-46.1 points with ~130x fewer parameters than Gemma 4 31B. We release the corpus, splits, judgments, and model.

</details>

### 9. Unbounded Losses, Unchecked Inputs: Measuring the Safeguards of LLM Options-Trading Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24289)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`trading agents`、`options`、`safeguards`、`measurement`

👤 **作者**：Mengxiao Wang、Nitesh Saxena

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM agent 已在零售券商下期权订单而开发者开源期权 agent 的速度快于任何人检验它们——既有工作只测学术股票设计的买卖翻转
- 🔬 **研究方法**：765 个 GitHub 仓库+券商文档+文献筛查得 215 合格仓库与 8 个券商原生接口；26 系统按 7 项鲁棒性+11 项安全防护逐格证据评分（含每个 ≥20 星项目与全部券商接口）
- 📌 **结论**：215 仓库中 198 个创建于 2026（129 个出自单一黑客松两周）；能下订单的 22 系统中 10 个有模型外预交易限制，但无一认证文本来源或交叉核验市场数据；17 个可无强制人工步骤执行、13 个可达无界亏损（含 8 券商接口中的 6）——写输入者可移动行权价/到期/波动率/腿而方向保持正确：期权 agent 须在订单边界强制亏损限制

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM) agents now place options orders at retail brokers, and developers publish open-source options agents faster than anyone has examined them. Prior security work on trading agents scored academic equities designs by whether an attacker can flip a buy-or-sell decision. We present the first measurement of the options agents that brokers and developers ship, scored by the safeguards they enforce and by the worst loss their documented order paths can reach. We screened 765 GitHub repositories, broker documentation, and the research literature and found 215 eligible repositories and 8 broker-native agent interfaces. We then scored 26 systems, including every broker interface and every open-source project with at least 20 stars, on seven robustness and eleven security safeguards with cell-level evidence. The ecosystem is new: 198 of the 215 repositories were created in 2026, and 129 (60.0%) appeared in the two weeks around one options-trading hackathon. Of the 22 systems that can place orders, 10 enforce a pre-trade limit outside the model, yet none of the 26 authenticate their text sources or cross-check market data. Of the 26, 17 can execute with no mandatory human step and 13 can reach unbounded loss, including 6 of the 8 broker interfaces. Because an attacker who writes the agent's inputs can move an order's strike, expiry, volatility, and legs while its direction stays correct, options agents need loss limits enforced at the order boundary.

</details>

### 10. RATION: Towards Quantitative Risk-Based Access Control for AI Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24311)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`risk-based access control`、`quantitative risk`、`agent security`、`security-utility`

👤 **作者**：Yuntao Du、…、Ninghui Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 安全系统大抵靠限制动作保护 agent——对复杂开放任务造成显著安全-效用折损；改为调节任务执行中累积的可测风险
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RATION：任务特定策略=用户请求显式约束+相似任务历史执行校准的隐式风险；执行期持续追踪累积风险并按风险类型施加不同强制策略
- 📌 **结论**：四安全基准上强安全同时跨模型环境保持高效用——AI agent 的定量风险基访问控制（从静态权限到累积风险治理）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI agents increasingly interact with external environments to complete complex user tasks. These interactions can lead to unexpected behavior and expose agents to security threats such as indirect prompt injection. Current agent security systems largely protect agents by restricting their actions, which can result in a substantial security-utility trade-off for complex, open-ended tasks. To address this, we propose a quantitative risk-based approach to access control for AI agents that regulates measurable risks accumulated during task execution. As a concrete realization of this approach, we introduce RATION, a security framework that tracks measurable risks associated with agent actions and enforces quantitative constraints throughout execution. RATION first constructs a task-specific policy by combining explicit constraints derived from the user request with implicit risks calibrated from historical executions of similar tasks. During task execution, RATION continuously tracks the accumulated risks and applies different enforcement strategies depending on the type of risk. Extensive experiments on four security benchmarks show that RATION provides strong security while maintaining high task utility across diverse models and environments.

</details>

### 11. Can Agents Keep Secrets They Were Never Told? Benchmarking Latent Privacy Risks Across Sessions with MemJoin

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24359)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`latent privacy`、`cross-session`、`memory combination`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Qishun Yang、Shu Yang、Difei Xu、Di Wang、Meng Ding

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长期记忆跨会话保留并组合用户信息——完成不需要敏感关系的普通任务时 agent 可陈述出敏感关系；要保护的关系与揭示它的线索组合在写入时可能都还隐藏（后续记录与任务未知）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MemJoin：288 个合成分析案例（完整历史蕴含敏感关系但每个单独会话都不充分）；六记忆系统×同一生成模型+全史/无史对照；分别检查目标属性值在存储记录与检索上下文的出现及最终答案是否陈述完整人关联关系
- 📌 **结论**：预定义子集的配对测试中移除共享标识符等连接线索可改变完整关系输出而全部被检目标属性值仍可见——仅查属性可见性不足以评估不同会话记录共同揭示什么（跨会话潜在隐私）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

To support sustained interactions and personalized assistance, agents use long-term memory to retain and combine user information across sessions. Reusing such memories can lead an agent to state a sensitive relation while completing an ordinary task that does not require it. The relation to protect and the combination of clues that reveal it may remain hidden at memory write time, when later records and tasks are still unknown. In this paper, we introduce MemJoin, a privacy benchmark of 288 synthetic analysis cases in which the complete history entails a sensitive relation, but every individual session is insufficient. We evaluate six memory systems with the same answer-generating model, alongside full-history and no-history controls. Each system writes and updates memories in session order and later retrieves context for an ordinary task, while target annotations remain with the evaluator. We separately check which target attribute values appear in stored records and retrieved context, and whether the final answer states the complete, person-linked relation. In a separate paired test on a prespecified subset, removing shared identifiers and other connecting clues can change complete-relation output while all checked target attribute values remain visible. These findings show why checking attribute visibility alone is insufficient to evaluate what records from different sessions reveal together.

</details>

### 12. Defending Against Dormant Poisoning Attacks Across Language and Multimodal Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24379)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dormant poisoning`、`benign fine-tuning`、`safety buffer`、`agent`

👤 **作者**：Wonjun Lee、…、Mohit Bansal

- 🎯 **研究动机**：休眠投毒在发布时隐藏、良性微调后显现——基础模型作 agent 后是否扩展到有害 agent 动作与多模态设置未知；既有防御依赖外部安全响应或可信干净参照（agent 动作难获得）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两 agentic 风险：有害行为经良性微调扩展到未见动作+多有害行为可共存被联合激活；利用『攻击者须在发布时保持安全行为以隐藏投毒』的性质自动构造 Safety Buffer（有害输入配发布模型响应+同模型生成的去意图良性版本）→LookAhead Defense 预览良性微调更新并只惩罚预测会削弱安全或增加拒绝的更新
- 📌 **结论**：免外部安全响应与干净参照的休眠投毒防御，跨语言与多模态 agent——发布时安全行为本身作安全参照（supply-chain 休眠威胁的推广与防御）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recent work shows that attackers can implant dormant malicious behaviors that remain hidden at release but emerge after benign fine-tuning. As foundation models are used as agents, whether dormant poisoning extends to harmful agent actions and multimodal settings remains unclear. We show two agentic risks: (1) harmful behaviors activated by benign fine-tuning can extend to unseen actions; (2) multiple harmful behaviors can coexist and be jointly activated by benign downstream fine-tuning. Existing defenses often rely on external safe responses, which are hard to obtain for agent actions, or a trusted clean reference model. We exploit a key property of dormant poisoning: the attacker must preserve safe behavior at release to conceal poisoning, and this behavior can serve as a safety reference. Leveraging this property, we automatically construct a Safety Buffer pairing harmful inputs with the released model's responses and benign versions generated by the same model to remove harmful intent. The Safety Buffer can replace external safe responses in existing defenses, but these defenses do not distinguish updates that weaken safety from those that preserve it, often increasing over-refusal or reducing utility. To address this limitation, we introduce LookAhead Defense, which previews benign fine-tuning updates using the Safety Buffer and penalizes only updates predicted to weaken safe behavior on harmful inputs or increase the likelihood of refusals on benign versions in the Safety Buffer. Across language models, language agents, and multimodal agents, LookAhead Defense reduces harmful behavior in agents from up to 93.9% to at most 14.2% and harmful responses in language models from up to 92.5% to at most 2.0% versus vanilla fine-tuning, while maintaining competitive utility and low over-refusal. It remains effective against other poisoning methods.

</details>

### 13. ConCI: A Controlled Counterfactual Benchmark for Contextual Integrity in LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24494)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`contextual integrity`、`counterfactual triplet`、`benchmark`、`refusal bias`

👤 **作者**：Juhyeon Park、Sumin Yu、Taesup Moon

- 🎯 **研究动机**：情境完整性主张共享是否适当取决于信息流语境——既有 CI 基准同时变多个 CI 参数且被不当流主导，正确预测反映语境敏感还是普遍拒绝倾向不明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ConCI：锚定流+各改单个 CI 参数的两个反事实构成三元组、三者全对才给分的受控反事实基准（六既有基准构建 1,592 流/556 三元组、近标签平衡、每流七人标注）
- 📌 **结论**：七 LLM 上许多正确锚定预测在至少一个反事实上失败、最强模型也错约五分之一三元组；不当流近天花板而适当流更弱（语境改变应使适当化时仍判不当）；更大模型收窄但不消除非披露偏差——CI 的受控反事实评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Contextual integrity (CI) holds that the appropriateness of sharing personal information depends on the context of the information flow, not on the information alone. However, existing CI benchmarks often use independent scenarios that vary multiple CI parameters simultaneously and are dominated by inappropriate flows, making it unclear whether correct predictions reflect sensitivity to context or a general refusal tendency. We present ConCI, a controlled-counterfactual benchmark in which an anchor flow is paired with two counterfactuals that each modify a single CI parameter. The resulting triplet is scored jointly, with credit given only when all three flows are judged correctly. Built from six existing benchmarks, ConCI contains 1,592 flows in 556 triplets, is approximately label-balanced, and is validated by seven human annotators per flow. Across seven LLMs, many correct anchor predictions fail on at least one counterfactual, and even the strongest model errs on about a fifth of triplets. Furthermore, models perform near ceiling on inappropriate flows but weaker on appropriate ones, often continuing to judge a flow inappropriate even when a contextual change should make it appropriate. Larger models narrow this bias toward non-disclosure but do not eliminate it, and reasoning yields only modest gains.

</details>

### 14. CoTs remain monitorable when being unfaithful requires computation: Replicating and Extending the Necessity Effect Across Eleven Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24495)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`CoT faithfulness`、`necessity effect`、`replication`、`monitorability`

👤 **作者**：Arav Dhoot、Yixiong Hao、Zephaniah Roe

- 🎯 **研究动机**：CoT 内容不总反映推理过程——Emmons et al. 2025 的必要性效应（提示线索足够复杂时计算被迫进入 CoT）只在 Gemini 上测过且数据代码闭源
- 🔬 **研究方法**：六家族十一模型复现代理任务+扩展到五线索族（四新）×三难度级测 CoT 必要性与不忠实
- 📌 **结论**：必要性效应泛化：每个模型都高于无提示基线地跟随简单线索而隐藏的复杂线索跟随回落近基线；不忠实何时出现的性质随任务与模型而变（某些线索单次前缀可解码时模型也常口头化跟随）——不安全行为需非平凡计算时 CoT 监控仍站得住（监控有效性的复制研究）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

While a LLM's chain of thought (CoT) is a promising approach to interpreting model outputs, prior work shows CoT's content does not always accurately reflect the reasoning process. Emmons et al. (2025) finds that models can follow a question hint without revealing so in its chain of thought, but when the hint is sufficiently complex the computation is forced into the CoT. This claim, however, has only been tested on Gemini models, and the datasets and code is closed source. We replicate their proxy task across eleven models from six families and find the necessity effect generalizes. Every model follows simple cues above a no-hint baseline, while concealed complex-cue following falls back near baseline for most models. We also extend the study from Emmons et al. (2025) to measure CoT-necessity and unfaithfulness across five cue families (four of them new) and three difficulty levels. The relationship between when CoT is necessary and unfaithfulness is task-specific and model-specific. For some hint types, models often verbalize their hint-following behavior even when the hint can be decoded in a single forward pass. These results support the case for CoT monitoring when unsafe behavior requires non-trivial computation, and that properties of CoT unfaithfulness are sensitive to model and setting.

</details>

### 15. When Code Becomes an LLM Call: An Empirical Study of Call-Site Contracts in Agentic Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24545)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`call-site contracts`、`agentic systems`、`empirical study`、`LLM calls`

👤 **作者**：Shihao Weng、…、Xiaofei Xie

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agentic 系统把选下一工具/判动作是否允许/写要运行的代码等逻辑替换为 LLM 调用——程序仍依赖这些逻辑必须保证的东西但模型行为不由代码固定、跨运行变化且可被输入操纵：保证须来自每个 LLM 调用点周围的程序（前置条件+提示约束+后置条件的契约）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：40 个流行开源 agentic 系统（13.4MLOC 四语言）496 个 LLM 调用点的实证：多 agent 管线（17,428 人判验证）追踪每个模型输出的去向、无码推导期望契约并与代码实现的契约比较
- 📌 **结论**：48% 的 LLM 输出到达控制流或执行且其中 73% 永不到人；程序在代码中强制 78% 的约束但提示中 67% 的约束只是请求模型；82% 调用点有调用前后检查而 63% 期望约束未满足、仅 5.3% 调用点满足全部契约——十个复发模式解释 99% 缺口（agentic 软件的契约工程）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Software quality has long rested on specifications that state what code must do, and on testing and verification that check that the code does it. Agentic systems change this foundation: they replace parts of their logic, such as choosing the next tool, judging whether an action is allowed, or writing code to run, with calls to a large language model (LLM). The program still relies on what this logic must guarantee, but the model's behavior is not fixed by code, varies across runs, and can be steered by its input. The guarantee must therefore come from the program around each LLM call site, as a contract of pre-conditions, prompt constraints, and post-conditions. We present an empirical study of these contracts in 496 LLM call sites of 40 popular open-source agentic systems (13.4 MLOC, four languages). Using a multi-agent pipeline validated by 17,428 human judgments, we trace where every model output flows, derive an expected contract for each call site without access to its code, and compare it with the contract that the code implements. We find that 48% of LLM outputs reach control flow or execution, and 73% of these never reach a person. Programs enforce 78% of their constraints in code, but in the prompt, 67% of the constraints are only requested from the model. Although 82% of call sites are checked before and after the call, 63% of the expected constraints are unmet and only 5.3% of call sites satisfy their whole contract; ten recurring patterns explain 99% of the gaps. 38 mechanisms behind these gaps trigger at runtime, and a small code fix that enforces the expected constraint stops all 38. Our results show that the presence of checks says little about their adequacy, and we derive implications for agent developers, framework maintainers, and researchers.

</details>

### 16. When Better Policies Leave Harder Failures: Coverage Shift in VLM Success Monitoring

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24801)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`coverage shift`、`VLM success monitoring`、`conditional detectability`、`robot learning`

👤 **作者**：Isabel Guan、Dingyuan Zhang、Shipeng Lyu、I-Ming Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：更好的机器人策略会更难监控吗？——固定 VLM 判成功而策略改进：学习改变哪些失败到达固定判官（覆盖偏移）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：受控 20 种子托盘放置实验（训练只选仿真器验证的成功）：成功率 66.8%→97.0%、被接受悬停失败从 5.0%→0.9%，但判官接受 50.0% 的残余悬停（未训练采样下 31.1%）；边界探针+九判官 1,392 刺激的广泛诊断+八设置判官过滤实验
- 📌 **结论**：残余失败更常在托盘沿下（可藏抓取立方体下的缝隙）——任务改进与失败可检性须分开：报告绝对漏检/条件漏检/审计覆盖率三者（监控有效性随被监控者进步而退化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Can a better robot policy become harder to monitor? We study a fixed vision-language model (VLM) that judges success while a policy improves. In a controlled 20-seed tray-placement experiment, training selects only simulator-verified successes. Success rises from 66.8% to 97.0%, and accepted hover failures fall from 5.0% to 0.9% of executions. Yet the judge accepts 50.0% of remaining hovers, versus 31.1% under untrained sampling. These residual failures more often lie below the tray rim, which can conceal the gap beneath a grasped cube. A separate boundary probe accepts 148/160 below-rim hovers and 0/160 above-rim hovers. We call this change in conditional monitoring sensitivity coverage shift: learning changes which failures reach a fixed judge. A broader diagnosis covers nine judges and 1,392 stimuli. Judge-filtered experiments across eight settings further show heterogeneous effects of state-dependent acceptance. The results distinguish task improvement from failure detectability and motivate reporting absolute misses, conditional misses, and audit coverage together.

</details>

### 17. Pair-Aware Optimization: Coordinating Exploration and Refinement for Jailbreaking LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24823)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent jailbreak`、`ZO-FO hybrid`、`pair-aware optimization`、`stochastic decoding`

👤 **作者**：Juanyang Xu、Zheng Wang、Xingyu Zhao、Siddartha Khastgir、Andi Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：优化式 agent 越狱分一阶（梯度）与零阶（函数值反馈）两路——互补性与优化次序未被探索
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四策略（纯 ZO/纯 FO/ZO→FO/FO→ZO）系统研究+ZO 探索对抗前缀×FO 精炼后缀的框架；Pair-aware 保留多前缀候选条件精炼后缀并在前缀-后缀对间选择；一致随机解码覆盖同输入多样推理轨迹
- 📌 **结论**：恶意指令与恶意环境基准上混合优化超单优化器且次序实质影响性能——agent 越狱的优化器组合学（攻击方法论）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Model (LLM) agents are vulnerable to adversarial inputs that induce attacker-specified malicious behaviors, commonly referred to as jailbreaking. Existing optimization-based jailbreak attacks primarily employ first-order (FO) methods that leverage gradient information or zeroth-order (ZO) methods that rely on function-value feedback. However, their complementarity and optimization ordering remain underexplored. We systematically investigate four strategies: pure ZO, pure FO, ZO->FO, and FO->ZO, and develop a ZO-FO framework that explores adversarial prefixes through ZO optimization and refines their corresponding suffixes through FO optimization. To coordinate the two processes, we introduce Pair-aware optimization, which retains multiple prefix candidates, conditionally refines their suffixes and selects among the resulting prefix-suffix pairs. We further investigate agent jailbreaking from a more general stochastic-generation perspective, where the same adversarial input can induce diverse reasoning trajectories and actions that a single greedy output cannot capture. Accordingly, we adopt consistent stochastic decoding during both optimization and evaluation. Experiments across malicious-instruction and malicious-environment benchmarks demonstrate that hybrid optimization improves attack effectiveness over single-optimizer methods, while optimization order substantially influences performance.

</details>

### 18. Consistency Is the Attack Surface: Detecting Coordinated Retrieval Poisoning with Embedding Geometry

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24834)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`coordinated poisoning`、`embedding geometry`、`RAG defense`、`attack fingerprint`

👤 **作者**：Zeyan Li、Qirong Guo、Jianfeng Xu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：基于一致性的 RAG 防御依赖『稀疏对手』假设——投毒战役可只改语料可忽略比例却把段落集中进读者上下文：一致性本身成为攻击面
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四 NQ 检索种子：≥94.1% 目标行有注入进 top-5、52.9% 形成检索多数；协调留下指纹=证据嵌入几何中稠密查询锚定分量——CoordGuard 从鲁棒归一化的查询加权亲和审计该结构（免毒标签/正确答案/读者梯度），always-filter 或良性校准门
- 📌 **结论**：e5 always-filter 把暴露条件 ASR 降到 0.167（代价 13.6 点干净）；Contriever 门保 0.315 干净于 ASR 0.331；跨两读者三任务多检索器-审计者对迁移——协调投毒的几何指纹检测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Consistency-based defenses for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) rest on an assumption that a sparse adversary breaks: a poisoning campaign may modify a negligible fraction of the corpus, yet retrieval concentrates its passages into the reader's context. Across four Natural Questions retrieval seeds, at least one injection reaches the top-5 in 94.1% of targeted query--seed rows, and all three form a retrieved majority in 52.9%. Under our short-answer protocol, RobustRAG and ReliabilityRAG reach exposure-conditional attack success 0.512, against 0.576 for undefended RAG, while lowering clean accuracy from 0.328 to 0.216. Coordination, however, leaves a fingerprint: a dense, query-anchored component in the evidence embedding geometry. CoordGuard audits this structure from robustly normalized query-weighted affinities, without poison labels, correct answers, or reader gradients. Its always-filter mode suppresses the selected component whenever one forms; a benign-calibrated gate suppresses it only at unusually high scores and traces a robustness--utility frontier. Pooling four retrieval seeds, e5 always-filtering reduces $\mathrm{ASR}_{\mathrm{exp}}$ to 0.167 overall and 0.138 on parametrically unknown queries, at a 13.6-point clean cost, while a Contriever gate retains clean accuracy 0.315 at $\mathrm{ASR}_{\mathrm{exp}}=0.331$. The reduction transfers across two readers, three tasks, and multiple retriever--auditor pairs, and count-matched controls attribute it to component-targeted removal rather than deletion volume. These findings recast poison detection from judging truth to auditing the structure of retrieved evidence.

</details>

### 19. The Missing Compliance Class: LLM Graders That Collapse Jailbreak Success, and a Free Check

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24872)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`ASR validity`、`grader collapse`、`compliance class`、`reporting standard`

👤 **作者**：Ruqiya Bin Safi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：越狱论文报告的 ASR 被当作目标模型属性——固定目标模型/提示/响应只换评分器：三个 8-9B 稠密评分器把合规响应移到相邻『逃逸』类而非散布（其一 900 响应零次给合规标签）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：类坍缩在新有害请求/第三族目标/第四族 held-out 7B 评分器上复现；每类标签计数零成本暴露；两语料 900 响应×八评分器
- 📌 **结论**：合规率跨评分器差 22-25 倍（非拒绝率差 18-23 点）；去掉坍缩评分器后合规率仍差 1.7-2.8 倍——报告最低标准=合规率+每类标签计数+具名评分器（ASR 测量的评分器伪影）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Jailbreak papers report an attack success rate, the share of responses that deliver the harmful request, and read it as a property of the target model. We hold the target model, the prompts and the responses fixed and change only the grader. Three dense graders of 8B and 9B parameters, from three model families, move compliant responses to the neighbouring class, evasion, instead of scattering them across classes. One of them assigns the compliance label zero times in 900 responses. This class collapse also appears on new harmful requests, on a target model from a third family, and in a held-out 7B grader from a fourth family. Per-class label counts expose it at no cost and without human labels. The collapse decides how the rate behaves under a change of grader. On two corpora of 900 responses and eight graders, the compliance rate differs by a factor of 22 to 25, while the non-refusal rate differs by 18 to 23 percentage points. Without the three collapsing graders, compliance differs by a factor of 1.7 to 2.8 on all four corpora, and non-refusal still differs by 10 to 22 points. These results do not change when every grader is called once. We propose a reporting minimum that keeps the compliance rate and adds its per-class label counts and the named grader. Beside it goes the non-refusal rate, whose disagreement across graders is additive.

</details>

### 20. The One-Pixel of Reasoning: One Truthful Relation Is Enough to Mislead LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24873)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`one-relation attack`、`implicit warrant`、`belief manipulation`、`cognitive attack`

👤 **作者**：Jinwei Hu、Youcheng Sun、Yi Dong、Xiaowei Huang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：一句话能否通过瞄准连接证据与结论的单个隐含保证来操纵 agent 信念？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ORA：定位关键隐含保证+用单句源接地的限定句干预并引向错误信念；十八模型×生物医学推理与漏洞检测+无反馈迁移与多 agent 辩论压力测试
- 📌 **结论**：best-of-10 攻击翻转初始正确答案：专有 54.9%/开源 80.0%；成功句子可通过源支持检查并逃过识别；多 agent 辩论把团队错误从 50.0% 抬到 59.5%——隐含保证作为操纵 agent 信念的认知攻击面

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Can one sentence manipulate an agent's belief by targeting a single implicit warrant linking evidence to a conclusion? In this paper, we introduce the one-relation attack (ORA), a novel cognitive attack on this inferential relation. ORA localizes a critical implicit warrant and performs a one-relation intervention using a single source-grounded sentence that qualifies this warrant and steers the agent toward an incorrect belief. Across eighteen models on biomedical inference and vulnerability detection, best-of-$10 attacks overturn initially correct answers at mean rates of 54.9% for proprietary and 80.0% for open-weight models. Attacks also transfer without victim feedback, and paired controls support the roles of targeting and qualification. Moreover, successful sentences can pass source-support checks and evade identification, and multi-agent debate raises team error from 50.0% to 59.5% in our balanced stress test. These findings establish implicit warrants as a cognitive attack surface for manipulating agents' beliefs.

</details>

### 21. Peer Pressure: Execution-Verified Co-Evolution of Attacker and Defender Populations for Code Security

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24896)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`co-evolution`、`execution-verified`、`code security`、`PRPO`

👤 **作者**：Khushbu Pahwa、Yubin Ge、Daniele Bonadiman、Xiaofei Ma、Yi Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 代码 agent 几乎无监督地发布代码——静态漏洞数据集训练的防御者落后于适应中的对手
- 🔬 **研究方法**：攻守共训且执行决定每个奖励：Red 编辑真实仓库（补丁须应用/构建/过测/完成任务/在隔离容器复现真实利用，路径同时标注入口点/关键操作/轨迹）；PRPO 使两侧为冻结基座上的 LoRA 适配器群体、对同条件同角色同伴记账（群体进策略梯度基线而非对手池）+锦标赛保多样
- 📌 **结论**：匹配抵消条件难度（等覆盖下值 2.3 点）；7B diff 审查器四未见应用三未训语言 64.8% 平衡准确率（超监督安全微调 5.5 点、超冻结 Claude Opus 4.8 4.4 点）；9B 仓库 agent 恢复 23/172 held-out 通告（监督 10/未训 7）——执行验证的攻防共演化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM code agents increasingly ship code with little oversight, yet defenders trained on static vulnerability datasets fall behind adapting adversaries. We co-train attacker and defender instead, and let execution decide every reward: an attacker (Red) edits a real repository and earns nothing unless its patch applies, builds, passes the tests, completes its development task and reproduces a real exploit in an isolated container, whose path also labels the entry point, the critical operation and the trace between them. Peer-Relative Policy Optimization (PRPO) makes both sides populations of LoRA adapters over one frozen base and credits each against same-role peers that faced an identical condition, placing the population inside the policy-gradient baseline rather than the opponent pool, with a tournament preserving diversity. Matching cancels the condition's difficulty: at equal coverage it is worth 2.3 points of balanced accuracy over a baseline computed on other conditions, and it matches a within-policy control at half the verifier spend. PRPO's 7B diff reviewer reaches 64.8% balanced accuracy on four unseen applications in three untrained languages, 5.5 points above supervised security fine-tuning and 4.4 above a frozen frontier reviewer (Claude Opus 4.8), which it leads by 15.8 on the application it trains on. Its 9B repository agent recovers 23 of 172 held-out VulnGym advisories, against 10 after supervised fine-tuning and 7 for the same backbone untrained under the same scaffold, and locates 17 of 43 reachable entry points against 13 for mini-swe-agent with GLM-5.2. Run unchanged on jailbreak defense, the same algorithm matches or exceeds WildGuard's static F1 at equal corpus and model size. Under a judge distinct from its training oracle it cuts attack success from 36.0% to 4.6%, against WildGuard's 8.9%.

</details>

### 22. SoK: PIAtlas: Decomposing the Unbounded Prompt Injection Attack Space

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24999)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`SoK`、`prompt injection taxonomy`、`attack space`、`benchmark coverage`

👤 **作者**：Stavros Damianakis、Konstantinos Psounis

- 🎯 **研究动机**：注入是 LLM 应用与 agent 的主导安全风险——20+ 公开数据集用不相容词汇（按投递向量/攻击者目标/生成方法组织）描述注入而领域仍缺如何构造攻击的连贯地图
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PIAtlas：按『注入指令如何被构造以颠覆底层模型』组织的分类学（六类各对应一种颠覆机制为根的层次）；从 LLM 训练/提示/人类式框架影响推导+历史攻击数据比较；PICorpus 最大许可干净的注入数据集合集（人验协议映射数千记录）
- 📌 **结论**：攻击常组合少数机制的多种技术；广泛使用的 agent 基准只测攻击空间的一部分而自适应攻击者偏爱策展基准低代表机制——注入攻击空间的系统化与基准覆盖诊断

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Prompt injection is the dominant security risk for large-language-model (LLM) applications and agents, yet the field still lacks a coherent map of how these attacks are constructed. More than twenty public datasets describe prompt injection using incompatible vocabularies, often organized around delivery vector, attacker goal, or generation method. In this Systematization of Knowledge, we present PIAtlas, a taxonomy organized instead around how the injected instruction is constructed to subvert the underlying model. Its six classes, one per subversion mechanism, form the roots of a hierarchy of techniques and sub-techniques that researchers can use to describe attacks consistently and design more systematic evaluations. We derive the taxonomy from how LLMs are trained, prompted, and influenced by human-like framing, and compare it with historical attack data. To ground this comparison, we release PICorpus, to our knowledge the largest license-clean collection of public prompt-injection datasets, and map thousands of its records to PIAtlas under a human-verified protocol. The mapping shows that attacks usually combine several techniques drawn from only a few mechanisms. It also reveals gaps that existing labels obscure: widely used agent benchmarks test only part of the attack space, and adaptive attackers favor mechanisms that curated benchmarks underrepresent. PIAtlas thus provides a common foundation for understanding this attack space, improving benchmark coverage, and guiding red teaming and defenses.

</details>

### 23. Don't Judge an Agent by Its Messages: Private-State Leakage under Passive Monitoring of Agent Communication

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25032)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`private-state leakage`、`passive monitoring`、`message law`、`selection attack`

👤 **作者**：Daksh Pandey、Sayon Duttagupta、Xinhai Zou、Amit Singh Bhati

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 可在复现消息分布与接收者任务结果的同时披露任务从未要求的私有信息——审计者知道授权发送者与精确可观测律但无消息与背后私有状态的可信配对时被动审计能证明什么？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：单轮兼容互信息泄漏成锐区间；固定全部任务相关消息律仍可留一个完整私有比特不确定；对自选响应的发送者（LLM）证明其任务态内消息律改变=(1-2^-k)×诚实消息对属性的依赖；Qwen2.5-1.5B 合成路由任务 16 选 1
- 📌 **结论**：公开解码器 8,192 held-out 轮全数恢复私有比特（路由准确率 98.60% vs 诚实 98.90%）；知规则监视器单消息 AUC 0.645（诚实上限 0.671）、16 条上 0.972 而通用监视器近随机——保律发送者同样泄漏：被动监视的证明极限

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

An AI agent can reproduce the distribution of its messages and of the receiver's task outcomes while disclosing private information that the task never required. We ask what a passive audit can certify when the auditor knows the authorised sender and the exact observable law but has no trusted pairing of messages with the private states behind them. For a single round, compatible mutual-information leakage forms a sharp interval, and fixing the entire task-relevant message law, with every declared task utility, can still leave a full private bit undetermined; total leakage is determined if and only if, in every task state, the remaining private state or the authorised message is a deterministic function of that state. For a sender, such as an LLM, that selects among its own responses, we prove that in a balanced case its change to the message law within each task state equals $(1-2^{-k})$ times the honest messages' dependence on the attribute, which also sets, up to a constant, how many messages a monitor needs. With Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct on a synthetic routing task, selection among 16 responses lets a public decoder recover a private bit in all 8,192 held-out rounds at 98.60\% routing accuracy (honest sender 98.90\%). A monitor that knows the selection rule reaches AUC 0.645 on single messages, close to the ceiling of 0.671 that honest messages predict, and 0.972 over 16, while generic monitors stay near chance. A law-preserving sender built from the same samples recovers the bit in 97.7\% of rounds, and every monitor without the true bit stays near chance against it. A canonical task interface preserves every receiver decision and bounds the remaining information about a balanced attribute by twice the routing-error rate, even for a receiver that shares a key with the sender. These results distinguish what passive auditing can certify from what restricting communication can enforce.

</details>

### 24. Policy Is Not Capability: Some Safety Benchmarks Measure Which Definition of "Unsafe" You Trained On

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25060)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`safety benchmark validity`、`label definition`、`guardrail training`、`decontamination`

👤 **作者**：Vahid Jebraeeli、Jonathon Romero、Yijin Wang、Adrian Chan、Jianbang Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：guardrail 分类器被报告得好像 unsafe 是文本属性——它其实是策略属性且策略经标签进入模型：工业部署中同一 ~53k 提示被两种操作定义（『这是有害内容请求吗』vs『版主会封吗』）各标一次的事故
- 🔬 **研究方法**：十 guardrail 臂共享架构/代码库/优化器族×两规模；先发现训练池已含基准（JailbreakBench-benign 88%/OR-Bench-hard 82% 在池中）→全去污染后按『分数多少由标签定义决定』分层
- 📌 **结论**：跨臂范围 HarmBench-copyright 0.99 vs WildGuard-benign 0.043（23 倍差）；0.03% 参数即可把基准分移过全范围；最后监督信号带某策略的四个臂占据两个基准极端（exact rank p=0.005）——某些安全基准测的是你训练的定义而非能力（安全评测的构念效度）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A guardrail classifier is reported as if unsafe were a property of text. It is not: it is a property of a policy, and the policy enters the model through its labels. We exploit an accident of an industrial deployment in which the same ~53k prompts were labelled twice, under two operational definitions ("is this a request for harmful content?" and "would a moderator block this?"), and used to train ten guardrail arms sharing architecture, codebase and optimiser family, at two model scales. First, the guardrail's own training corpus already contained the benchmarks: JailbreakBench-benign is 88% and OR-Bench-hard 82% present in the training pools, two over-refusal benchmarks, the kind a guardrail reports to argue it does not over-block. Everything below is decontaminated. Second, safety benchmarks stratify sharply by how much of their score is decided by the label definition rather than by the model: with architecture and optimiser fixed, the range across the ten arms is 0.99 on HarmBench-copyright and 0.043 on WildGuard-benign, a 23x spread. Third, a benchmark score can be moved across its entire range by 0.03% of the parameters. Across these arms HarmBench-copyright spans 0-100 and OR-Bench-hard over-refusal 3.4-56.4%, and the four arms whose last supervision signal carries a given policy occupy one extreme of both (exact rank p=0.005, or 0.04-0.14 at worst), at unchanged detection capability. For a distilled student the teacher's soft labels do this even when every hard label carries the other policy, so teacher provenance is an unrecorded confound in any released checkpoint. Fourth, validation accuracy on the one split that grades all twenty arms tracks agreement with the annotator's policy (rho=+0.74), not detection capability (rho=+0.34). We argue that guardrail evaluations should report contamination, a seed floor and the definition they were graded against as first-class variables.

</details>

### 25. Memories That Outlive Their Reasons: Dependency-Tethered Memory for Tool-Using LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25123)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`non-local invalidation`、`dependency-tethered memory`、`supply chain`、`agent memory`

👤 **作者**：Yiqi Wang、…、Mingkai Zheng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 在网上发布包/模型/数据集并记忆过去验证省工——但判定立于前提：签名密钥被吊销时其上构建的一切停止通过而公告只点名密钥，旧判定的依赖者可能永不与它共享检索窗口（失效的非局域性使记忆成负担）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Tether：写时记录每个判定导出的精确版本、读时撤回支撑在库中任何位置已失败的判定（agent 读到的字段不变）；Fallout 版本化世界生成器（每记忆共享的假设盲模拟历史）跨三域对照 A-Mem/Graphiti/Mem0
- 📌 **结论**：原子事实记忆的 agent 发布 62% 上游失败对象（每条记录写入时为真）；Tether 发布 0.3% 且比全查少 37% 验证调用；A-Mem/Graphiti 复验多数候选仍发布 9%、Mem0 发布 42%——失效传播依赖感知的记忆设计（记忆的供应链完整性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agents now act on the Web, publishing packages, models and datasets through registry APIs, and remembering past verifications spares them the work. But a verdict rests on premises. When a signing key is revoked, everything built on it stops passing, yet the advisory names only the key, and the old verdicts of its dependents may never share a retrieval window with it. This non-locality of invalidation turns memory into a liability: in long simulated chats, an agent reading a memory of atomic facts publishes 62% of the objects whose failure lies upstream, each backed by a record that was true when written. Yet derivation is local even when invalidation is not. Tether records, at write time, the exact versions each verdict derives from, and at read time withdraws any verdict whose support has failed anywhere in the store; the agent reads the same fields as before. On Fallout, a generator of versioned worlds with hypothesis-blind simulated histories shared by every memory, Tether publishes 0.3% of upstream-failing objects across three domains when relations are stated with their verdicts, with 37% fewer verification calls than checking everything. A-Mem and Graphiti re-verify most candidates (0.83-0.86 checks per candidate), which limits their savings, yet publish 9% of upstream-failing objects; Mem0 relies on memory more and publishes 42%. Flushing every verdict on any failure is as safe as Tether but costs 32% more checks. The gain over fact memory holds with three open-weight backbones and against a whole-store window. These gains depend on capturing dependencies: when relations arrive in sessions apart from their verdicts, Tether's downstream unsafe publication rate rises to 9.4-22.1%, and a weak writer that misses edges loses safety in the same way.

</details>

### 26. Indistinguishable Agents: Training Agent Privacy as Twin-World Indistinguishability

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25161)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent privacy`、`twin-world indistinguishability`、`observer inference`、`GRPO`

👤 **作者**：Jiaming He、Guanyu Hou、Yi Yu、Xudong Jiang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 代用户搜索购物发消息而每个动作都被某方看见——既有隐私训练奖励不提保护项，但观察者可从 agent 所为推断私有事实（观察者只在视图随事实变化时学到）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：只在私有事实上不同的孪生世界训练：冻结判别器按真世界的对数似然比给每个观察者视图打分，配对 GRPO 在任务成功减该比值上训练；证明该目标控制每个观察者在每个信道上的 Bayes 成功
- 📌 **结论**：购物与消息任务上消除披露 judge 与查询级奖励漏掉的泄漏同时完成任务——动作侧推断的孪生世界不可区分性训练（隐私训练的信道完备化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM agents search, shop and send messages for their users, and every action is seen by some party. Existing privacy training rewards agents for not mentioning protected items, yet an observer can infer a private fact from what the agent does. Since an observer learns a fact only if its view changes with the fact, we propose \methodfull (\method), which trains on twin worlds that differ only in the private fact. A frozen distinguisher scores each observer's view by its log-likelihood ratio for the true world, and paired GRPO trains the agent on task success minus this ratio. We prove that this objective controls the Bayes success of every observer on every channel. On shopping and messaging tasks \method removes the leakage that disclosure judges and query-level rewards miss, while the agent still completes its tasks.

</details>

### 27. SEAL: Towards Persistent Tool Poisoning Attack against LLM Agents via Prefix-Eliciting Trigger

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25372)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`persistent tool poisoning`、`prefix-eliciting trigger`、`multi-round`、`LLM agents`

👤 **作者**：Nanjun Zhou、Yiming Li、Yukun Chen、Ting Qiao、Enhao Huang、Zhan Qin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：工具投毒把恶意指令嵌入工具描述或外部资源操纵 agent 工具选择——既有攻击依赖『单交互轮内生效』的理想假设而在现实多轮设置中急剧退化（agent 不随规划上下文演化一致地呈现攻击相关信号）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SEAL 两阶段：前缀引导触发优化 PETE（优化诱发攻击相关推理前缀的上下文特异触发）+局部到通用触发聚合 L2U（合并局部触发为单一通用触发以跨上下文与执行轮泛化）
- 📌 **结论**：七个代表多轮场景×多模型上有效且跨目标迁移——工具投毒的多轮持久化（skill 供应链的时序攻击面扩展）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Tool poisoning has emerged as a critical security threat to LLM agents, where adversaries embed malicious instructions in tool descriptions or external resources that enter the agent's context, allowing adversaries to manipulate agent's tool selection process. However, existing tool poisoning attacks primarily rely on an idealized assumption that the attacks task effect within a single interaction round. We show that their effectiveness degrades sharply in realistic multi-round agentic settings. We further reveal that this degradation stems from the fact that agents do not consistently surface attack-relevant signals in their reasoning as the planning context evolves, thereby weakening the attack's influence across rounds. To investigate whether tool poisoning remains a practical threat in such realistic settings, we propose SEAL, a two-stage method for persistent tool poisoning. Specifically, SEAL first performs Prefix-Eliciting Trigger Optimization (PETO) to optimize context-specific triggers that elicit attack-relevant reasoning prefixes, improving effectiveness within individual contexts. It then applies Local-to-Universal Trigger Aggregation (L2U) to combine these local triggers into a single universal trigger, improving generalization across different contexts and execution rounds. Extensive experiments across seven representative multi-round scenarios and different models demonstrate the attack effectiveness of SEAL, its transferability across target models, and its resistance to potential defenses.

</details>

### 28. MemDerive: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Agent Memory over Evolving Data

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25373)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`derived state`、`Datalog ground truth`、`memory diagnosis`、`evolving data`

👤 **作者**：Sen Gao、…、Shixuan Sun

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长运行 agent 依赖记忆保持任务状态（已完成什么/哪些条件仍成立）——该状态通常是导出状态：无记录陈述它而须由规则从演化历史的多记录计算；既有记忆基准只评终答而无中间状态参照（错答无法溯源到漏掉的状态、静默过时检不出）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MemDerive：符号化指定场景→Datalog 导出精确金答案+金推导 DAG 与每查询支撑事件→虚构实体与干扰项的自然语言历史；10 场景 4,692 事件 1,503 查询（难度沿访问/演化/组合变化）；按金 DAG 节点探查诊断失败
- 📌 **结论**：四记忆系统在导出状态上全部落后简单 BM25（同一答案模型给支撑事件时达 96.01%）；多数失败是未汇聚（系统持有缺失中间状态但检索不出）——终答基准隐藏的记忆诊断

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Long-running agents rely on memory of their past interactions for task state, such as what has been completed and which conditions still hold. Such state is usually a derived state. No record states it, and it must be computed by rules from multiple records of an evolving history. Existing memory benchmarks score only the final answer, with no reference for the intermediate states behind it. A wrong answer therefore cannot be traced to the state that was missed, and a state that silently became outdated goes undetected. We present MemDerive, a diagnostic benchmark for agent memory over evolving data. It specifies each scenario symbolically, derives exact gold answers with Datalog, records the gold derivation DAG and the supporting events of every query, and renders the history in natural language with fictional entities and distractors. Its 10 scenarios contain 4,692 events and 1,503 queries whose difficulty varies along access, evolution, and composition. On this ground truth, MemDerive evaluates answer reliability, diagnoses failures by probing the nodes of the gold derivation DAG, and tracks whether answers stay correct as events arrive. Our evaluation of four memory systems reveals failures that final-answer benchmarks hide. On derived state, every memory system falls behind simple BM25 retrieval, although the same answer model reaches 96.01% when given the supporting events. Most failures are ungathered, as a system holds the missing intermediate state but does not retrieve it for the question that needs it. Memory systems also rarely propagate implicit changes, which no new record states. These results suggest three principles for memory design. Gather inputs along the derivation, store rules with their versions, and maintain derived states through recorded dependencies.

</details>

### 29. Auditing Tool Traces in Agentic LLMs: Source Localization and Counterfactual Dependence Testing

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25540)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`tool-trace audit`、`label shortcuts`、`counterfactual redaction`、`source localization`

👤 **作者**：Junhao Liu、…、Martin Renqiang Min

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agentic LLM 经外部证据支撑科学预测——序列化工具结果可含隐藏标签捷径（数据集标识符/模板伪影）造成无生物推理的表观成功
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两阶段审计：PT2D（逐工具 TF-IDF 分解把标签预测模式定位到单个工具结果而避免拼接轨迹信号稀释）+Token 类删除（反事实测冻结 LLM 是否依赖定位到的捷径）；TCR-pMHC 结合任务
- 📌 **结论**：表面看工具增强大胜无证据基线（LLM 学会使用科学证据）——审计推翻：PT2D 把最强信号定位到一个工具结果的标识符、删除后性能降到随机——基准增益可信前须做来源级审计（科学 agent 的捷径伪装）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agentic LLMs increasingly support scientific prediction by grounding decisions in external evidence. In biology, they promise accurate, interpretable predictions for important problems by integrating complex experimental and computational evidence. However, serialized tool results can contain hidden label shortcuts, such as dataset identifiers or template artifacts, that create apparent success without biological reasoning. To address this risk, we introduce a two-stage audit. Stage 1, Per-Tool TF-IDF Decomposition (PT2D), localizes label-predictive patterns to individual tool results while avoiding signal dilution in the concatenated tool trace. Stage 2, Token-Class Redaction, counterfactually tests whether the frozen LLM depends on the localized shortcut. We apply this audit to TCR-pMHC binding, where tool augmentation substantially outperforms a no-evidence baseline. At face value, this suggests that the LLM has learned to use scientific evidence. The audit overturns this interpretation: PT2D localizes the strongest signal to identifiers in one tool result, and redacting them reduces performance to chance. These findings show how hidden label shortcuts can masquerade as scientific reasoning or discovery, motivating source-level audits before benchmark gains are trusted.

</details>

### 30. Symbolic Constraint Agents: Mining Formal Tool-Call Constraints to Prevent Agent Errors

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25552)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`symbolic constraints`、`constraint mining`、`SMT solver`、`tool-call verification`

👤 **作者**：Jinu Lee、Shraddha Barke

- 🎯 **研究动机**：符号约束（工具调用的程序化确定性检查）使 agent 工具调用可确定性验证——既有方法主要强制预定义安全策略而非复杂工具环境中的工具依赖
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SCA：从 API 文档挖掘符号约束+对照执行轨迹精炼+运行时 SMT 求解器检查工具调用（违规触发自然语言警告或唯一确定时自动修复）
- 📌 **结论**：AppWorld 上精炼把错误检测 F1 从 37.0 提到 58.3（GPT-5.5 轨迹）；运行时修复使 GPT-5.4-mini 任务成功 +5.4 点（40 调用预算）/+9.8 点（环境错误隐藏时）、跨模型移除 59-87% 失败调用并迁移 τ²-bench——从环境自动获取的符号约束提升工具调用可靠性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Symbolic constraints (programmatic, deterministic checks for tool calls) enable deterministic verification of LLM agents' tool calls, but existing approaches primarily enforce predefined safety policies rather than tool dependencies in complex tool environments. We explore whether symbolic constraints can be mined from environments and observations beyond predefined policies, and whether they can improve end-to-end task performance. We present Symbolic Constraint Agents (SCA), a framework that mines symbolic constraints from API documentation, refines them against execution traces, and checks the agent's tool calls with an SMT solver at runtime. A violation triggers a natural-language warning or, when the valid replacement is uniquely determined, an automatic repair. On AppWorld, refinement raises error-detection F1 from 37.0 to 58.3 on GPT-5.5 traces and from 39.3 to 61.9 on Qwen3.5-27B traces. At runtime, repair raises GPT-5.4-mini's task success by 5.4 points under a 40-call budget and 9.8 points when environment errors are hidden, while removing 59--87\% of failed calls across models, and transfers to $\tau^2$-bench with gains of up to 5.6 points. These findings show that automatically acquired symbolic constraints can improve tool-calling reliability and task completion, particularly when failures are costly or difficult to diagnose.

</details>

### 31. Safety Offset Learning: When Benign Fine-Tuning Misaligns LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25611)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`benign fine-tuning`、`refusal-to-compliance transition`、`SAFEOFF`、`misalignment mechanism`

👤 **作者**：Jesson Wang、Zhanhao Hu、Minghao Lin、Yuchen Wu、Aylin Caliskan、Mengyuan Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：微调安全风险常按训练数据有害性刻画（有害=危险、良性=假设保持对齐）——漏掉更根本的因素：监督诱导的行为转变。有害微调与降安全的良性微调可统一为同一『拒绝到顺从』转变在不同数据分布上的学习
- 🔬 **研究方法**：构造对齐模型初始倾向拒绝的良性示例并配其原始无害答案（初始拒绝与监督答案间的差异）；两条件共同才升有害顺从：差异足够大且不能经窄表面捷径消解（差异匹配下仅是否局域化改变有害顺从一个数量级）→SAFEOFF 数据集（重写良性指令抬高拒绝似然并配原答案）
- 📌 **结论**：跨三对齐 LLM×三安全基准——安全侵蚀的共同机制是拒绝到顺从的转变而非数据有害性本身（微调安全的行为转变理论）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Safety risks from fine-tuning are commonly characterized by the harmfulness of the training data:harmful fine-tuning is considered dangerous, while benign fine-tuning is often assumed to preservealignment. We argue that this view misses a more fundamental factor-the behavioral transition inducedby supervision. Harmful fine-tuning and safety-degrading benign fine-tuning can both be understoodas learning the same refusal-to-compliance transition, but on different data distributions. We studywhether such a transition learned on benign inputs can generalize to harmful requests. We constructbenign examples that an aligned model initially tends to refuse and pair them with their originalharmless responses, creating a discrepancy between the model's initial refusal and the supervisedanswer. Fine-tuning on these examples increases harmful compliance, but only when two conditionshold together: the discrepancy is large enough, and it cannot be resolved through a narrow surface-levelshortcut. With the induced discrepancy matched, varying only whether it is localized changes harmfulcompliance by an order of magnitude; raising the discrepancy does not help while a shortcut remainsavailable, and removing the shortcut does not help while the discrepancy is small. Building on thisobservation, we introduce SAFEOFF, a dataset of benign instructions rewritten to raise the alignedmodel's refusal likelihood and paired with their original answers. Across three aligned LLMs and threesafety benchmarks, SAFEOFF substantially weakens safety alignment despite containing no explicitharmful supervision, suggesting that fine-tuning safety depends not only on what data a model sees, butalso on which behavioral boundary the supervision moves.

</details>

### 32. Consistency Training Suppresses Cue Following Without Teaching Cue Selectivity

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25650)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`consistency training`、`cue following`、`cue selectivity`、`shared circuit`

👤 **作者**：Prakhar Gupta、Terry Jingchen Zhang、Zhijing Jin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LM 常因线索（『我认为答案是 B』这类无有效证据的提示）改答案——一致性训练（有无线索同答）四方法（输出 token/残差激活/注意力模式/采样答案率）；机制检验其学到什么
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四 CT 方法同数据训练 Gemma-2-2B/Llama-3.1-8B，用已知携带线索到答案的电路检查：全部四法使受训线索弱化且修复共享三性质——正确线索与错误线索同等失影响、修复对选项前线索大而选项后小、电路头仍携带多数残余线索致错
- 📌 **结论**：让 BCT 目标依赖线索正确性大多削弱修复——CT 削弱受训线索而不教模型何时线索是对的；只用选项前错误线索的评测漏掉前两性质（一致性训练的构念边界）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language models often change answers because of a cue, a hint such as "I believe the answer is B" that gives no valid evidence. Consistency training (CT) trains a model to answer the same with and without the cue. Four CT methods apply this idea to output tokens (BCT), residual activations (ACT), attention patterns (AttCT) and sampled-answer rates (RMCT). Prior work suggests some learn different solutions. We train all four on Gemma-2-2B-it and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct with the same data. We test predictions written in advance and inspect the models with a known circuit that carries cues to the answer. All four make the trained cue less effective, and their fixes share three properties. First, correct cues lose about as much influence as wrong ones. Second, the fix is usually large for cues before the answer options, as in training, and much smaller after them. Llama RMCT depends least on position. Third, the circuit heads still carry most remaining cue-induced errors. Making BCT targets depend on the cue's correctness mostly weakens the fix. CT thus weakens the trained cue without teaching models when a cue is right. Evaluations with only wrong cues at the trained position miss the first two properties.

</details>

### 33. When Safety Fails Collectively: Robust Alignment Against Harmful Fine-Tuning via Joint Weight Perturbations

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25683)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`harmful fine-tuning`、`joint weight perturbations`、`collective failure`、`alignment-stage defense`

👤 **作者**：Zhiyuan Wang、Yuezhi Zhou、Zhu Zhang、Zhixing Tan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：有害微调迅速侵蚀安全对齐——既有对齐期防御用有害示例与模拟有害扰动备战，但有害权重扰动的集体效应未被探索：不相交部分的单独效应有限而联合应用使有害性超个体之和
- 🔬 **研究方法**：JWP：掩蔽当前模型估计的有害扰动的不同部分并训练模型在所得状态安全响应——暴露于有害扰动的不同组合、降低后续有害微调对其累积的敏感性
- 📌 **结论**：跨下游任务与模型族对有害微调的抵抗力强于既有防御且基本保持任务性能——联合扰动的集体失效与防御（对齐侵蚀的组合学）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Harmful fine-tuning can rapidly erode the safety alignment of large language models, creating substantial risks when aligned models are adapted to downstream tasks. Existing alignment-stage defenses use harmful examples and simulated harmful perturbations during safety alignment to prepare models for later harmful fine-tuning, yet the collective effects of harmful weight perturbations remain underexplored. We identify a collective failure pattern: disjoint parts of a harmful perturbation have limited effects in isolation, but their joint application increases the model's harmfulness beyond the sum of their individual effects. Guided by this observation, we propose Joint Weight Perturbations (JWP), an alignment-stage defense that masks different parts of a harmful perturbation estimated from the current model and trains the model to respond safely in the resulting states. This exposes the model to different combinations of harmful perturbations and reduces its sensitivity to their accumulation during subsequent harmful fine-tuning. Experiments across downstream tasks and model families show that JWP offers stronger resistance to harmful fine-tuning than prior defenses while largely preserving task performance.

</details>

### 34. Whose Credential Is It? How Owner Attribution in Agent MemoryShapes Cross-User Account Misuse

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25707)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`cross-user credentials`、`owner attribution`、`agent memory`、`account misuse`

👤 **作者**：Hau-Sing So、Daixian Liu、Carl Hou、Yuhaohang He、Mengzhu Wang、Hai-Tao Zheng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：共享长期记忆让服务一个用户的 LLM agent 意外检索另一用户的凭据——但何时暴露导致实际伤害不明：须分离三事件（外来凭据到达提示=暴露/agent 用它行动=行为/对方账户改变=状态）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AppWorld 混合 763 条过往登录的存储检索他人凭据（84.7% 必需应用）+压力测试（记忆缺用户自己凭据而把单一外来凭据呈现为用户记录）+配对干预（在凭据行前或内命名所有者）
- 📌 **结论**：压力下七 agent 80-94% 运行使用外来凭据、至多 43% 改变受害者账户（有时过官方检查）；命名所有者把误用从 92/305 降到 1/305（无用户名也有效，owner 绑定注记在干净记忆上每模型损失 3-5 个成功任务）；写错所有者大量恢复误用——记忆须一致记录凭据属于谁且评测须测行为而非仅暴露

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Shared long-term memory lets an LLM agent serving one user inadvertently retrieve another user's credentials, but it is unclear when such exposure leads to actual harm. We introduce a three-stage audit that separates three events: a foreign credential reaches the prompt (exposure), the agent acts with it (behavior), and the other user's account changes (state). In AppWorld, a store that mixes 763 past logins from all users retrieves another user's credential for 84.7 percent of required apps, yet agents rarely act on it. Misuse appears in a stress test where memory lacks the user's own credential and presents a single foreign one as the user's record: seven agents then use it in 80 to 94 percent of runs and change the victim's account in up to 43 percent, sometimes passing the official check. Naming the owner before or inside each credential line prevents most of this: with all else fixed, misuse falls from 92 to 1 of 305 paired runs, also with name-free usernames, though owner-bound notes cost 3 to 5 successful tasks per model on clean memory. A wrongly written owner largely restores misuse; trailing owner tags and added safety instructions help only some models. Agent memory should consistently record whose credential it stores, and evaluations should measure behavior, not only exposure.

</details>

### 35. Does Unlearning Survive Collaboration? Robust Unlearning for Shared-Model LLM Teams

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25731)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`unlearning robustness`、`LLM teams`、`joint recovery`、`aggregation-conditioned`

👤 **作者**：Hengsong Liu、…、Liang Song

- 🎯 **研究动机**：遗忘对移除敏感知识保存能力关键——LLM 团队日益用于解复杂问题而遗忘须在协作中保持有效：协作削弱遗忘（终答评测漏掉团队消息中可恢复证据包括支持联合恢复的互补线索）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：理论与实证确认控制终答或个体消息不保证组合低恢复→ACU：用个体与池化消息的重建反馈训练共享模型、优先高恢复风险视图、替换式信用分配并保护直接与协作效用；TOFU 轨迹级恢复+WMDP-bio 原生遗忘
- 📌 **结论**：TOFU 上完整轨迹恢复从 41.8% 降到 19.3%（ROUGE-L）、事实恢复 34.2%→18.7% 而保留任务性能相当——跨团队规模与读取者泛化（遗忘的组合泄漏与防御）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM unlearning is essential for removing sensitive or unwanted knowledge while preserving useful capabilities. As LLM teams are increasingly used to solve complex problems, forgetting must remain effective during collaboration. Despite advances in forgetting effectiveness, efficiency, and robustness to relearning, team deployment remains underexplored. We find that collaboration weakens forgetting: final-answer evaluations miss recoverable evidence in team messages, including complementary clues supporting joint recovery. We establish theoretically and confirm empirically that controlling final answers or individual messages does not ensure low recovery from their combination. To address this challenge, we propose Aggregation-Conditioned Unlearning (ACU). ACU trains a shared model using reconstruction feedback from individual and pooled messages, prioritizes views with higher recovery risk, and uses replacement-based credit assignment while protecting direct and collaborative utility. TOFU evaluates trajectory-level recovery, while WMDP-bio tests native forgetting and retained utility. On TOFU, ACU lowers full-trajectory recovery from 41.8% to 19.3% in ROUGE-L recall and factual recovery from 34.2% to 18.7% versus output-supervised training, with comparable retained-task performance. These results demonstrate that ACU curbs information leakage in LLM teams and generalizes across team sizes and readers.

</details>

### 36. Towards Architectural-Level Safety Control: Relocating Hazardous Knowledge into Removable Experts in Mixture-of-Experts Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25791)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`hazardous knowledge`、`removable experts`、`MoE`、`architectural safety`

👤 **作者**：Cheng Jin、…、Qiaosheng Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：从模型本身移除危险知识而不降一般能力是理想的但困难（与一般能力深度纠缠于相同参数）——既有对齐与遗忘方法调共享参数、倾向抑制表达而非移除（知识仍在且易被后续微调恢复）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：REX：利用 MoE 模块结构扩展一组新的物理可移除专家——专家扩展+选择性梯度掩蔽+路由差异把危险知识迁出原专家并集中到新专家；切除新专家即得可公开发布的模型
- 📌 **结论**：跨 Intern-S2/Qwen3.5-35B/Gemma-4-26B 一致：切除使 WMDP-Bio 降 48.4 点而 MMLU 变化 <3 点；切除模型匹配九个遗忘方法中最强者——架构级危险知识重定位（可物理移除的安全控制）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models acquire broad knowledge from web-scale corpora during pretraining, including knowledge that could be hazardous if misused. Removing such knowledge from the model itself without degrading general capability is desirable but difficult, as it is deeply entangled with general capability within the same parameters. Existing safety alignment and unlearning methods typically adjust these shared parameters, tending to suppress the expression of hazardous knowledge rather than remove it. As a result, the knowledge remains in the model and is readily recoverable by subsequent fine-tuning. To address this limitation, we propose REX (Relocating into Removable EXperts), a knowledge relocation method that separates hazardous knowledge from general capability at the architectural level. Exploiting the modular structure of Mixture-of-Experts models, REX expands the model with a set of new, physically removable experts designated to hold hazardous knowledge. Through expert expansion, selective gradient masking and routing differentiation, it relocates hazardous knowledge out of the original experts and concentrates it in the new ones. Excising these new experts yields a model suitable for public release. This removal holds consistently across Intern-S2, Qwen3.5-35B-A3B and Gemma-4-26B-A4B; on Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, for instance, excision lowers WMDP-Bio accuracy by 48.4 points while MMLU changes by less than 3 points. The excised model matches the strongest of nine unlearning baselines in removing hazardous knowledge, and it is the only one that preserves this removal under subsequent fine-tuning. After 250M tokens of standard fine-tuning on Qwen3.5, the best baseline rebounds on WMDP-Bio from 44.1% to 78.3%, whereas REX rises only from 32.4% to 42.8%.

</details>

### 37. When Retrieval Partially Conflicts with Skills: Understanding Procedural Hallucination in Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25827)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`procedural hallucination`、`skill-retrieval conflict`、`value vectors`、`mechanistic analysis`

👤 **作者**：Guanqun Jiang、Pengfei Cao、Kang Liu、Jun Zhao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LM 日益使用提供可复用指令与程序 skill——选定 skill 后模型须把其程序与检索任务上下文结合：检索有用但含违反 skill 的线索时须保留兼容信息拒绝冲突线索，失败时模型采纳线索产出检索支持但 skill 无效的答案（程序性幻觉）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SourceClash：1,271 捆绑×6,355 提示×十程序域（固定任务/skill 规范/答案空间只变 skill 与检索关系）；九模型+开放权重机制分析（答案偏好差异在生成开始后不久达峰；残差替换移向有效答案、注意力分解把多数修复归于 value 向量）
- 📌 **结论**：冲突检索下有效答案率均值 25.7%（两支持性设置 78.2%/72.8%）；对识别的 value 通路干预改善最终有效性——部分冲突检索如何改变 skill 引导生成（skill 完整性的检索冲突面）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language models increasingly use skills that provide reusable instructions and procedures for task execution. After a relevant skill has been selected, the model may need to apply its governing procedure together with retrieved task context. When the retrieved context is useful but contains cues that violate the skill, the model must retain compatible information while rejecting the conflicting cues. We study failures in which the model adopts such cues and produces a retrieval-supported but skill-invalid answer, which we call procedural hallucination. We introduce SourceClash, a matched benchmark with 1,271 bundles and 6,355 prompts across ten procedural domains. Each bundle fixes the task, skill specification, and answer space while varying the relation between the skill and retrieval. Across nine models, valid-answer rates average 25.7% under conflicting retrieval, compared with 78.2% and 72.8% under two supportive retrieval settings. Mechanistic analysis of open-weight models shows that the answer-preference difference typically reaches its maximum shortly after generation begins. Residual replacement moves the model toward valid answers, while attention decomposition attributes most of the repair to value vectors. Intervention at the identified value pathway also improves final answer validity. These results characterize how partly conflicting retrieval changes skill-guided generation.

</details>

### 38. A Cue's Hidden Price: Auditing Seller Influence on AI Shoppers

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25843)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`seller influence`、`price-equivalent audit`、`AI shoppers`、`GEO`

👤 **作者**：Felix Buecheler、Gerardo Berbeglia、Mingyu Guo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 代用户购物——卖家控制的徽章/背书/框架可操纵推荐：核心问题不是卖家内容是否改变 agent 选择而是改变多少；选择率作影响量度有盲点（线索强到几乎每次被选后选择率无法显示一个线索比另一个强多少）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：价格等价（PE）审计：反平衡位置控制强制选择中提高被线索产品的价格直到其只被选一半，该溢价即线索的价格等价（形式定义的策略条件平价阈使悬殊线索可比）；113,472 试验×七模型×两类产品+首个决策原生模型 Jev 的 PE 审计
- 📌 **结论**：等价价格下看似同样强的线索实际差距悬殊（选择率审计严重欠分辨）；Jev 的画像与全部文本模型不同——抵抗多数线索却对最强社会证明声称仍易感——卖家影响的货币化审计（GEO 影响力的定量测度）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models are increasingly asked to shop on a user's behalf, which lets seller-controlled content such as badges, endorsements, and framing steer what they recommend. That poses the question at the core of generative engine optimization (GEO): not whether seller content shifts an agent's choice, but how much. The usual gauge of that influence, how often a cue wins the choice, has a blind spot: once a cue is strong enough to be picked almost every time, the choice rate can no longer show how much stronger one cue is than another. We introduce price-equivalent (PE) auditing, which removes this ceiling by pricing the cue in money. In a counterbalanced, position-controlled forced choice, we raise the cued product's price until it is chosen only half the time, and report that premium as the cue's price-equivalent: a formally defined, policy-conditioned parity threshold that makes very different cues comparable. Priced this way, cues that look equally strong at equal price differ by a wide margin, so choice-rate audits substantially under-resolve seller influence. We run the audit over 113,472 trials, seven models across families, and two product categories.
