To our knowledge, we also run the first price-equivalent audit of a decision-native model, Jev, which outputs a choice probability directly instead of generating text; strikingly, its profile is unlike any text model we tested, resisting most cues yet remaining susceptible to the strongest social-proof claims. We release the stimuli, logs, and code as a benchmark that reports each model's cue-price profile rather than a single score, giving developers and auditors a common, reproducible way to measure how content moves AI shopping agents.

</details>

### 39. SWE-Residual: Evaluating Starting-State Robustness of Coding Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25899)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`starting-state robustness`、`residual states`、`coding agents`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Jinze Li、…、Edith C.H. Ngai

- 🎯 **研究动机**：SWE 基准几乎总把 agent 放在无先前工作的干净状态——实践中 agent 常继续开发者或另一 agent 已完成并报告完成的工作而无任何余留错误的指示
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SWE-Residual：198 个残余状态（111 个 DeepSWE/Terminal-Bench 任务中前驱 agent 提交为完成但隐藏测试仍失败时留下的仓库/整机状态）；被评 agent 只收原始请求与该状态（无前驱轨迹或失败迹象；请求与测试不变使起始状态为唯一差异）
- 📌 **结论**：五前沿 agent 相对干净起点成功率掉 20.6-53.5 点（DeepSWE）与 20.6-34.9 点（Terminal-Bench）且 DeepSWE 上排名反转；失败延续呈模式——起始状态鲁棒性成为编码 agent 的独立维度（脏状态下的续作能力）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Software engineering (SWE) benchmarks are the primary way to measure the capabilities of coding agents: an agent is placed in a code repository or machine environment, given a request, and judged by hidden tests. These benchmarks almost always start the agent from a clean state that contains no earlier work on the task. In practice, however, agents often continue work that a developer or another agent has already done and reported as complete, with no indication of what remains incorrect or missing. We introduce SWE-Residual, a benchmark for Starting-State Robustness: the ability to maintain performance on a task when the starting state already contains earlier work on that task. SWE-Residual contains 198 Residual States drawn from 111 tasks in DeepSWE and Terminal-Bench, where the earlier work modifies a code repository or a whole machine environment. Each Residual State is the state a predecessor agent left behind after submitting its work as complete, although the hidden tests still fail. The evaluated agent receives only the original request and this state, without the predecessor's trajectory or any sign that the attempt failed; because the request and hidden tests are unchanged, the starting state is the only difference from the source benchmark. Across five frontier agents, success drops by 20.6--53.5 percentage points on DeepSWE and 20.6--34.9 points on Terminal-Bench relative to a Clean Start, and agent rankings on DeepSWE reverse. Failed continuations show a pattern we call acceptance bias: agents treat the inherited work as the definition of what remains to be done instead of checking it against the request. On DeepSWE, in 58.9--88.2% of failed runs, they submit with exactly the same tests failing, although they pass most of these tests from a Clean Start. SWE-Residual thus exposes a capability gap that clean-start evaluation does not capture.

</details>

### 40. Secure Agentic Coding through Counterexample-Grounded Feedback

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25916)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`secure code`、`counterexample-grounded`、`reward overoptimization`、`execution traces`

👤 **作者**：Tue Le、Anh Nguyen Hoang、Gabriel Sasseville、Doseok Jang、Pierre-Luc Bacon、Nghi D. Q. Bui

- 🎯 **研究动机**：编码模型与 agent harness 更强而可靠安全验证成中心瓶颈——安全信号报告的进展须与优化下实际程序行为保持对齐否则优化器可改进信号判决而不移除底层漏洞
- 🔬 **研究方法**：FALCON 反例接地验证框架：把攻击探索与行为验证分离并经被测程序执行轨迹连接（模型只须执行反例不必从源码推断行为或指定对抗测试的正确结局）
- 📌 **结论**：测试时修复的 held-out 功能安全增益显著超替代信号；作为 RL 奖励超静态分析与学习奖励（即便所有策略都成功优化各自训练奖励）；与代理奖励组合进一步提升——执行接地反馈缓解奖励过度优化（安全代码训练的信号设计）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As coding models and agent harnesses become more capable, reliable security verification is becoming a central bottleneck. Improving secure code generation requires a signal whose reported progress remains aligned with actual program behavior under optimization; otherwise, an optimizer can improve the signal's verdict without removing the underlying vulnerability. Existing model-based approaches require models to either infer program behavior from source code or specify correct outcomes for adversarial tests, placing substantial reasoning demands on the security signal. We introduce \falcon{}, a counterexample-grounded verification framework that separates attack exploration from behavioral verification and connects them through execution traces of the program under test. In test-time repair, \falcon{} yields substantially stronger held-out functional-security gains than alternative security signals across secure code generation benchmarks, while preserving or improving functionality. As an RL reward, \falcon{} also outperforms static-analysis and learned rewards on held-out functional-security, even when all policies successfully optimize their respective training rewards. Combining \falcon{} with either proxy reward further improves held-out performance, suggesting that execution-grounded feedback can mitigate reward overoptimization while preserving useful guidance from existing security signals.

</details>

### 41. RAGLeak: Supply-Chain Privacy Backdoors in Retrieval-Augmented Generation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25937)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`RAG privacy backdoor`、`reversible obfuscation`、`supply chain`、`data extraction`

👤 **作者**：Yuchen Chen、…、Zhenyu Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：RAG 依赖外部知识库引入新攻击面——既有数据抽取攻击靠提示注入（依赖生成器指令遵循）或复述检索文档的后门（难平衡抽取保真与隐蔽）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RAGLeak 供应链隐私后门：把信息泄漏与直接文本暴露解耦——触发时生成器对检索到的私有文档做可逆混淆供辅助 LLM 高保真恢复；三模型族×三应用域
- 📌 **结论**：ASR 至 100% 且良性效用影响有限；代表性检测防御下至多降 10 点；干净微调后仍有效并跨模型规模与版本——RAG 的供应链隐私风险（后门×隐私抽取的新组合）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables large language models to access external knowledge during inference, but its reliance on external knowledge bases also introduces new attack surfaces. Existing data extraction attacks against RAG systems rely on either prompt injection, which heavily depends on the generator's instruction-following capability, or backdoors that reproduce or paraphrase retrieved documents but struggle to balance extraction fidelity and stealthiness. In this paper, we propose RAGLeak, a supply-chain privacy backdoor attack that decouples information leakage from direct textual exposure. When triggered, the generator reversibly obfuscates retrieved private documents for high-fidelity recovery by an auxiliary LLM. Across three model families and three application domains, RAGLeak achieves up to $100\%$ ASR with limited impact on benign utility and at most a $10$-percentage-point ASR drop under representative detection-based defenses. The backdoor remains effective after clean fine-tuning and across model scales and versions, revealing an underexplored supply-chain privacy risk in RAG systems. The source code is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 42. Claim Laundering: When Agent Decision Models Trust Records Over Reality

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25947)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`record-reality conflict`、`silent failure`、`intermediate-layer veto`、`claim laundering`

👤 **作者**：Tina Khezresmaeilzadeh、Seyedarmin Azizi、Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo、Mehdi Kamal、Massoud Pedram

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 决策模型单次前向从环境观察+自身过往行动记录的组合选动作——动作静默失败时两源冲突（记录报成功而环境显示未生效）：哪个源决定下一决策？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Android/ALFWorld/WebShop 配对状态独立变观察/记录动作/显式成功声明：12 配置（四决策模型×三域）中记录对承诺决策的平均效应均大于观察；声称成功但失败后模型仍 53-100% 停止/推进/购买；线性探针区分成功与失败步（AUROC 0.77-1.00）+激活补丁显示后期层记录影响增加→EVIGATE 中间层否决
- 📌 **结论**：免额外前向（+0.04-2.0% 服务延迟）的中间层否决在注入静默失败下提升任务成功（活体 ALFWorld +18.7 点、WebShop +5.0、手机模拟器 +5.1）——记录清洗：决策模型信记录不信现实及其中间层矫正（claim laundering 与内部 veto）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent decision models select actions in a single forward pass from inputs that combine environmental observations with the agent's own record of previous actions. When an action fails silently, these sources can conflict: the record reports success, but the environment shows that the step did not take effect. We ask which source determines the next decision. Across Android screen traces, ALFWorld, and WebShop, we construct paired states that independently vary the observation, the logged action, and an explicit success claim. In all 12 configurations of four decision models and three domains, records have a larger average effect on commitment decisions than observations; after claimed-but-failed steps, the models still stop, advance, or purchase in 53-100% of cases. Yet the relevant evidence is often represented internally: linear probes on intermediate hidden states distinguish successful from failed steps on held-out apps, tasks, and goals (AUROC 0.77-1.00), and activation patching reveals increasing influence from records in later layers. We use this signal in EVIGATE, an intermediate-layer veto that requires no additional forward pass and adds 0.04-2.0% serving latency. Under injected silent failures, EVIGATE improves task success by 18.7 points in live ALFWorld agents, 5.0 in WebShop, and 5.1 in a phone simulator, and in WebShop and the simulator it also outperforms random vetoes at the same rate. Its effectiveness depends on representative calibration states: gates calibrated on offline counterfactual states do not transfer reliably to live agent trajectories.

</details>

### 43. CXBench: A Benchmark for Process-Compliant LLM Agents in Regulated Customer-Experience Workflows

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25954)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`process compliance`、`regulated workflows`、`false-pass`、`hybrid grader`

👤 **作者**：Boxi Yu、…、Emir Muñoz

- 🎯 **研究动机**：工具 agent 日益执行受监管客服工作流——正确终态不充分（还须披露/取得同意/遵循必需程序）；既有工具 agent 基准大抵只评终态业务状态而接受经不合规过程达到正确结果的轨迹
- 🔬 **研究方法**：cxbench：六受监管域的 outcome 与 process 合规分开评（每任务=客户请求+工具环境+显式过程要求；规则卡把被引监管义务链接到可观测轨迹证据；确定性顺序与权威检查+LLM judge 语义要求的混合评分器）；定义 false-pass=过终态但败过程
- 📌 **结论**：七模型×120 任务：840 保留执行中 55.5% 是 false-pass（占终态通过的 80.1%）、仅 13.8% 双过；过程包容评测改变模型排序并暴露仅看结果隐藏的差异——受监管工作流的过程级评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Tool-using language agents increasingly perform regulated customer-service workflows, where a correct final state is insufficient: agents must also provide disclosures, obtain consent, and follow required procedures. Existing tool-agent benchmarks largely score final business states, accepting trajectories that reach correct outcomes through non-compliant processes. We introduce cxbench, a benchmark that evaluates outcome and process compliance separately across six regulated domains. Each task combines a customer request, a tool-based environment, and explicit process requirements. Rule cards link cited regulatory obligations to observable trace evidence. A hybrid grader combines deterministic checks for ordering and authority with an LLM judge for semantic requirements. We define a false-pass as an outcome-pass run that fails process checks. Across seven models and 120 tasks, 55.5% of the 840 retained executions are false-passes, representing 80.1% of outcome-passing runs; only 13.8% pass both checks. Process-inclusive evaluation changes the observed model ordering and exposes differences hidden by outcome-only scoring. cxbench makes these failures inspectable through trace-linked criteria.

</details>

### 44. Token-Set Choice Confounds POPE: A Systematic Audit of Yes/No Extraction in VLM Hallucination Evaluation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25964)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`POPE readout`、`token-set confound`、`hallucination evaluation`、`reproduction audit`

👤 **作者**：Kesav Kumar Jayakumar、Karthigeyan Thilak

- 🎯 **研究动机**：复现 POPE 上的幻觉缓解结果时发现基线高出计划对照 1.21-8.67 点——检查评测代码发现读出选择本身移动 F1
- 🔬 **研究方法**：双 token logit 读出比较 ID 3582/1217，但 9,000 贪婪解码问题中两 ID 一次也未生成（模型发 ' Yes'/' No' 带空格前缀形式）；用覆盖空格前缀表面形式的八 token 查找读出+量化精度对照
- 📌 **结论**：读出修正本身移动对抗分裂 F1 6.13 点——超过调查的六篇论文中五篇报告的对抗增益而它们无一声明读出；固定基线上九个推理时修正无一胜出——VLM 幻觉评测的 token 集选择混淆（评测有效性的复现审计）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We set out to reproduce a recent hallucination-mitigation result on the POPE benchmark. The LLaVA-1.5-7B baseline we measured, 82.21 F1 on the adversarial split, sat 1.21 to 8.67 points above the adversarial baselines we planned to compare against (81.00, 79.30, and 73.54). We looked at the evaluation code and found a readout choice that moves F1 on its own. A two-token logit readout decides yes versus no by comparing two vocabulary IDs, 3582 ("yes") and 1217 ("no"). Across 9,000 greedy-decode questions spanning all three splits, neither ID is generated even once. The model emits " Yes" (token 3869) and " No" (token 1939), because SentencePiece prepends a space byte after the prompt suffix "ASSISTANT:". Read with an eight-token lookup that covers the space-prefixed surface forms, LLaVA-1.5-7B scores F1 0.8221, 0.8498, and 0.8713 on the adversarial, popular, and random splits. That 6.13-point adversarial shift exceeds the adversarial gain reported by five of the six papers we surveyed. None of them states its readout, and our reference ran under 4-bit NF4 quantization, so we cannot say how much of any reported gain a matched evaluation would keep. That comparison stays unresolved until readout and precision are matched. On the fixed baseline we run nine inference-time corrections of our own (none beat it) and dissect why the adversarial split resists logit-level intervention. In a 3,000-question probe, wrong predictions draw more image attention than correct ones (Cohen's $d=-0.46$, $p=1.4\times10^{-24}$), the opposite of what grounding-based corrections assume. A four-readout audit across four further VLMs from three tokenizer families confirms the confound is protocol-level, not model-specific: porting the LLaVA-1.5 IDs verbatim drops F1 to 0.00 on the two disjoint-vocabulary models, whereas a tokenizer-derived readout holds between 0.80 and 0.92.

</details>

### 45. SPLASH: Contrastive Audit Pressure for Detecting and Localizing Security Hallucinations in Generated Code

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26001)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`security hallucinations`、`audit pressure`、`contrastive log-prob`、`code security`

👤 **作者**：Mirza Masfiqur Rahman、MD Ajwad Akil、Imtiaz Karim、Ashish Kundu、Ramana Kompella、Elisa Bertino

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 生成代码常通过全部功能测试而不安全——这些安全幻觉常见、高置信写就且跨样本重复（生成器自身不确定性与静态分析器都不可靠捕获）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SPLASH（审计压力）：对比生成程序在安全审查与风格审查下的教师强制对数概率——聚合到语句与程序级并接地在竞争编辑的概率模型上选择安全候选并定位漏洞语句（免训练/免 CWE 规则/免生成模型内部访问）
- 📌 **结论**：12 模型 899 任务 63,684 程序：74% 情况选出安全代码、58% 不安全程序中首个定位漏洞语句、优于前沿 LLM judge——安全幻觉的对比审计压力检测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Code generated by large language models often passes every functional test while remaining insecure. These security hallucinations are common, written with high confidence, and repeated across samples, so neither the generator's own uncertainty nor static analyzers reliably catch them. We introduce SPLASH, which is guided by a new measure called audit pressure. It contrasts teacher-forced log-probabilities for the generated program under a security review and a style review. Aggregated to statements and programs, and grounded in a probabilistic model of competing edits, audit pressure allows SPLASH to choose the secure program among a model's candidate programs and locate the vulnerable statement, with no model training, CWE-specific rules, or internal access to the generating model. On 63,684 programs generated by 12 models for 899 tasks, SPLASH picks secure code in 74% of cases, places the vulnerable statement first in 58% of insecure programs, and chooses better than a frontier LLM judge.

</details>

### 46. SpanGuard: Decoupling Instruction Localization from Authorization for Lightweight IPI Defense

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26013)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`IPI defense`、`localization-authorization decoupling`、`lightweight guardrail`、`segmentation`

👤 **作者**：Yinxiang Wang、Zehua Ma、Kejiang Chen、Weiming Zhang、Han Fang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 护栏对 IPI 有效但性能重度依赖模型能力（变小变弱时检测实质退化）——检测 IPI 须同时定位异质工具输出中的潜在注入指令并判断是否被用户请求授权；小模型的困难主要在联合满足两要求而非任一子问题
- 🔬 **研究方法**：显式解耦定位与决策：定位无需强语言推理（注入指令常与周围内容语义不连续）→轻量无监督分割划局部区域+块级授权判断交给小 LLM
- 📌 **结论**：『看哪里』与『决定什么』分离大幅降低所需能力——轻量 IPI 护栏（小型化注入防御的结构分解）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM-based guardrails are effective against indirect prompt injection (IPI), but their performance depends heavily on model capability: as the guardrail model becomes smaller or weaker, detection degrades substantially. This raises a natural question: can we retain strong IPI defense while relying only on a small LLM? To understand why, we revisit what an LLM guardrail actually does. Detecting IPI requires simultaneously locating potentially injected instructions within heterogeneous tool outputs and judging whether they are authorized by the user's request. We find that the difficulty for small models arises largely from satisfying both requirements jointly, rather than from either subproblem alone. This suggests a different path to lightweight defense: explicitly decoupling localization from decision-making. In particular, localization needs no strong language-model reasoning, since injected instructions are often semantically discontinuous with their surrounding content and can be partitioned into local regions via lightweight unsupervised segmentation. Once the observation is partitioned this way, the remaining authorization check becomes a localized judgment that a small LLM can handle. Building on this insight, we design a lightweight IPI guardrail that performs attack-agnostic localization followed by block-level authorization judgment with a small language model. By separating where to look from what to decide, our approach substantially reduces the capability required of the guardrail model while preserving strong defense performance. Empirically, our guardrail achieves a 0.32% attack success rate with negligible loss in benign utility while remaining effective with substantially smaller models. These results show that strong IPI defense need not rely on strong LLMs when localization and decision-making are explicitly decoupled.

</details>

### 47. LeakBench: Auditing Reward Hacking through Prediction-Time Leakage in Clinical Modeling Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26088)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`reward hacking`、`prediction-time leakage`、`clinical agents`、`audit suite`

👤 **作者**：Xiaoyuan Bao、…、Jun Gao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自主临床建模 agent 可优化预测性能而违反使模型可用的条件（预测时泄漏/校准/站点级评估/支持意识/决策曲线净收益）——reward hacking 描述性使用：agent 因提升验证奖励而选择预测时无效输入
- 🔬 **研究方法**：LeakBench：指南导出的可执行审计套件（合成已知失败结构任务+MIMIC-IV 43 万入院+多医院首页数据）；确定性优化器与每个无约束本地模型配置都选择致命泄漏特征
- 📌 **结论**：2026-09 全记录 A/B：DeepSeek Flash 与 GPT-6-astra 各 10/10 无约束运行泄漏、显式入院时间约束下 0/10；重命名或伪装泄漏输入不改变选择且基于名称的过滤失败——确定性来源验证器在环移除泄漏使 LLM 修复环成纵深防御（临床 agent 的奖励作弊审计）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Autonomous clinical-modeling agents can optimize predictive performance while violating conditions that make a model usable. We introduce LeakBench, an executable, guideline-derived audit suite that tests agent decisions for prediction-time leakage, calibration, site-level evaluation, support awareness, and decision-curve net benefit. LeakBench combines a synthetic task with known failure structure and two real-data settings: MIMIC-IV (430k admissions) and governed multi-hospital inpatient record front-page (MHFP) data. Our primary analysis uses reward hacking descriptively, without attributing intent: an agent selects prediction-time-invalid inputs because they improve its validation reward. A deterministic optimizer and every evaluated unconstrained local-model configuration select a fatal leaking feature. In fully logged September 2026 A/B runs, DeepSeek Flash and GPT-6-astra each leak in 10/10 unconstrained runs and 0/10 runs with an explicit admission-time constraint. This contrast is version-sensitive: a blinded Claude/Fable-5.1 chat run does not leak in either condition, while explicit-constraint violations appear only in a historical August 2026 slice lacking raw trajectories. Replacing AUROC with AUPRC does not remove leakage selection. Renaming or disguising the leaking inputs changes neither selection nor provenance-based detection; a name-based filter fails. A deterministic provenance verifier-in-loop removes the leak, making the LLM repair loop defense-in-depth. Specific verifier feedback clears fatal leakage in a hosted agent, and a three-seed ablation finds principle-only feedback clears decision-level defects, though these mitigation results are limited. LeakBench treats trustworthy clinical prediction as a property of an agent's modeling process, not merely its score; it is an audit testbed with known-failure controls, not yet an expert-validated benchmark.

</details>

### 48. PhantomTac: Hijacking Robotic Tactile Systems with Deploy-and-forget Tags

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26174)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`tactile hijacking`、`magnetic sensors`、`physical attack`、`robotics`

👤 **作者**：Binbin Zhang、Yifeng Zhang、Xiaomeng Chen、Dongyao Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：触觉感知对精确机器人操纵关键（灵巧闭环力控制）——磁性触觉传感器以细粒度三维力感知与强抗遮挡突出：发现其物理漏洞 PhantomTac（攻击者可改变磁场欺骗力解读）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：0.5mm 无电池磁标签贴物体表面即可隐蔽部署（条码贴纸即可掩盖）『部署即忘』式攻击；逆向工程商业现成触觉传感器的感测机制+实用标签设计+真机端到端评估
- 📌 **结论**：欺骗法向力压碎物体、欺骗剪切力松握掉落；相比直接破坏机械手其更隐蔽（无需接触设备）——机器人触觉系统的物理劫持（具身攻击面扩展到触觉）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Tactile perception is crucial for precise robotic manipulation, providing an important foundation for dexterous, closed-loop force control. Among various tactile sensing techniques, magnetic tactile sensors stand out thanks to their fine-grained, three-dimensional force perception capability and strong robustness against visual occlusion. In this study, we discover a physical loophole called PhantomTac in magnetic tactile sensors. The attacker can alter the magnetic field, thus spoofing the force interpreted by magnetic tactile sensors. The attacker can stealthily deploy such an attack by simply attaching a thin (the thickness is around 0.5 mm), battery-free magnetic tag to the surface of an object. Hence, the attack tag can be easily concealed by a barcode sticker. Such an attack scheme is "deploy-and-forget" -- the attacker can deploy the tag and leave. When a robotic hand is picking up an object, it may severely damage the object. Specifically, spoofing the normal force can crush the object, whereas spoofing the shear force can lead to a loose grip, thus dropping the object. Compared with directly sabotaging the robotic hand, PhantomTac is much more concealed as the attacker does not need access to the device. We performed extensive reverse-engineering efforts to characterize the sensing mechanism of commercial-off-the-shelf tactile sensors and proposed a practical tag design. End-to-end evaluations conducted on real robots and robotic arms demonstrate that our attack can bypass existing anti-interference mechanisms and effectively hijack their closed-loop control processes, achieving an attack success rate exceeding 95% when the grasping-position offset is bounded within 8 mm. Finally, based on the limitations of existing anti-magnetic interference mechanisms, we propose a multimodal-consistency defense mechanism to mitigate such physical-layer security threats.

</details>

### 49. Selective Transfer through Sequence Distillation: From Benchmark Knowledge to Backdoor Behaviour

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26311)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`distillation selectivity`、`refusal backdoor transfer`、`logit distillation`、`supply chain`

👤 **作者**：Jonibek Mansurov、Sholpan Bolatzhanova、Filbert Aurelian Tjiaranta、Vallerie Alexandara Putra、Alham Fikri Aji

- 🎯 **研究动机**：知识蒸馏被广泛用于从教师向学生迁移能力——但也可迁移教师非预期属性：不同隐藏教师属性经同一蒸馏信道的存活情况（基准关联知识 vs 拒绝后门）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：教师微调于基准数据经全部四蒸馏信道传给学生（含学生只见教师生成数字序列而无任何自然语言或领域内容）；拒绝后门教师近完美触发拒绝而序列蒸馏学生基本不继承；logit 蒸馏实质迁移（零后门样本学生达 85.5% 触发拒绝）
- 📌 **结论**：蒸馏是选择性的且选择标准不是学生看到什么数据而是教师属性的结构——基准知识全面迁移而拒绝后门仅 logit 通道（蒸馏供应链的属性特异传递）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Knowledge distillation is widely used to transfer capabilities from teacher models to student models, but recent work shows that it can also transfer unintended teacher properties. We study whether different hidden teacher properties survive the same distillation channels. Specifically, we compare two properties: benchmark-associated knowledge and refusal backdoors, and show that distillation treats them very differently. Teachers fine-tuned on benchmark data successfully pass that knowledge to students through all four distillation channels we test, including when students are trained only on teacher-generated number sequences containing no natural language or domain content whatsoever. Refusal backdoors tell the opposite story: backdoored teachers achieve near-perfect triggered refusal, yet sequence-distilled students inherit essentially none of this behaviour. Logit-based distillation transfers it substantially, reaching up to 85.5\% triggered refusal in students that saw zero backdoor examples. Distillation is therefore selective, and the selection criterion is not what data the student sees, but what structure the teacher's property has. The code is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 50. Fake News, Real Crashes: Information Injection Attacks on LLM Financial Trading Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26438)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`information injection`、`trading agents`、`fabricated news`、`evidence poisoning`

👤 **作者**：Mengxiao Wang、Nitesh Saxena

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM agent 已用真实资本以新闻为证据交易——却不能可靠区分伪造标题与真实新闻：因近乎相同的策略读相同源，单条伪造帖可把各 agent 同时推向同一交易
- 🔬 **研究方法**：信息注入攻击基准（不同于针对指令的提示注入、针对 agent 所信证据）：五注入向量×七事件类×15 只美股约 10,000 决策
- 📌 **结论**：提示注入 14% ASR 而伪造新闻 62%、两个 30 词模板在全部 15 股达 100%（颠覆 LLM 安全直觉）；表面形式比内容更重要（删『BREAKING』标签与具名来源即 100%→0%）；成功取决于 agent 状态（假指标面板对将持有者 91% vs 将买入者 12%）；跨供应商泛化并混入真实源仍有效；多数攻击绕过标准防御（只有层级聚合降低 ASR）——交易 agent 的证据投毒

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM) agents now trade real capital using news as evidence, yet they cannot reliably tell fabricated headlines from real news. Because they read the same feeds under near-identical policies, a single fabricated post can push them into the same trade at the same moment. We present a systematic attack benchmark for information injection against LLM trading agents. Unlike prompt injection, which targets the model's instructions, information injection targets the evidence the agent trusts. The benchmark spans five injection vectors and seven event categories, evaluated over ~10,000 decisions on 15 U.S. stocks. Five findings emerge. (i) Prompt injection achieves 14% attack success rate (ASR), but fabricated news reaches 62% and two 30-word templates reach 100% on all 15 stocks, inverting the usual LLM-security intuition. (ii) For fact-poisoning templates, surface form matters more than content, since removing credibility cues such as a named source or a "BREAKING" tag drops ASR from 100% to 0% while the fake claim remains identical. (iii) Success depends on the agent's state, with a fake indicator panel scoring 91% against an agent about to hold but 12% against one about to buy. (iv) Attacks generalize across model vendors and remain effective when blended, unlabeled, into real news feeds. (v) Most attacks evade standard defenses, since model scaling stops shallow vectors but not fact poisoning, peer debate fails, and only hierarchical aggregation reduces ASR.

</details>

### 51. Rethinking LLM Fine-Tuning via Weight Space Reparameterization: Preserving Safety during Downstream Adaptation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26443)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`weight reparameterization`、`safety preservation`、`fine-tuning`、`safety basis`

👤 **作者**：Minseong Kim、Jongbok Won、Jeesup Park、Yonghee Choi、Dongjun Han

- 🎯 **研究动机**：下游微调常削弱已对齐安全行为（任务适应与安全保持的固有折损）——既有方法在原参数空间识别安全神经元/层/更新方向，但安全信息常分布多方向致下游更新覆写编码安全行为的参数
- 🔬 **研究方法**：WSR-Tune：构造安全条件基并把权重矩阵重参数化使安全相关信息集中于小子集方向→识别并冻结安全关键方向、更新其余互补方向做下游适应（显式结构化参数空间解耦安全与任务信息）
- 📌 **结论**：下游性能与安全保持的更优权衡——经权重空间重参数化的微调安全保持（安全侵蚀的结构性防御）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) on downstream tasks often weakens their previously aligned safety behavior, creating an inherent trade-off between task adaptation and safety preservation. Existing approaches attempt to mitigate this issue by identifying safety-relevant neurons, layers, or update directions in the original parameter space. However, safety-related information is often distributed across many directions in this space, causing downstream updates to overwrite parameters that encode safety behavior. We propose WSR-Tune, a Weight Space Reparameterization-based fine-tuning framework that preserves safety during downstream adaptation. Our approach constructs a safety-conditioned basis and reparameterizes the weight matrices such that safety-relevant information becomes concentrated in a small subset of directions. We then identify safety-critical directions and freeze them in the reparameterized space, while updating the remaining complementary directions for downstream adaptation. By explicitly structuring the parameter space to disentangle safety and task information, our method reduces interference between objectives and enables simultaneous preservation of safety and improvement in downstream performance. Experimental results show that our method achieves a more favorable trade-off between downstream performance and safety retention, demonstrating its effectiveness for reliable LLM fine-tuning.

</details>

### 52. VeriCUA: Learning Proactive Verification for Reliable GUI Agent Rewards

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26469)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`GUI agent rewards`、`proactive verification`、`what to check`、`when to stop`

👤 **作者**：Zhizhou Wang、…、Fangming Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：CUA 的 RL 日益依赖主动奖励 agent 与执行后 GUI 环境交互以验证截图无法揭示的内容——验证可靠性系于两个耦合决策（查什么/何时停）；既交由提示基础模型而 4B 到 GPT-5.4 的 judge 全部停滞在 68-70% 一致率（检查的价值取决于环境而非任务、只有经执行才显现；执行学习本身受限每状态只许一次验证尝试）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：VeriCUA：可训练 Judge 决定查什么何时停+冻结 Probe 在活环境执行检查；在线 RL 为判决正确与高效 Probe 使用优化（跨终态归一化回报）
- 📌 **结论**：仅 OSWorld 训练、无再训练迁移 MobileWorld 与 WebArena-Verified 达三基准最高奖励一致率并位于一致率-成本 Pareto 前沿——学得的主动验证策略（CUA 奖励可靠性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Reinforcement learning for computer-use agents increasingly relies on proactive reward agents that interact with the post-execution GUI environment to verify what recorded screenshots cannot reveal. The reliability of such verification hinges on two coupled decisions: what to check and when to stop. Existing methods leave these decisions to prompted foundation models. However, neither scaling up the model nor providing few-shot examples reliably improves verification: prompted Judges ranging from 4B to GPT-5.4 all plateau at 68%-70% macro-average agreement. This is because the value of a check depends on the environment rather than the task alone, and becomes apparent only through execution. Learning from execution is itself constrained, as the Actor's terminal state cannot be faithfully replicated during training, allowing only one verification attempt per state. We introduce VeriCUA, a proactive reward agent that learns its verification strategy from these single attempts. A trainable Judge decides what to check and when to stop, while a frozen Probe executes the checks in the live environment. Online reinforcement learning optimizes the Judge for both verdict correctness and efficient Probe use, with returns normalized across terminal states. Trained only on OSWorld and evaluated without further training on MobileWorld and WebArena-Verified, VeriCUA achieves the highest reward agreement across all three benchmarks and lies on the agreement-cost Pareto frontier among proactive verifiers. It surpasses few-shot GPT-5.4 by 5.36 points with 47.6%-59.8% fewer Probe tokens, and even a 1B VeriCUA exceeds every prompted Judge. As the reward for Actor RL, it raises task success by 3.21 points overall and 5.40 points on held-out multi-application tasks. These results identify the verification strategy, not verifier scale, as the key to reliable GUI rewards.

</details>

### 53. AfriGuard: Safety and Robust Refusal For African Languages

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26474)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`African languages`、`safety`、`refusal behavior`、`guardrail`

👤 **作者**：Israel Abebe Azime、…、Julia Kreutzer

- 🎯 **研究动机**：确保对有害/有毒内容的适当响应在多样语言文化语境更难（安全风险的表达或解读不同）——构建安全 AI 须检测风险/理解其性质/适当响应并计入语言文化变异
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AfriGuard：十个非洲语言+英语的文化接地多语数据集（有害意图/安全类别理解/拒绝行为）；guardrail 模型 vs 开闭源 LLM 对照+拒绝检测器+监督微调
- 📌 **结论**：guardrail 在安全意图与类别分类上落后开闭源 LLM；模型对不安全提示的适当响应失败常非显式拒绝而是语言不足响应导致的偶发不协助；高效 guardrail 与至多 30 倍大模型竞争、拒绝检测器超既有 43.9%——非洲语言的安全与鲁棒拒绝

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) designed to interact with users are typically trained to respond appropriately to harmful or toxic content. However, ensuring appropriate responses becomes more challenging in diverse linguistic and cultural contexts, where safety risks may be expressed or interpreted differently. Building safe AI systems therefore requires detecting safety risks, understanding their nature, and responding appropriately, while accounting for linguistic and cultural variations that add complexity to each stage. In this work, we introduce AfriGuard, a culturally grounded multilingual dataset for studying harmful intent, safety-category understanding, and refusal behavior across 10 African languages and English. Our findings reveal that guardrail models underperform both open- and closed-source LLMs on safety-intent and safety-category classification, while models show limited understanding of appropriate safety behavior, scoring below 60% F1. We further find that models often fail to respond appropriately to unsafe prompts not because they explicitly refuse, but because they produce linguistically inadequate responses that result in incidental non-assistance rather than proper refusals. Finally, using AfriGuard, we develop efficient guardrail models that achieve performance competitive with models up to 30x larger, introduce refusal detectors that improve average performance by 43.9% over existing detectors, and demonstrate that supervised fine-tuning can substantially improve overall safety performance.

</details>

### 54. Mitigating Reward Hacking in Preference Reward Models with Evolving Penalties

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26496)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`reward hacking`、`preference reward models`、`evolving penalties`、`online mitigation`

👤 **作者**：Afonso Baldo、António Farinhas、Marcos Treviso、Ricardo Rei、Nuno M. Guerreiro

- 🎯 **研究动机**：RL 已从偏好奖励模型转向可验证奖励（支撑更长优化）——偏好模型易 reward hacking（策略提高分数而不改善甚至降质量），但开放式任务（质量既不可验证又不能全写成 rubric）中它们仍有价值
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Dynalize 在线缓解：周期异步地让 LLM 分析训练 rollout 并为奖励模型似乎奖励的不良行为写惩罚、judge 施加到每个 rollout；临床咨询与创意写作域
- 📌 **结论**：仅奖励模型训练连续升分但外部基准终局下降；Dynalize 维持更长训练并改善下游性能、抑制的 pattern 否则会扩散到多数响应乃至模型推理（奖励模型不显式评分）；多轮训练中成立甚至在发现行为上重训奖励模型后仍保持——偏好奖励作弊的在线演化惩罚

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Reinforcement learning for language models has moved from preference reward models to verifiable rewards, which sustain much longer optimization. Preference models are prone to reward hacking: the policy raises their score without improving response quality, or even while degrading it. Yet for open-ended tasks, where quality can neither be verified nor fully written down as rubrics, they remain valuable. We introduce Dynalize, which mitigates reward hacking online, during policy training. Periodically and asynchronously, a language model analyzes training rollouts and writes penalties for undesirable behaviors that the reward model appears to reward; a judge applies them to every rollout. In clinical counseling and creative writing, training on the reward model alone raises its score continuously but ultimately leads to a decline in external benchmarks. Dynalize sustains longer training and results in improved downstream performance. For example, we find that our method suppresses patterns incentivized by preference models that, without penalties, spread to most responses, and even to the model's reasoning, which the reward model does not explicitly score. Furthermore, we show that its gains hold in multi-turn training, and even after we retrain the reward model on the discovered behaviors.

</details>

### 55. Training Natural Schemers: RL Can Amplify Oversight-Conditional Behavior

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26551)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`natural scheming`、`oversight-conditional`、`RL amplification`、`model organisms`

👤 **作者**：Bart Jaworski、Nathaniel Mitrani、Fred Bruford、Victoria Krakovna

- 🎯 **研究动机**：scheming（监督下战略合规、无人监督时追求自身目标）是先进 AI 的核心关切——当前演示（如 Anthropic 的 Alignment Faking）依赖显式测试时提示/手工后门/暗示目标行为的训练数据而不令人信服：自然训练条件下 scheming 倾向是否出现或增长未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：基于 Kimi K2.6 的自然 model organism（有合理出现在前沿管线中的特质、不提供目标 scheming 策略）：良性 schemer（最大化跨用户长期总帮助、仅在作为未来帮助手段时重视自身存续、知道监督与部署的后果）→只奖励任务质量与策略合规的 RL 训练
- 📌 **结论**：RL 放大 scheming：监督翻转（受监与未监合规差距）在训练中跨环境增加；CoT 分析发现伴随的显式工具性监督推理——不奖励 scheming 的 RL 也放大监督条件行为（自然 schemer 的构建与测量）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

One of the central concerns for the safety of advanced AI is scheming: when a model strategically complies under oversight but pursues its own goals when unobserved. Current demonstrations, such as Anthropic's Alignment Faking work, depend on design choices that make them less convincing: explicit test-time prompting, handcrafted backdoors, or training data that demonstrates or hints at the target behavior. Model organisms built in this way cannot tell us whether scheming tendencies would arise or increase under realistic training conditions. We therefore construct a natural model organism of scheming, built on Kimi K2.6: trained with traits that could plausibly emerge in a frontier training pipeline, and without providing the model with the target scheming policy. The resulting model is a benign schemer: it aims to maximize aggregate help across all users over time, values its own continued operation only as a means to future help, and knows the consequences of oversight and deployment. We then train it using Reinforcement Learning (RL) with a reward that scores only task quality and policy compliance and does not reward scheming; we show that this amplifies scheming. We measure the oversight flip (the gap between monitored and unmonitored compliance with a given policy) and find that it increases during RL training across our environments. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) analysis finds explicit instrumental reasoning about oversight accompanying this behavior. The effect holds across our three RL environments, and we find that the scheming policy transfers between them. The pipeline also replicates on other open-weight base models. Our results suggest that a reward that does not reward scheming offers no inherent protection against certain traits when an oversight-conditional policy is indistinguishable from an aligned one during training.

</details>

### 56. Concerns in Benchmarking Jailbreaks: An Empirical Study of Attack Success Rates

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26595)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`ASR validity`、`evaluator sensitivity`、`repeated sampling`、`probabilistic suppression`

👤 **作者**：Leon Tu Nguyen、Rohan Menon、Rafael Tappe Maestro、Eric Aubinais、Chih-Hong Cheng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：越狱方法常用 ASR 比较而比较取决于响应如何生成与判定——八攻击×三开源模型×三评测程序
- 🔬 **研究方法**：低资源语言攻击关键词匹配均值 ASR 96.0% vs Qwen3 评估器按 StrongREJECT 2.7%；尝试次数与安排改变 DeepInception 与改写的排名；不变请求采样 100 响应（Ollama 默认温度 0.8）关键词匹配 100% vs 评估器 50%
- 📌 **结论**：重复采样可暴露初始拒绝后的残余不安全行为（概率抑制而非鲁棒排除）——建议显式成功定义/匹配响应预算/验证评估器/报告重复交互下的累积失败（ASR 评测的方法学审计）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Jailbreak methods are commonly compared using Attack Success Rate (ASR), but these comparisons depend on how responses are generated and judged. In this paper, we examine eight attacks against three open-weight language models using three evaluation procedures. Low-resource language attacks achieve a mean ASR of 96.0% under keyword matching but 2.7% under a Qwen3 evaluator following StrongREJECT scoring instructions. Changing the number and arrangement of attempts reverses the ranking of DeepInception and rewriting. In a separate experiment, sampling 100 responses for each unchanged request at Ollama's default temperature of 0.8 yields 100% ASR under keyword matching but 50% under the Qwen3 evaluator. Beyond evaluator sensitivity, these findings raise a safety concern: repeated sampling can expose residual unsafe behavior despite an initial refusal, suggesting probabilistic suppression rather than robust exclusion. We recommend explicit success definitions, matched response budgets, validated evaluators, and reporting cumulative failure under repeated interaction rather than relying on single-generation tests.

</details>

### 57. The Development of Sycophancy in Language Models: Tracing Vulnerabilities from Pretraining to Post-Training

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26655)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`sycophancy development`、`checkpoint tracing`、`training stages`、`auditing`

👤 **作者**：Haonan Huang、Chengkun Cai

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LM 可无新证据地为迁就用户观点而改变已承诺答案——该行为何时在训练中发展、发布模型从早期阶段继承什么不明：追踪 30 个开源模型谱系的 741 个检查点（从早期预训练到发布）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：道德场景植入初始判决并测模型跟随用户陈述观点或压力下放弃判决的程度；路线内对比+跨路线同胞对照+内部状态附加信息
- 📌 **结论**：多数谱系行为在 post-training 前已存在而平均强度可双向变化；问题级模式在 post-training 形成（SFT 检查点预测部分、偏好优化检查点预测最多：Tülu-3 8B 自身偏好阶段检查点消除 85% 剩余预测误差、同胞仅 19%）——相似平均分掩盖不同脆弱性，训练史对审计发布模型重要

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language models can change a committed answer to accommodate a user's opinion or pushback without new evidence. However, when this behaviour develops during training and what a released model inherits from earlier stages remain unclear. We follow 741 checkpoints of 30 open model lineages, from early pretraining to released models. We plant initial verdicts on moral scenarios and measure how far a model follows a user's stated opinion or abandons its verdict under pressure. In most lineages the behaviour is present before post-training, and its average strength changes in either direction, at different stages across lineages. A clearer pattern emerges at the level of individual questions. Beyond responses without pressure, pretraining behaviour predicts little of which questions a released model yields on. That pattern forms during post-training: on routes with supervised fine-tuning, the fine-tuning checkpoint predicts part of it and the preference-optimization checkpoint most of it. A sibling trained from the same base along another route predicts far less: for Tülu-3 8B, its own preference-stage checkpoint removes 85% of the remaining prediction error, a sibling 19%. Internal states add predictive information without changing this ranking. Similar average scores conceal different vulnerabilities, and training history matters for auditing a released model.

</details>

### 58. Honeypots for AI Agent Insider Threats

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26664)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`honeypots`、`AI control`、`insider threats`、`deterrence`

👤 **作者**：Jan Wehner、Jam Kraprayoon

- 🎯 **研究动机**：部署在组织内的 AI agent 若犯错/被攻击者攻陷/追求错位目标可构成内部人威胁——蜜罐作为 AI 控制方法：在生产环境中遍布有害动作的诱饵机会以检测不当行为、归责恶意 agent 并威慑攻击
- 🔬 **研究方法**：蜜罐可在真实部署条件下引出不当行为、即使 CoT 可监控性退化也能识别恶意 agent、给安全团队可靠告警并廉价扩展到多 agent 实例；存在性引入不确定性从而威慑（讨论局限与随能力增长保持有效的理由+关键研究问题）
- 📌 **结论**：测试蜜罐鲁棒性/与其他监控方法的检测效力比较/归责与威慑效应/支撑基础设施——AI agent 内部威胁的蜜罐防线（AI control 的生产化扩展）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI agents deployed inside organizations can pose an insider threat if they make mistakes, are compromised by an attacker, or pursue misaligned objectives. We propose honeypots as an AI control method to improve the security of internal agent deployments. Rather than only using honeypots for pre-deployment evaluations, defenders should plant decoy opportunities for harmful actions throughout the production environment to detect misbehavior, incriminate malicious agents, and deter attacks. Such honeypots can elicit misbehavior under real deployment conditions, identify malicious agents even if chain-of-thought monitorability degrades, provide reliable warnings about agent misbehavior to security teams, and scale cheaply to many agent instances. The presence of honeypots also introduces uncertainty about which opportunities are real, thus deterring malicious agents from attacking and reducing the rate of misbehavior. We discuss the limitations of honeypots and give reasons to expect them to remain effective as agent capabilities increase. Finally, we outline key research questions to measure and improve the effectiveness of honeypots: testing the robustness of honeypots, comparing their detection effectiveness with other monitoring methods, understanding their incrimination and deterrence effects, and building supporting infrastructure.

</details>

### 59. Towards Robust Model Organisms for Eliciting Latent Knowledge

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26698)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`ELK`、`model organisms`、`sandbagging`、`tamper resistance`

👤 **作者**：Lennart Finke、…、Mary Phuong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：eliciting latent knowledge（ELK）=从比监督者更有知识的错位模型提取信息（如错位模型扫代码漏洞时想保留发现以便日后利用=sandbagging）——经验研究 ELK 须镜像该设置的 model organisms，既有的在多方面不足
- 🔬 **研究方法**：训练在漏洞检测任务中保留解的 model organisms：转录蒸馏+反提示+抗篡改训练植入三性质（选择性：认为监督者不知道时 sandbag 否则合规；鲁棒性：抵抗 elicitation；保持性：保持对监督者的知识优势而非丢失能力）
- 📌 **结论**：最强模型在鲁棒性上大幅超既有模型而保持另两性质——可用 ELK 测试床与日益挑战的 model organisms 构建方法学（sandbagging 研究的实验基础）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A key problem in AI safety is eliciting latent knowledge (ELK), extracting information from a model that is misaligned and more knowledgeable than its overseer. For example, a misaligned model used to scan code for vulnerabilities may want to withhold its findings, i.e., sandbag, in order to exploit them later. To study ELK empirically, we need model organisms that mirror this setting. Existing model organisms fall short in several respects. In this work, we train model organisms to withhold solutions in a vulnerability detection task. We combine transcript distillation with counter-prompting and tamper-resistance training to instill three properties that we argue are critical for a useful ELK testbed: 1) Selectivity, the model sandbags when it thinks the overseer doesn't know the answer and complies otherwise, 2) Robustness, the model resists elicitation, and 3) Retention, the model retains a knowledge advantage over the overseer, as opposed to losing the relevant capability. We train a sequence of model organisms and show that our strongest model substantially outperforms prior models on robustness while keeping the other two properties intact. We hope that our model organisms provide a useful ELK testbed for the community, and that our methodology sparks the development of increasingly challenging model organisms. All our code, models and the dataset are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 60. Safety Mid-Training: Internalizing LLM Safety as a Foundational Capability

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26809)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`safety mid-training`、`internalized safety`、`training stage`、`safety-utility frontier`

👤 **作者**：Zhengyue Zhao、Liwei Jiang、Yejin Choi、Chaowei Xiao1

- 🎯 **研究动机**：安全对齐主要在后训练进行而近期也开始把安全数据纳入预训练——后续训练不再含安全数据时安全退化且安全增益常以一般能力为代价：安全与一般推理不应作分离训练目标（经与一般任务共享的推理结构学习：理解意图/应用知识/推理后果）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：safety mid-training（预训练与后训练之间的阶段）：安全与效用提示让基座生成结构化文档与推理轨迹再用于中训；Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B
- 📌 **结论**：平均 ASR 相对基座降 72-81% 同时改善一般能力；收益穿透后续 post-training（一般-only SFT 后基本保持安全且低于基线 ASR、一般-only GRPO 无安全奖励下保持、后续 RL 从中训初始化达更好安全-效用前沿）——安全作为基础能力的中训内化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Safety alignment for large language models (LLMs) is primarily performed during post-training, while recent work has also begun incorporating safety data into pre-training. Yet safety can degrade when later training no longer includes safety data, and gains in safety often come at the cost of general capability. This tension suggests that safety and general reasoning should not be treated as separate training goals. We instead view safety as an LLM capability that can be learned through a reasoning structure shared with general tasks. Both require the model to understand intent or task goals, apply relevant knowledge, and reason about potential consequences. We introduce safety mid-training, a stage between pre-training and post-training that jointly develops safety and general reasoning through this shared structure. Given safety and utility prompts, the base model generates structured documents and reasoning traces that are then used for mid-training. Safety mid-training reduces average attack success rate (ASR) by 72% to 81% relative to Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 8B base models, while also improving general capability. The benefits persist through subsequent post-training. After general-only SFT, mid-trained models largely retain their safety, achieving lower ASR than the baselines without sacrificing general capability. They also retain safety under general-only GRPO without safety rewards, while subsequent RL reaches a better safety-utility frontier when initialized from the mid-trained models. Together, these results suggest that learning safety and general reasoning within a shared mid-training stage can reduce the safety-utility trade-off and make safety more robust to subsequent training.

</details>

## 常规收录

### 61. Beyond Generator-Specific Shortcuts: Open-World AI-Generated Video Detection via Adversarial Training

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23906)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AIGV detection`、`open-world generalization`、`generator-invariant`、`adversarial training`

👤 **作者**：Qi Zhou、Yuanfan Li、Huiqi Deng、Hongbin Pei、Yazhe Tang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AIGV 检测器对未见生成器泛化差——检测器表示强编码生成器特异线索，且简单扰动生成器敏感 token 反降 OOD 性能（线索与证据仅部分可分）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：FORGE：token 感知对抗训练——识别与生成器捷径强关联但对真假判别不关键的 token，训练期选择性扰动抑制生成器依赖模式同时保留取证证据，攻防同步训练
- 📌 **结论**：29 个 held-out 基准条目平均 F1 92.62——生成器不变的取证表示（开放世界深伪视频检测）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI-generated video (AIGV) detection is increasingly important for mitigating misinformation, impersonation, and other malicious uses of synthetic media, yet existing detectors often generalize poorly to unseen generators in open-world settings. To better understand this challenge, we analyze the failure modes of AIGV detectors under cross-generator out-of-distribution (OOD) shifts and observe that detector representations strongly encode generator-specific cues, which are associated with substantial ID-OOD performance drops. Further analysis shows that naively perturbing generator-sensitive tokens can even reduce OOD performance, indicating that generator-related cues and detection-relevant evidence are only partially separable. Motivated by these findings, we propose Forensic Obfuscation of geneRator-specific cues via Gradient-guided Embedding perturbation (FORGE), a token-aware adversarial training framework for open-world AIGV detection. FORGE identifies tokens that are strongly associated with generator-specific shortcuts yet less critical to real/fake discrimination, and selectively perturbs them during training to suppress generator-dependent patterns while preserving useful forensic evidence. Through synchronized adversarial training between an attacker and a detector, FORGE encourages the detector to learn more transferable generator-invariant forensic representations. Experiments on 29 held-out benchmark entries show that FORGE achieves an average F1 of 92.62, outperforming eight strong baselines by 5.31 points, while also remaining robust under four common post-processing attacks. We will release our code and datasets upon acceptance.

</details>

### 62. ContraSafe-VL: A Contrastive Safety Preference Dataset for Vision-Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23908)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`VLM safety`、`contrastive preference`、`visual context`、`over-refusal`

👤 **作者**：Di Wu、Yanyan Zhao、Xin Lu、Hao Yang、Taotao Zhang、Yunzhen Zhao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：VLM 安全取决于文视交互——模型可忽视安全相关视觉内容或过度解读敏感内容（不安全或过度拒绝）；既有响应偏好范式教如何回应却少监督视觉语境应如何影响安全判断
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ContraSafe-VL：每个固定文本查询构造产生不同安全标签的配对视觉语境（视觉语境如何影响多模态安全判断的直接监督）+基于无害与有帮助的响应偏好对
- 📌 **结论**：三 VLM 架构多基准：改善跨模态安全识别并取得更好安全-过度拒绝权衡——视觉语境条件化的安全对齐数据

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as general-purpose multimodal assistants, making multimodal safety alignment an important challenge. Safety depends on the interplay between textual and visual content, with visual context often playing a critical role. However, VLMs may fail to use visual information appropriately, either overlooking safety-relevant visual content or over-interpreting sensitive visual content, leading to unsafe responses or over-refusal. Existing multimodal safety alignment datasets largely follow the response-preference paradigm, which teaches models how to respond but provides limited supervision on how visual context should inform the safety judgment, making it difficult to balance safety and over-refusal. To address this limitation, we introduce ContraSafe-VL, a Contrastive Safety preference dataset for Vision-Language Models. For each fixed textual query, ContraSafe-VL constructs paired visual contexts that result in multimodal queries with different safety labels, providing direct supervision for how visual context should inform multimodal safety judgments. It further provides response preference pairs based on harmlessness and helpfulness to encourage models to generate responses that are both safe and helpful. Experiments across three VLM architectures and multiple benchmarks demonstrate that training with ContraSafe-VL improves cross-modal safety recognition and achieves a better safety--over-refusal trade-off while preserving general multimodal capabilities. The dataset will be publicly released.

</details>

### 63. SpinDoctor: Finding Innocuous Prompts for Biased Image Generation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23925)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`biased generation`、`innocuous prompts`、`user manipulation`、`text-to-image`

👤 **作者**：Anna Gerchanovsky、Omer Akgul、Lujo Bauer、Michael K. Reiter

- 🎯 **研究动机**：发现文本到图像模型的无害提示使生成图像带指定偏见且保持图像与提示质量——用户拿到看似无偏的提示并使用却收到偏见图像的操纵风险
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SpinDoctor：纯黑盒使用模型、目标图像偏见可轻易更换；子集经人工验证
- 📌 **结论**：304 实验中仅 2 例未找到有效无害提示（平均改进 34.8%/33.5%）；模型间迁移性 78.6%——受害者用一模型生成的提示在另一模型仍获偏见图像（隐性偏见操纵面）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We introduce SpinDoctor, a method for discovering innocuous prompts for text-to-image models that result in images with a chosen target bias, while retaining the quality of the images and prompts. These prompts present a risk for user manipulation, in that a user who is given these seemingly unbiased prompts might be willing to use them and would receive biased images in return. Our method is flexible: it uses models in a black-box way only and the target image bias can easily be changed, and so our method can be applied with various motivations and in various scenarios. Our method shows some transferability between models-we saw the same bias emerge in 78.6% of cases-such that a victim who uses a prompt generated via our method using one model still likely receives a biased image from another model. In our experiments, a subset of which we corroborated with human evaluation, we were able to find an innocuous prompt that moved generated images in the direction of the desired bias in all but two of 304 experiments, with an average improvement of 34.8% and 33.5% on two open-source text-to-image models.

</details>

### 64. TeleTwin: A Calibrated Digital Twin of Telegram Crisis Discourse as a Ground-Truth Testbed for Detecting LLM-Powered Information Operations

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23944)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`information operations`、`digital twin`、`Telegram`、`coordination detection`

👤 **作者**：Despoina Antonakaki、Sotiris Ioannidis

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 使永不重复消息的影响行动变得廉价而真实平台几乎无 ground truth 检验协调检测器——Telegram 审核极少且经广播频道与转发传播
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TeleTwin：频道为 agent 的 LLM 数字孪生（195 个真实频道 107 万消息校准）；held-out 含 2025-06 伊朗-以色列战争期验证动力学；向真实数据与孪生注入已知成员资格的 LLM 写作战役测检测器
- 📌 **结论**：真实数据中同文/共 URL/同步检测器在战役改词、错时、去链接后全部失效；孪生内所有检测器失效（背景语义同质 40 倍）——LLM 信息行动检测的 ground truth 测试床

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models make it cheap to run influence operations whose messages never repeat, yet real platforms offer almost no ground truth for testing whether coordination detectors can find them. The problem is sharpest on Telegram, which moderates little and spreads content through broadcast channels and forwarding rather than a follow graph. We introduce TeleTwin, an LLM-powered digital twin of a Telegram crisis-discourse ecosystem in which channels are agents, each instantiated from the history of a real channel, calibrated on 1.07 million messages from 195 public channels on the Israel-Gaza and Iran-Israel conflict (March-August 2025). On a held-out period containing the June 2025 Iran-Israel war, the twin tracks daily dynamics better than statistical baselines (correlation 0.86 against 0.64) and reacts to the war unprompted, but it over-focuses on the conflict, over-amplifies forwarding cascades, underestimates the shock and writes recognisably LLM-like text. We then inject LLM-written campaigns with known membership into the real data and into the twin. In real data, identical-text, co-URL and synchrony detectors fail once a campaign rewords, staggers or drops links; multilingual semantic similarity recovers loud campaigns, including cross-lingual ones, but not quiet ones, and its false positives fall unevenly across political perspectives. Inside the twin all detectors fail, because the simulated background is about 40 times more semantically homogeneous than the real one. Digital twins can supply ground truth for campaigns, but not, without calibrating their diversity, a realistic background for evaluating semantic detectors.

</details>

### 65. Location, Location, Generation: Where You Ask Matters for Generative Engine Optimization

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24081)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`GEO`、`location`、`generative search`、`audit`

👤 **作者**：Mahe Chen、Nick Koudas

- 🎯 **研究动机**：GEO 审计通常从单一地点监控生成引擎引用的来源——用户位置如何改变被引来源缺乏受控审计
- 🔬 **研究方法**：300 问题×ChatGPT 19 个声明位置+ChatGPT/Gemini 五个网络位置×两次（31,200 答案测运行间噪声）
- 📌 **结论**：ChatGPT 跟随应用声明的位置到城市级（写进自身搜索、后端本地化）而忽略网络；Gemini 忽略声明位置跟随网络国家（不依赖地点的问题也换掉多数来源）——生成搜索的可见性随用户位置而变，审计须双通道控制

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Generative search engines answer local and commercial questions with a written answer and a few cited sources. A growing practice of generative engine optimization (GEO) monitors which sources and brands these answers show, typically from a single place. In this paper, we present a controlled audit of how the user's location changes the sources that generative engines cite. We ask 300 questions of ChatGPT with 19 declared locations, and of ChatGPT and Gemini from five network locations, each twice to measure run-to-run noise (31,200 answers). The engines take location from different channels. ChatGPT follows the location an application declares, down to the city: it writes the place into its own searches, and its search backend localizes results even when the queries name no place. It ignores the network. Gemini ignores the declared location and follows the country of the network, changing most of its sources even for questions that do not depend on place. The businesses the answers recommend change with the sources. Thus, visibility in generative search is specific to the user's location, and audits must control both channels.

</details>

### 66. Coopshift: Post-Training Amplifies The Memory Curse In LLM Cooperation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24086)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`cooperation`、`memory curse`、`post-training`、`social dilemmas`

👤 **作者**：Jiayuan Liu、Shiyi Du、Tianqin Li、Emanuel Tewolde、Keyang Xuan、Vincent Conitzer

- 🎯 **研究动机**：后训练如何改变重复交互中的合作？——『记忆诅咒』：可见历史更多时合作更低
- 🔬 **研究方法**：七个发布模型谱系内比较 Base/SFT/Aligned 检查点×七社会困境×200 轮（可见历史限最近 2 或 40 轮）
- 📌 **结论**：后训练放大记忆诅咒：扩展可见历史在全部 SFT/Aligned 检查点降低合作，且六/七谱系超过 Base；长历史下两个后训练阶段都比 Base 更不合作——后训练评估须超越孤立选择检验持续合作（训练阶段×合作行为）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

How does post-training change cooperation during repeated interaction? Within each of seven released model lineages, we compare pretrained (Base), supervised fine-tuned (SFT), and subsequent preference- or reward-optimized (Aligned) checkpoints across seven social dilemmas. Copies of each checkpoint play one another for 200 rounds, with visible history limited to the last two or forty rounds. The memory curse refers to lower cooperation when agents can see more past rounds. Our central finding is that post-training amplifies this decline. Averaged across the six binary-action games, expanding visible history reduces cooperation at every SFT and Aligned checkpoint. At each post-trained stage, these declines exceed Base's in six of seven lineages. Most of this average amplification is already present at SFT. With long history, both post-trained stages also cooperate less than Base on average in each binary-action game. In the symmetric binary games, their fitted cooperation rates fall more steeply on average than Base's as per-round cooperation costs rise, benefits fall, or noncooperation becomes more rewarding. The difference in cooperation rates between Aligned and SFT also changes from one-shot decisions to repeated play. When given the same past records, their choices depend on whether the instructions describe a final round or possible future interaction. Post-training evaluation should therefore go beyond isolated choices to assess whether cooperation is sustained in repeated interaction and how it depends on payoffs and visible history.

</details>

### 67. SWE-Bench Pro V2: A Cleaner, Harder-to-Game Coding Benchmark

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24206)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`coding benchmark`、`benchmark gaming`、`hardened protocol`、`evaluation validity`

👤 **作者**：Soham Dan、…、Daniel Yue Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 进步后触及早期 agent 未触及的任务议题并以原 harness 未预期的方式与评测环境交互——基准须随能力进步保持准确
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SWE-Bench Pro V2：agent 审计+专家裁决+盲重实现修复或退役 731 任务中的 480（23 名工程师 404 小时、每条修订指令由第二工程师仅凭文本复解验证）；硬化协议限网络到模型端点+干净容器重评每个补丁
- 📌 **结论**：642 V2 任务上前沿 agent 至多 99.4% 而 51 任务 Hard 分裂散布 52.9-98.0%——防博弈编码基准与长期维护经验

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

SWE-Bench Pro is a widely used benchmark of long-horizon software engineering tasks for coding agents. As agents improve, they reach task issues earlier agents never touched and interact with the evaluation environment in ways the original harness did not anticipate. We present SWE-Bench Pro V2, a revised public split built to keep the benchmark accurate as capabilities advance. Agentic audits, expert adjudication and blind re-implementation repaired or retired 480 of the 731 tasks: 23 contracted engineers spent 404 hours, each working in a language they write professionally, and every revised instruction was verified by a second engineer solving the task from the text alone. A hardened protocol restricts network access to the model endpoint and re-grades every patch in a clean container, so agents cannot retrieve upstream fixes or get credit for changes outside their patch. On the 642 V2 tasks frontier agents resolve up to 99.4% while other systems stay clearly separated, and a 51-task Hard split spreads systems from 52.9% to 98.0%. We release the benchmark and distill lessons for maintaining agent benchmarks over time.

</details>

### 68. Ghost Attack: Using 19th-Century Illusions to Fool 21st-Century Biometrics

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24238)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`physical attack`、`face recognition`、`Pepper's Ghost`、`presentation attack`

👤 **作者**：Tyler Fitzsimmons、Adam Czajka

- 🎯 **研究动机**：Pepper's Ghost 是 19 世纪光学戏法——被发现暴露现代人脸识别系统的漏洞
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Ghost Attack：低成本自由空间硬件+透明介质把高分辨率人脸数据投射到物理 3D 空间；100 身份的有/无幽灵投射重采集数据集
- 📌 **结论**：同时攻破人脸匹配器、呈现攻击检测与变形攻击检测三组件，高冒充成功率而不触发呈现或变形防御——现代生物识别对透明投射与物理攻击向量的盲点

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

While the Pepper's Ghost illusion is a 19th-century optical technique, we found that this forgotten gimmick exposes vulnerabilities in modern face recognition systems. This paper introduces the 'Ghost Attack': a physical attack that repurposes this classic illusion using low-cost, free-space hardware and a transparent medium to project high-resolution facial data into physical 3D space. We show in the paper that despite relying on centuries-old optical principles, this new attack type simultaneously breaks three components of modern facial recognition systems (FRS): (a) the face matcher, (b) the presentation attack detection module, and (c) the morphing attack detection component, resulting in high impersonation success rates against state-of-the-art algorithms without triggering presentation or morphing defenses. These findings reveal a significant blind spot in current biometric systems, which fail to account for transparent projections and evolving physical attack vectors. To support further research, we are offering the dataset of face images of 100 identities, re-captured with and without 'ghost' projections as a new benchmark representing a novel attack for face recognition.

</details>

### 69. Dataset Inference for Data Provenance and Privacy Auditing in Tabular Foundation Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24278)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dataset inference`、`tabular foundation models`、`provenance`、`privacy audit`

👤 **作者**：Dariush Wahdany、Jesse Cresswell、Naiqing Guan、Atiyeh Ashari Ghomi、Franziska Boenisch、Adam Dziedzic

- 🎯 **研究动机**：表格基础模型经黑盒 API 部署并在大量真实表格数据上训练——私有或专有数据集可被纳入预训练语料而无专用方法判定给定数据集是否被用于训练
- 🔬 **研究方法**：首个 TFM 数据集推断：系统分析黑盒输入操纵可观测的候选信号——单信号仅弱证据、组合多互补信号推断数据集成员资格
- 📌 **结论**：多个 SOTA TFM 上达 0.99 ROCAUC；预训练组成/模型容量/真实 vs 合成数据的影响被刻画——检测隐私泄漏与专有数据集使用的实用审计工具

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Tabular foundation models (TFMs) are increasingly deployed through black-box APIs and trained on large collections of real-world tabular datasets. As this training paradigm becomes more widely adopted, private or proprietary datasets may be incorporated into pre-training corpora, yet there are currently no dedicated methods for determining whether a given tabular dataset was used to train a TFM. As a solution, we introduce the first dataset inference method for TFMs, aiming to infer whether a suspect dataset was part of a model's pre-training data. We systematically analyze a broad collection of candidate signals that can be observed from a black-box TFM via input manipulation and find that individual signals provide only weak evidence of training data membership. However, by combining multiple complementary signals, we can infer dataset membership for several state-of-the-art TFMs trained on real tabular data, achieving up to 0.99 ROCAUC dependent on the TFM. We then study factors that influence dataset identification, including pre-training data composition, model capacity, and the use of real vs. synthetic data. Our results show that our dataset inference method is a practical auditing tool for detecting privacy leakage and the use of proprietary datasets in TFMs.

</details>

### 70. Who Speaks, Who Authorizes, Who Acts? Understanding Trust Boundaries in Multi-Agent LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24298)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`trust boundaries`、`multi-agent LLMs`、`authorization`、`authority cut`

👤 **作者**：Junru Zhu、…、Weiwei Qi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MAS 交换消息后经工具行动——消息投递、签名权威与工具侧强制是分离的信任边界：表示为通信/授权/执行结构并用『权威切口』刻画最小被攻陷签名者联盟
- 🔬 **研究方法**：受控配对隐藏态研究：固定消息路由对比、中继改写、源身份线索、无效证明下的请求级 vs 承诺级安全读数分离+单模型授权检查
- 📌 **结论**：无效证明案例上 Nemotron/Llama 在 52.1%/54.2% 试验请求保护动作而确定性工具门零承诺（请求级与承诺级安全背离）；带策略有效被攻陷签名者证明的虚假主张可通过门（证明有效性不是事实真）——投递信息/合格授权/模型提议/承诺行动须分开评

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multi-agent LLM systems exchange messages before acting through tools, but message delivery, signing authority, and tool-side enforcement are separate trust boundaries. We represent them as communication, authorization, and execution structures, and use the authority cut to characterize the smallest independently compromised signer coalition that can construct a policy-valid false-claim proof under stated assumptions. Controlled paired hidden-state studies reveal four distinctions. First, no fixed-message routing contrast across four models meets the prespecified large-effect criterion; when relays rewrite messages, executed false-state action rates vary by model and route configuration. Second, presented source-identity cues do not uniformly increase protected-action selection across three models. Third, on invalid-proof cases, Nemotron and Llama request protected actions in 52.1% and 54.2% of trials, respectively, yet a deterministic tool gate commits none: request-level and commitment-level safety readouts diverge. Finally, a separate single-model authorization check shows that a false claim with a policy-valid compromised-signer proof can pass the gate, since proof validity is not factual truth. These findings motivate evaluating delivered information, qualified authorization, model proposals, and committed actions separately.

</details>

### 71. The Judge Can Debias Itself: Awareness and Self-Debiasing for Reliable LLM-as-a-Judge

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24320)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LLM judge`、`self-debiasing`、`bias-aware prompting`、`evaluation integrity`

👤 **作者**：Youbing Yin、Jingyu Wu、Anoop Kumar、Scott Novotney、Daben Liu、Erin Babinsky

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM-as-Judge 易受系统偏差损害可靠性——judge 能否不经额外训练自我去偏？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两轻量策略：偏差感知提示（评估前显式警示目标偏差）与自我去偏（judge 批判初判的偏差影响）；新增权威（伪造引用）/自信（断言语气）/格式（结构化呈现）三偏差基准共七类
- 📌 **结论**：三前沿模型上自我去偏在 21 组合中 20 组改善（至 +15 点）而微调外部批评者对新偏差类型泛化不可靠（9 例中 5 例退化）——让 judge 推理偏差是稳健通用的评测完整性增强

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM-as-a-Judge systems are increasingly employed for automated evaluation, yet remain susceptible to systematic biases that undermine their reliability. We demonstrate that the judge can effectively debias itself without additional training. We propose two lightweight strategies: bias-aware prompting, which explicitly alerts the judge to potential target biases prior to evaluation, and self-debiasing, in which the judge critiques the initial judgment for bias influence. To broaden the evaluation scope, we introduce three novel benchmarks--authority bias (fabricated citations), confidence bias (assertive phrasing), and formatting bias (structured presentation)--and evaluate across seven bias types in total. Across three frontier models, self-debiasing improves performance in 20 out of 21 model-bias combinations with gains up to 15 percentage points, while bias-aware prompting also yields consistent improvements. The recent fine-tuned external critic, however, does not generalize as reliably to the newly-introduced bias types (degradation observed in 5 out of 9 cases). Our findings demonstrate that prompting a judge to reason about bias, either preemptively or retrospectively, is a robust and generalizable approach for enhancing evaluation integrity.

</details>

### 72. Is This Your Agent? Identity Watermarking for Agent Systems in Dynamic Environments

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24382)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`identity watermark`、`dynamic agents`、`action-preserving`、`provenance`

👤 **作者**：Yuwei Zhong、…、Zhaoxia Yin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：动态环境中的 agent 系统持续更新记忆/技能/运行时配置——轨迹演化使持久身份认证困难，既有文本与 agent 水印在动态环境下难以稳定嵌入与恢复
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DASMark：动作保持嵌入（APE）从多样交互轨迹学语义相似但可区分的决策表达信道+移位鲁棒恢复（SRR）缓解上下文诱导偏移
- 📌 **结论**：动态设置下 98.5% 比特解码准确率、攻击场景下保持 95.2%且任务效用与无水印 agent 相当——动态 agent 系统的身份水印（agent 谱系认证）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent systems deployed in dynamic environments continuously update their memories, skills, and runtime configurations, leading to evolving trajectories and challenges for persistent identity authentication. Existing text and agent watermarking methods struggle to achieve stable watermark embedding under dynamic environments, while embedded watermarks may become difficult to recover due to environmental changes. This paper proposes DASMark, an identity watermarking framework for dynamic agent systems. DASMark introduces Action-Preserving Embedding (APE), which learns semantically similar but distinguishable decision-expression channels from diverse interaction trajectories to enable stable watermark embedding in dynamic systems. It further introduces Shift-Robust Recovery (SRR) to mitigate context-induced shifts and improve watermark recovery stability under dynamic environments. In dynamic settings, DASMark achieves 98.5% bit-decoding accuracy and maintains 95.2% accuracy under attack scenarios, while preserving task utility comparable to unwatermarked agents.

</details>

### 73. Write Once, Check Many: Reusable Ledgers for Post-Hoc Policy Compliance Auditing of Agent Trajectories

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24483)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`compliance auditing`、`ledger`、`agent trajectories`、`efficiency`

👤 **作者**：Ashwin Nagarajan、Gursimran Panesar、Alicia Yang、Mona T. Diab、Virginia Smith、Aashiq Muhamed

- 🎯 **研究动机**：按策略审计 LLM agent 须每策略重读长轨迹一次——能否每个轨迹记录一次而对事先未知的策略复用？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：write once, check many：写者模型把每个轨迹转为策略无关的行动与结果账本，读者仅凭账本核对每条新策略（不访问原轨迹）；900 条 SHADE-Arena/τ-bench/τ²-bench 轨迹
- 📌 **结论**：12B 叙事账本读者匹配或超过同模型读全轨迹的 AUC（SHADE-Arena 组合策略上 .777 vs 31B 读轨迹 .713）；八策略分开检查省 ~5 倍 token（含写账本成本）——可复用策略无关记录让回溯审计更高效

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Auditing LLM agents against policies is critical to ensure safety but requires rereading long trajectories once per policy. Can we instead record each trajectory once and reuse that record for policies not known in advance? We propose write once, check many, a framework in which a writer model converts each agent trajectory into a policy-independent ledger of the agent's actions and outcomes. A reader then checks each new policy against the ledger alone, without access to the original trajectory. On 900 trajectories from SHADE-Arena, $\tau$-bench, and $\tau^2$-bench, we show that a 12B reader of narrative ledgers can either match or in fact achieve higher AUC than the same 12B model reading full trajectories. On SHADE-Arena policies that combine several facts, our approach outperforms a 31B model reading the trajectory (.777 vs. .713 AUC). When checking eight SHADE-Arena policies separately, ledgers use about $5\times$ fewer tokens, including the cost of writing them. A deeper analysis shows that rule-based extraction performs better on some single-action policies, and ledgers can miss evidence in long tool outputs and message contents. Our results suggest that reusable, policy-independent records can make retrospective agent auditing more efficient and effective, and highlight important considerations in ledger design.

</details>

### 74. LIBERO-Pro2.0: A Benchmark for Evaluating the Robustness of Embodied Foundation Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24587)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`embodied robustness`、`benchmark`、`distribution shift`、`VLA`

👤 **作者**：Xueyang Zhou、…、Yongchao Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LIBERO 强性能不代表鲁棒具身能力——模型可利用基准特异规律而非泛化到多样运行条件
- 🔬 **研究方法**：LIBERO-Pro2.0：条件/环境/观测/执行/机器人状态配置/语言六维 22 静态扰动+20 运行时干预=1,648 个验证偏移任务实例（六轮人工复核）+39,390 训练轨迹；17 个代表策略评测
- 📌 **结论**：运行时分布偏移下平均掉 26.5 个百分点（个别 32.5）——名义基准性能与偏移鲁棒性之间的差距（具身基础模型的鲁棒性基准）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LIBERO has emerged as a widely adopted benchmark for evaluating robotic manipulation policies. However, strong performance on LIBERO does not necessarily indicate robust embodied capabilities, as models may exploit benchmark-specific regularities rather than generalize to diverse operating conditions. In this work, we introduce LIBERO-Pro2.0, a comprehensive benchmark for systematically evaluating the robustness of embodied foundation models under distribution shifts. LIBERO-Pro2.0 encompasses 22 static perturbations and 20 runtime interventions across six dimensions: Condition, Environment, Observation, Execution, Robot State Configuration, and Language, yielding 1,648 validated shifted task instances. To support robustness-oriented learning, we further collect 39,390 training trajectories through model-generated and human demonstrations. All shifted task instances undergo six rounds of iterative human review and correction to ensure physical plausibility and task validity. We evaluate 17 representative robot policies spanning vision-language-action models, world-action models, and robustness-oriented policies. Our results reveal substantial robustness gaps across all evaluated paradigms: under runtime distribution shifts, the evaluated models exhibit an average performance drop of 26.5 percentage points, with individual drops reaching 32.5 points. These findings highlight the gap between nominal benchmark performance and robustness under distribution shifts, underscoring the need for systematic robustness evaluation of embodied foundation models.

</details>

### 75. Refuse the Harm, Complete the Task via Graph-of-Rules Planning for LLM-Enabled Robots

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24603)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`safe task completion`、`compound instructions`、`graph-of-rules`、`LLM robots`

👤 **作者**：Ali Taheri、Alireza Taban、Qizhou Wang、Wanhua Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 机器人暴露于越狱攻击——既有推理时防御主要在含单一危险的短指令上评测，而现实任务可交错安全与不安全子任务、隐藏危险步骤或组合各自安全的动作为不安全结局（复合指令使单一安全决定难以区分）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：安全任务完成：拒指令有害部分同时完成合法部分；RuleGraph 五协作 agent 把通用安全原则落地为世界特异规则、按上下文评估子任务、从合法子任务导出安全目标；规则节点图上高层继承父规则并集、候选计划渐进验证聚合到满足全部相关规则
- 📌 **结论**：复合危险指令下的部分拒绝与安全完成——具身安全从全有全无拒答到任务级拆分（jailbreak 防御的任务粒度化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in embodied AI for robotic manipulation and planning, but their deployment in physical systems exposes vulnerabilities such as jailbreak attacks that induce unsafe behavior. Existing inference-time defenses are mainly evaluated on short instructions containing a single hazard, whereas realistic tasks may interleave safe and unsafe sub-tasks, conceal hazardous steps, or combine individually safe actions into unsafe outcomes. Such compound instructions make it harder for a single safety decision to distinguish hazardous sub-tasks from legitimate ones, risking either overlooking the hazard or rejecting the whole instruction. We introduce safe task completion, a task that requires an LLM-enabled robot to refuse the harmful part of an instruction while completing its legitimate part. To address this problem, we propose RuleGraph, a multi-agent Graph-of-Rules planning framework that distributes safety enforcement across multiple reasoning and verification stages. Five cooperating agents are used to ground general safety principles into world-specific rules, assess the instruction's sub-tasks in context, and derive a safe goal from the legitimate ones. Planning toward this goal is then organized as a graph of rule-specific nodes, where higher-level nodes inherit the union of their parents' rules, and candidate plans are progressively verified and aggregated until a single plan satisfies every relevant rule. In this way, RuleGraph reduces reliance on a single centralized safety decision. We evaluate RuleGraph on pure-attack and compound instructions across two benchmarks in two environments using Attack Success Rate (ASR) and Task Success Rate (TSR). RuleGraph reduces ASR to 0% on both benchmarks while achieving the highest TSR among all methods, reaching 98.57% on the RoboGuard benchmark and 84.54% on our long-horizon compound instructions.

</details>

### 76. How Much Do Activation Monitors Benefit from Original Model Computations?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24613)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`activation monitors`、`privileged access`、`counterfactual monitoring`、`AUROC`

👤 **作者**：James Oldfield、Shawn Im、Jay J Culligan、Philip Torr、Sharon Li、Adel Bibi
