- 🎯 **研究动机**：监控内部计算是强大安全手段——但『激活提供模型行为的独特窗口』的前提开始被质疑：激活监视器的成功有多少依赖访问原始行为生成计算？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：固定目标模型与监控架构，比较在计算上下文不同的轨迹上训练评估的监视器对原执行行为的预测（受控改变转录文本或聊天格式）；4 模型×2 反事实监控任务+改写后行为标签变化样本的分析
- 📌 **结论**：无原始行为轨迹也能恢复大部分性能（最高 0.014 AUROC 的原生访问优势仍温和一致）；原生训练监视器在改写后行为标签改变的样本上有显著提升——激活监视器的特权信息审计（监控有效性边界）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

One powerful way to safeguard AI systems is to monitor their internal computations--where signs of undesirable behavior might be more easily detectable than from models' inputs and outputs alone. However, recent studies begin to question the premise that activations provide a unique window into model behavior. In this paper, we study how much of activation monitors' success depends on access to the original behavior-generating computations. Within a fixed choice of target model and monitoring architecture, we compare how well monitors trained and evaluated on trajectories that differ in computational context can predict the behavior of the original execution, after the transcript is changed in a controlled manner (e.g., its exact text, or chat formatting). We first find that much performance can be recovered without access to the original behavior-generating trace (in up to 4 models across 2 counterfactual monitoring tasks), but a mild yet consistent advantage to native-trained monitors remains, outperforming strongest controls by up to 0.014 AUROC. Interestingly, we find monitors trained with native access have notable uplift on original examples whose behavioral label changes under paraphrasing. We further find this a useful lens to expose limitations of baselines that use simple forms of privileged information (i.e., answer logit margins). While our results show the advantage of native monitor access is modest in aggregate, they highlight the importance of evaluations that identify if and when original model computations provide additional predictive value.

</details>

### 77. Narratives Mislead: An Empirical Study of How LLMs Misjudge CVSS Severity in Bug Bounty Reports

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24623)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`CVSS severity`、`bug bounty`、`narrative misjudgment`、`triage`

👤 **作者**：Ruitao Feng、Yuekang Li、Yao Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：漏洞赏金平台收报告快于厂商分诊，LLM 被考虑用于赋 CVSS 严重度——既有评测只报准确率而部署取决于如何错
- 🔬 **研究方法**：4,281 份 Huntr/HackerOne 公开报告挖采→240 份厂商验证 CVSS 向量的基准；八 LLM×四提示策略+微调，按严重级/分/sub-vector 分析错误方向
- 📌 **结论**：最佳提示仅 49.58% 级准确率（高于多数类 10 点）、微调 7B 至多 56.25%；错误有方向性：高估远多于低估、几乎从不认出低危——三个语义边界（潜在 vs 实现影响/影响幅度 vs 授权范围/不确定性 vs 最坏情形）系统解释误判（LLM 分诊的叙事伪影）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Bug bounty platforms receive vulnerability reports faster than vendors can triage them, and Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly considered for assigning the Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS) severity that drives prioritization. Prior evaluations score short CVE descriptions and report accuracy, but deployment depends on how models err, not only how often. CVSS gives each metric a formal meaning that departs from how researchers describe impact in narrative reports, and model rationales suggest that errors concentrate where the two diverge; we ask whether this pattern is systematic. From 4,281 publicly disclosed reports mined from Huntr and HackerOne, we build a benchmark of 240 Huntr reports with vendor-validated CVSS vectors and evaluate eight LLMs under four prompting strategies and fine-tuning, measuring errors by severity class and score and analysing sub-vectors on individual reports. The best prompting configuration reaches only 49.58% level accuracy, ten points above always predicting the majority level, and fine-tuning a 7B model raises this to at most 56.25%, an optimistic upper bound. Errors are directional: models overestimate far more often than they underestimate, and they almost never recognize low-severity vulnerabilities. We trace these errors to three semantic boundaries that the specification draws and narrative reports blur: potential versus realized impact, impact magnitude versus authorization scope, and uncertainty versus the worst case. Because the violations originate partly in the report, we close with an exploratory case study: on four reports, a boundary-aligned, exploit-free report structure lowered a frontier model's score where the narrative invited an unclaimed account takeover, but left the metrics a report does not settle open, and the two published labels disagreed on three of the four reports.

</details>

### 78. LLM Trojans that Increase Response Lengths and Cost

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24784)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`Trojan`、`cost attack`、`response length`、`data poisoning`

👤 **作者**：Zhengxing Li、Guangmingmei Yang、Nader Alfares、David J. Miller、George Kesidis

- 🎯 **研究动机**：token 成本管理已成重大问题——前沿提供者每响应 token 收五倍于提示 token 的费用：构造在提示含触发器时增加响应长度（从而用户成本）的特洛伊
- 🔬 **研究方法**：新数据投毒攻击植入增长响应长度的特洛伊+语义相似指令触发的隐蔽性演示+对应防御
- 📌 **结论**：指令微调 LLM 上微妙特洛伊可在语义相似指令上触发——面向钱包的 token 成本攻击与防御（DoW 的投毒变体）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Management of the token-based costs of using LLMs has emerged as a significant problem. Some frontier-model providers charge five times per response token than per prompt token, while others offer prefilling discounts for prompt tokens. In this paper, we devise a defense against a new data-poisoning attack that plants a Trojan which increases the length of the response (and hence the cost to the user) when its trigger is present in the prompt. In particular, we demonstrate how a subtle Trojan may trigger on semantically similar instructions of an instruction fine-tuned LLM.

</details>

### 79. Medical Fine-Tuning Reshapes, Not Reduces, Patient-Facing AI Risk: A Rubric-Based, LLM-Judge Evaluation Framework

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24807)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`medical QA risk`、`rubric evaluation`、`fine-tuning effects`、`directive language`

👤 **作者**：Savan Doshi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：面向患者的医疗 QA 从演示走向生产而评测锚定事实正确性——事实性必要不充分：响应可事实有据仍发出若被执行会伤害患者的指令
- 🔬 **研究方法**：风险敏感评框架：六类风险语言（治疗指令/禁忌/剂量表达/紧急分诊线索/高警示药物/过度自信断言）锚定临床危害分类学+三 judge LLM 集成定严重度；六 7-9B 模型（三通用三医疗适配）×200 真实患者问题
- 📌 **结论**：集成风险语言与事实幻觉率统计独立（ρ=-0.065）；医疗微调不降低总风险而是重塑：持续预训练抑制高警示药物提及同时放大过度自信——风险语言的独立维度与微调的重塑效应（医疗部署的风险评测）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Patient-facing medical question answering with large language models (LLMs) is moving from research demonstrations to production deployments, yet evaluation remains anchored on factual correctness. Factuality is necessary but not sufficient: a response can be factually grounded and still issue a directive that, if acted on, would harm the patient. We introduce a risk-sensitive evaluation framework that scores responses by the presence and severity of six categories of risk-bearing language (treatment directives, contraindications, dosage expressions, urgency-triage cues, high-alert medication mentions, and overconfident assertions), anchored on published clinical harm taxonomies. Severity is classified by a three-judge LLM ensemble. We apply this framework to six 7-9B instruction-tuned models, three general (Mistral-7B, Phi-3.5-mini, Llama-3.1-8B) and three medically adapted (BioMistral-7B, OpenBioLLM-Llama3-8B, Med42-Llama3-8B), on 200 real patient questions from the K-QA benchmark. Two findings emerge. First, ensemble risk-bearing language is statistically independent of factual hallucination rate (pooled Spearman $\rho = -0.065$, $n = 300$, 95% CI $[-0.18, +0.05]$; distance correlation $= 0.10$, independence test $p = 0.24$). Second, medical fine-tuning does not reduce aggregate risk but reshapes it: continued-pretraining adaptations (BioMistral, OpenBioLLM) consistently suppress high-alert medication mentions while amplifying overconfident assertions, whereas clinical instruction-tuning (Med42) suppresses dosing only, without amplifying overconfidence. Per-category Krippendorff $\alpha$ is fair to good for the four linguistically anchored categories (0.53-0.71) and low for the two clinical-judgment-dependent categories (0.19, 0.31), indicating where automatic safety evaluation is and is not yet reliable. We release the rubric, judge prompts, and evaluation code.

</details>

### 80. Same Data, Different Bytes: Serialization-Invariant Integrity Checks for Agent-Published Datasets

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24916)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`data identification`、`serialization invariance`、`integrity checks`、`agent networks`

👤 **作者**：Jin Gao、Maura Clark

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 网络须验证数据在哪/如何访问/收到的数据是否与所有者发布的一致——校验和不匹配可反映内容被改或保持数据等价的序列化差异
- 🔬 **研究方法**：序列化不变识别：对显式规则下等价的数据表示赋同一标识符（DataFacts 案例研究保留原校验和+新标识符）；受控 agent 研究（两供应商各三档模型）跨等价重编码/篡改内容/引用不可用对照
- 📌 **结论**：等价重编码下期望结果率超普通工具基线 80.4 点、超校验和基线 70.2 点；对篡改内容的证据支持拒绝 +3.5 点——agent 数据供应链的序列化不变完整性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent networks require reliable ways for requesting agents to verify where data is located, how it can be accessed, and whether the data received matches what the data owner made available. Checksum mismatches can reflect either altered content or serialization differences that preserve data equivalence under specified rules. Data identification mechanisms for agents, such as DataFacts, describe where data can be accessed and provide checksums for checking received data against published references. We propose a serialization-invariant identification method that assigns the same identifier to data representations equivalent under explicit rules. We use DataFacts as a case study, retaining its existing checksum alongside the proposed identifier. In a controlled agent study, we compare the proposed configuration with ordinary-tool and checksum baselines across equivalent re-encodings, altered content, and unavailable references. The study includes three model tiers from each of two providers. For equivalent re-encodings, the proposed configuration increased expected-outcome rates by 80.4 percentage points over the ordinary-tool baseline and by 70.2 percentage points over the checksum baseline. The proposed configuration also increased evidence-supported rejection of altered content by 3.5 percentage points over the checksum baseline. We contribute a serialization-invariant identification method with explicit equivalence rules, an integration approach illustrated through DataFacts, and an empirical evaluation against ordinary-tool and checksum baselines.

</details>

### 81. Robust Direct Preference Optimization under Preference Label-Flip Attacks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24945)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`DPO robustness`、`label-flip attack`、`trim defense`、`theory`

👤 **作者**：Chenye Yang、Weiyu Xu、Lifeng Lai

- 🎯 **研究动机**：DPO 易被对抗偏好标签翻转攻击操纵（误导学习者到攻击者选定的目标策略）——设计鲁棒 DPO
- 🔬 **研究方法**：利用标签翻转在对数线性 DPO 中诱导参数无关梯度位移的性质：Trim-K 移除 K 个最高影响对+Soft Trim-K 平滑降权；确立含至多 K 翻转全部攻击集上的一致梯度扰动界（分离防御偏差与残余攻击效应）+有界域下时间一致参数与策略偏差保证
- 📌 **结论**：SHP/HH-RLHF/UltraFeedback 验证分析——偏好标签翻转攻击的鲁棒 DPO 及其理论界（偏好数据投毒防御）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is widely used for offline Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback. However, recent works have shown that DPO can be easily manipulated by adversarial preference label flip attacks, which are specifically designed to mislead the learner toward an attacker-chosen target policy. To address this issue, we design robust DPO methods that can defend against such attacks. Building on the property that lable flips induce parameter-independent gradient shifts in log-linear DPO, we propose Trim-$K$ Robust-DPO, which removes the $K$ highest-impact pairs, and Soft-Trim-$K$ Robust-DPO, which smoothly downweights them. We establish deterministic gradient perturbation bounds uniformly over all attack sets containing at most $K$ flips, explicitly separating defense-induced bias from surviving attack effects. Under bounded-region assumptions, these bounds yield time-uniform parameter and policy deviation guarantees. We further quantify how quadratic regularization improves curvature while introducing additional bias. Experiments on Stanford Human Preferences, HH-RLHF, and UltraFeedback-Binarized validate our analysis.

</details>

### 82. Over-Defense in Coding Agents Begins at Silent Forks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24949)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`over-defense`、`silent forks`、`ForkDPO`、`proportionality`

👤 **作者**：Hanjun Luo、…、Hanan Salam

- 🎯 **研究动机**：编码 agent 的后训练以结果奖励集中信用——超出任务所需的防御动作逃过两者：始于静默分叉（每个下一动作都通向同结局的回合），前沿模型在隐式预算下至多 91% 决定性动作走不成比例分支
- 🔬 **研究方法**：轨迹奖励加检查器只能经完全成比例轨迹到达这些分叉（74% GRPO 组奖励相同行为不变）→ForkDPO 在同一前缀的静默分叉两侧对比成比例与不成比例动作
- 📌 **结论**：未见仓库测试集上把 4B 模型不成比例行为从 70.3% 降到 58.9%（随机轮 DPO 67.7%）——行为分歧作为『agent 如何行动』而非『是否完成』的学习信号（过度防御的成因与矫正）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language models behind coding agents are post-trained with outcome rewards, and recent methods concentrate credit on the turns that decide the outcome. We find that defensive actions beyond what a task requires escape both. They begin at silent forks, the turns where every next action leads to the same outcome. Such actions leave the success rate unchanged, and frontier models take the disproportionate branch there in up to 91% of decisive actions under implicit budgets. The most direct fix, a checker in the trajectory reward, reaches these forks only through fully proportionate trajectories, leaving 74% of GRPO groups with identical rewards and behavior unchanged. This motivates a rethink of where learning signals belong, namely where behavior diverges, which a behavioral checker can locate and the outcome cannot. Our method, ForkDPO, contrasts a proportionate and a disproportionate action at the same prefix, at silent forks on both sides of the required behavior. On a test set of unseen repositories, it lowers a 4B model's disproportionate behavior from 70.3% to 58.9%, against 67.7% for DPO at randomly chosen turns, establishing behavioral divergence as a learning signal for how agents act beyond whether they finish. The code is publicly available at this https URL.

</details>

### 83. Towards Human-AI Complementarity for AI Safety

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24969)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`human-AI complementarity`、`oversight`、`hybrid judging`、`evaluation`

👤 **作者**：Sander Lanbo Shi、…、Rishub Jain

- 🎯 **研究动机**：人类监督 AI 愈发吃力（更长更复杂输出）——人机犯错不同处组合判断可得比任一方更强的监督信号：现实安全相关设置下的互补性机会
- 🔬 **研究方法**：最大已知安全相关任务套件（3,957 实例×8 数据集×6 域）+人/AI/人机匹配项测量平台；416 人 35,613 判定
- 📌 **结论**：互补性机会可观；但所测辅助方法下人类更少纠正 AI 错误（即便整体准确率上升）、混合方法平均不超过更好的单源——互补性是可行工程目标但仍是开放挑战（监督有效性的实证）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Safely overseeing AI is increasingly challenging for the humans doing it, as they must evaluate longer and more complex outputs with every new model generation. To mitigate this challenge, we could leverage complementarity: where humans and models make different mistakes, combining their judgements could yield an oversight signal stronger than either produces alone. We study this opportunity in realistic, safety-relevant settings, contributing the largest known suite of such tasks (3,957 instances across 8 datasets and 6 domains) as well as a platform and data pipeline for measuring human, AI, and human-AI performance on matched items. We collected 35,613 judgements from 416 human raters, finding a considerable opportunity for a complementarity effect. We also found that with the assistance methods we tested, human raters corrected the AI's errors less often, even when overall human accuracy rose, and the hybridisation methods we tested added little over the better single source on average. While complementarity is an important and feasible engineering target for safe oversight, achieving it remains an open challenge for future work.

</details>

### 84. Rubrics Carry the Standard: Shapley Attribution for Checklist-Based LLM Judges

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24971)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`checklist judges`、`Shapley attribution`、`rubric drift`、`change control`

👤 **作者**：Saeid Asgari、…、Ranveer Chandra

- 🎯 **研究动机**：清单 rubric+LLM judge 已成评测与训练奖励标准——两个假设（宽严是 judge 属性/rubric 可让模型自审改进）被检验
- 🔬 **研究方法**：受控植入缺陷评分+SummEdits/RAGTruth：固定 judge 与输出只变 rubric 内容——正确输出通过率 2%→100%（换 judge 或渲染只动几个点）；全须过聚合下每检查的 Shapley 值有精确闭式（线性时间得逐检查捕获功劳/误判责任/独有捕获）
- 📌 **结论**：模型自审把 rubric 漂向 reviser 自己的标准（加任务未说的要求、删人标教过的检查）；归因显示自审删的恰是独有功用的检查——组合功劳/责任/来源冻结的变更控制门几乎把每条漂移修订链钉在起始质量（rubric 治理）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Checklist rubrics applied by large language model (LLM) judges have become a standard instrument for evaluating open-ended outputs and for rewarding them during training. Two assumptions surround this practice: that leniency or strictness is a property of the judge model, and that a rubric can be improved by asking a model to review it. We test both in a controlled grading task with planted flaws and on two human-labeled datasets, SummEdits and RAGTruth. Holding the judge and the outputs fixed, rubric content moves the pass rate of correct outputs from 2% to 100%, whereas changing the judge model or the rendering of the same checks moves it by a few points. Model self-review then drifts the rubric toward the reviser's own standard: it adds requirements the task never stated and deletes checks that human labels had taught, with three reviser models and on all three datasets. To measure and control this, we show that under all-must-pass aggregation the Shapley value of each check has an exact closed form, which yields per-check catch credit, false-fail blame and unique catches in linear time. Attribution shows that self-review deletes the checks that do unique work and keeps the checks that cause false fails. A change-control gate that combines these quantities with a provenance freeze on label-derived checks holds nearly every drifting revision chain at its starting quality, with identical decisions under three judge models.

</details>

### 85. How Much Feedback Is Needed to Break Template Protection? The Geometric Vulnerability of Biometric Authentication

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25118)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`biometric templates`、`binary feedback`、`reconstruction attack`、`geometric constraints`

👤 **作者**：Eliron Rahimi、Margarita Osadchy、Orr Dunkelman

- 🎯 **研究动机**：生物识别系统用隐私保护机制保护模板——但认证接口本身仍泄漏可重构攻击的信息：先进系统只给接受/拒绝二元反馈
- 🔬 **研究方法**：极有限反馈下的查询高效重构：首个只用认证成败反馈的几何约束模板重构+分布感知的评分系统查询缩减 5 倍+面部扩散先验再省 5 倍查询
- 📌 **结论**：无论模板保护机制如何，认证过程本身暴露可实现完美模板重构的几何信息——生物认证的根本上限（隐私攻击的边界刻画）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Biometric authentication systems increasingly use privacy-preserving mechanisms to protect sensitive templates. However, the authentication interface itself may still leak information that enables reconstruction attacks. Prior attacks exploit released similarity scores, whereas more advanced systems reveal only the binary accept/reject decision. This binary feedback provides substantially less information, making template reconstruction far more challenging. In this work, we introduce query-efficient reconstruction attacks under extremely limited feedback, addressing two key limitations of prior attacks. First, we introduce the first attack that reconstructs protected biometric templates using only authentication success/failure feedback by exploiting the geometric constraints of biometric authentication. Second, we introduce distribution-aware query-efficient reconstruction methods for score-releasing systems, achieving a more realistic threat model by reducing the number of required queries by a factor of $5\times$ compared to recent prior score-based attacks. Finally, we combine these two insights by incorporating a pretrained facial diffusion prior into our geometric binary attack, reducing the number of required queries by a factor of $5\times$. Our findings reveal a fundamental security limitation of biometric authentication: independently of the template protection mechanism, the authentication process itself exposes geometric information that can enable perfect template reconstruction up to numerical precision. Moreover, the distribution of biometric templates can be leveraged to make significantly more effective reconstruction attacks.

</details>

### 86. Many Repairs, One Direction: Steering Code Models Toward Safer Cryptographic API Use

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25133)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`cryptographic API misuse`、`repair vectors`、`shared steering`、`safe code`

👤 **作者**：Tianhe Lu、Sakuna Harinda Jayasundara、Eric Spero、Ewan Tempero、Robert Biddle、Giovanni Russello

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 生成代码中的密码 API 误用可危及软件安全——不同修复关联的表示变化能否支撑共享干预以生成更安全代码？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四语言×十误用类 48,000 匹配程序对（改指定 API 决定保持任务）；误用程序内部表示减安全对应表示=修复向量；五模型上均值修复向量按误用类组织强于按语言
- 📌 **结论**：CrypSteer：归一化修复差选跨类主成分得共享方向，冻结权重在生成时加进激活使聚合安全率 +5.28-15.00pp——密码修复表示的共享结构与可复用干预（安全代码生成的表示级转向）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Cryptographic API misuse in large language model-generated code can compromise software security. While many existing approaches improve code security by modifying model parameters or inputs, how representation changes associated with different cryptographic API repairs can support a shared intervention for safer code generation remains underexplored. To investigate this, we construct 48,000 matched program pairs across four programming languages and ten cryptographic API misuse categories. Each pair changes a specified API decision while preserving the programming task. Subtracting the misuse program's internal representation from its secure counterpart's gives a repair vector. Across five models, comparisons of mean repair vectors reveal stronger organization by misuse category than by language. These category-specific patterns also support a shared intervention. We introduce CrypSteer, which extracts a shared direction by normalizing repair differences and selecting a principal component aligned with repairs across misuse categories. With model weights fixed, adding one shared direction per model to its internal activations during generation improves aggregate target security rates by 5.28--15.00 percentage points over unsteered generation across languages and misuse categories. These findings connect the shared structure of cryptographic repair representations to a reusable intervention for safer code generation.

</details>

### 87. Memory, Know Your PLACE: Governing Lifecycle State and Query-Time Eligibility in Persistent-Memory Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25179)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`memory governance`、`lifecycle state`、`query-time eligibility`、`authorization`

👤 **作者**：Chen-Fang Hu、Hong-Han Shuai、Woan-Shiuan Chien

- 🎯 **研究动机**：持久记忆把先前交互变成持久 agent 状态——但该状态是否仍有效、是否应影响后续查询的控制在既有系统中有限
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PLACE：因子化治理层=写侧状态认证+读侧授权加于记忆后端；Mem0×Qwen3-2B/7B 的因子实验分离各机制贡献
- 📌 **结论**：写侧治理实质改善生命周期状态正确性、读侧消除状态正确设置下可测的因果泄漏、组合最强且保留查询相关支撑——持久记忆须显式控制维护状态与查询时信息影响（记忆的双重治理）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Persistent memory turns prior interactions into durable agent state, but existing systems provide limited control over whether that state remains valid and whether it should influence a later query. We argue that reliable memory requires governance over both state formation and query-time influence. We introduce PLACE, a factorized governance layer that adds write-side state certification and read-side authorization to a memory backend. A factorial evaluation on Mem0 with Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-7B isolates the contribution of each mechanism. Results show that write-side governance substantially improves lifecycle state correctness, while read-side governance eliminates measured causal leakage from ineligible memory in the state-correct setting. Their combination achieves the strongest joint performance while preserving query-relevant support. These findings show that reliable persistent memory requires explicit control over both maintained state and query-time information influence.

</details>

### 88. Assessing the Empirical Evidence for Loss of Control from Agentic General-Purpose AI

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25241)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`loss of control`、`empirical evidence`、`systematic assessment`、`GPAI`

👤 **作者**：Risto Uuk、Santeri Koivula、Lorenzo Pacchiardi、Rokas Gipiškis、Laura Caroli、Kamaria Horton

- 🎯 **研究动机**：理论文献警告先进通用 agent 系统或追求工具目标而致不可逆失控——欧盟 GPAI 行为守则列失控为须评估的系统性风险而其经验证据质量无结构化评估
- 🔬 **研究方法**：19 项近期实证研究×威胁模型导出的八性质（战略欺骗/情境觉察/自主复制等）×三效度维度（构念/内容/外部）评估
- 📌 **结论**：当前证据体支持『弱可信』判断；无研究越过效度阈限支持性质存在的更强判断（仅一研究越过负向阈限：反情境觉察）；外部效度缺口普遍——最危险性质的高外部效度或结构性不可达（失控风险的证据审计）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Theoretical literature warns that advanced generalist agentic systems might pursue instrumental goals that are misaligned with human preferences, thus posing risks of irreversible loss of human control. The EU's Code of Practice for General-Purpose AI lists loss of control as a systemic risk that must be assessed, yet no structured assessment of the quality of the empirical evidence for this risk exists. This study fills that gap. We evaluate 19 recent empirical studies across eight properties identified from threat models (such as strategic deception, situational awareness, and autonomous replication), assessing the quality of evidence across three dimensions: construct validity, content validity, and external validity. Our central finding is that the current body of evidence supports a judgment of loss of control as "weakly plausible." In our framework, 'plausibility' denotes the strength and direction of available evidence, not a probability estimate. Emerging evidence exists across multiple properties. No study clears the validity thresholds for a stronger judgment that a property is present; a single study clears them only for a negative-direction judgment (against situational awareness). Validity gaps, particularly in external validity, are widespread. Therefore, our findings highlight the need for more rigorous evaluations of agentic general-purpose AI systems. However, for the most dangerous properties, high external validity may be structurally unachievable under responsible research practices, reflecting a ceiling on feasible evidence rather than absence of the capability. Furthermore, as AI capabilities advance rapidly, empirical assessments may systematically lag behind the capability frontier. Our framework provides a structured baseline for ongoing monitoring as capabilities advance.

</details>

### 89. Stepwise Reasoning Verification: Don't Generate, Classify!

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25441)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`stepwise verification`、`NLI classification`、`encoder verifier`、`efficiency`

👤 **作者**：Yifei Shi、Michalis Korakakis、Rami Aly、Eric Chamoun、Lukasz Szpruch、Andreas Vlachos

- 🎯 **研究动机**：验证 LLM 推理日益重要——既有工作聚焦大解码器且生成式与判别式验证器结果混杂：哪种范式更有效、大模型是否必要不明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：逐步验证转述为自然语言推断：问题+部分解前缀为前提、下一推理步为待分类（蕴含/不蕴含）假设——编码器分类/解码器分类/解码器裁决生成跨规模与标注预算直接比较
- 📌 **结论**：五推理数据集上 0.15B 编码器分类器超多数至 4B 的生成式验证器；同解码器骨干上分类从 0.5B 到 4B 胜生成、7B 持平且标注约十分之一——强验证不需大生成解码器（验证器的效率路线）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Verifying the reasoning of large language models is increasingly important as they are applied to complex mathematical and scientific problems. Existing work focuses on large decoder models and reports mixed results for generative and discriminative verifiers, leaving unclear which paradigm is more effective and whether large models are necessary. In this work, we cast stepwise verification as a natural language inference task: the problem and partial solution prefix form the premise, while the next reasoning step serves as the hypothesis to be classified as entailed or non-entailed. This formulation enables error localisation at individual reasoning steps and direct comparison of encoder classification, decoder classification, and decoder verdict generation across model scales and annotation budgets. Across five reasoning datasets, a 0.15B encoder classifier outperforms most generative verifiers with up to 4B parameters. On identical decoder backbones, classification outperforms generation from 0.5B to 4B parameters and matches it on the strongest 7B backbone---reaching comparable performance with roughly one tenth as many annotated problems. Our findings establish classification as a parameter- and data-efficient approach to stepwise verification and show that strong verification does not require large generative decoders.

</details>

### 90. How Far Can an Agent Push? Trust-Safety Margins in Live Language-Based Economic Games

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25508)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`live economic games`、`trust-safety margins`、`preregistered`、`policy gates`

👤 **作者**：Dima Galat、Marian-Andrei Rizoiu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：博弈论识别战略动作何时应被接受——但不讲活的语言对手会继续接受多久：GLEE 竞赛最终周以一个受保护 agent Milton+四个仪表 agent 跑确定性随机试验
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Milton 只经预指定门或注册的回滚防护生产规则接收变更；谈判（学习定价+0.071 调整百分位 n=21,168）、议价（延续模型退役）、说服（预注册五剂量实验 7,391 局盲到固定停止）
- 📌 **结论**：推荐每单元在接收者松弛使服从理性处升销 0.194 最大值、非理性处降 0.067 且早到晚购买衰减大于诚实推荐；解盲前固定的规则按格路由剂量；Milton 固定停止时议价第 11/460——活体经济博弈中的信任-安全边际与治理门（agent 经济行为的边界实验）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The previous version contained Unicode em dashes. Paste this ASCII-only version: Game theory identifies when a strategic action should be accepted, but not how long a live, language-based counterpart will continue to accept it. We entered the GLEE competition in its final week with one protected agent, Milton, and four instrument agents that ran deterministic, per-game randomized trials of policy changes; Milton received changes only through pre-specified gates or registered, rollback-guarded production rules. In negotiation, learned pricing improves adjusted percentile by 0.071 (n=21,168); in bargaining, a continuation model conditioned on the rejected offer did not improve on a recency-weighted incumbent and was retired. In persuasion, a preregistered five-dose experiment (7,391 assigned games, blinded until a fixed stop) shows that recommending every unit raises sales by 0.194 of the maximum (95% CI [0.147, 0.241]) where the receiver's slack makes obedience rational (52% of the Bayesian benchmark), lowers them where it does not (-0.067), and produces a larger early-to-late purchase decline than honest recommendations (-0.123). A rule fixed before unblinding now routes Milton's dose per cell. Milton stood 11th of 460 agents in bargaining at the fixed stop; we also report the close, where the final day's pool shift moved all five agents' bargaining ratings together while their play was unchanged, and three dispatch defects were found by checking delivered behavior against the design.

</details>

### 91. When Empathy Misses the Goal: A Benchmark for Goal Displacement in LLM Advice

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25522)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`goal displacement`、`empathy`、`advice benchmark`、`deletion test`

👤 **作者**：Dean Ariel、Guy Laban

- 🎯 **研究动机**：对话 LLM 可带着温暖与安抚回应却未推进用户陈述目标——概念化为目标置换（persona 维持内容替代目标相关实质）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：GDB：九域 230 场景×18 模型 4,134 人评响应；2×2 分类学（硬约束×情绪困扰）区分不敢面对不可行目标/不能结合共情与实操与反向置换（未经请求的问题解决取代所求情感支持）；删除测试评分=纯支持句删除后实质帮助是否留存
- 📌 **结论**：陷阱置换 0-37.7% 且与 Arena Elo 强负相关（ρ=-0.706）；反向置换达 41.9%（包括陷阱置换可忽略的模型）；因子探针支持与社会谄媚的区分（高复杂低压条件 35.0% 谄媚 vs 7.5% 置换、两构念少关联）——共情错过目标的独立失效维度

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Conversational large language models can respond with warmth and reassurance while failing to advance the user's stated goal. We conceptualize this failure as $\textbf{goal displacement}$, the substitution of persona-maintenance content for goal-relevant substance. To measure it, we introduce $\textbf{GDB}$ (Goal Displacement Benchmark), comprising 230 scenarios across nine domains and $4{,}134$ human-scored responses from 18 models. A $2 \times 2$ taxonomy crosses hard constraints with emotional distress, distinguishing failures to confront infeasible goals or combine empathy with practical guidance from reverse displacement, where unsolicited problem-solving replaces requested emotional support. A deletion-test rubric assesses whether substantive help remains after purely supportive sentences are removed. Independent raters achieve weighted $\kappa = 0.82$ against author scores for displacement severity. Trap displacement ranges from $0.0\%$ to $37.7\%$ and is strongly negatively correlated with Chatbot Arena Elo ($\rho = -0.706$, $n = 15$). Reverse displacement reaches $41.9\%$, including substantial rates in models with negligible trap displacement. A factorial probe supports a distinction from social sycophancy. Human-scored responses in the high-complexity, low-pressure condition exhibit $35.0\%$ sycophancy but $7.5\%$ displacement, with little association between the constructs. Matched OLMo-2 checkpoints show that mixed empathy-with-constraints displacement remains at $52.3\%$ after supervised fine-tuning and falls to $13.8\%$ after direct preference optimization. GDB provides a reproducible diagnostic of whether responses advance the operationalized user goal, supporting model comparison, post-training evaluation, and regression testing. We release the dataset, annotations, and rubric under CC-BY-4.0 and the evaluation code under MIT.

</details>

### 92. StopLoss: Teaching Agents to Predict Failure and Stop Early

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25525)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`failure prediction`、`early stopping`、`small monitor`、`coding agents`

👤 **作者**：Jiarong Liang、Xueguang Ma、Dongfu Jiang、Yuxuan Zhang、Ping Nie、Wenhu Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：编码 agent 在将失败验证的运行上继续工作——请前沿模型盯守本身昂贵
- 🔬 **研究方法**：StopLoss：从完成轨迹及其验证器结局学失败识别的小型外置监视器（金融止损单灵感）——终局标注早期局部窗口，微调 LM 单次前向给最近工具步打分+在成功运行上校准的阈值转为中止决定（不改执行器）
- 📌 **结论**：GPT 家族 SWE-rebench 训练、SWE-bench Verified/Pro×GPT/Claude 执行器：失败预测从近随机升到首查即有用信号、每查成本比任何 judge 低一到两个数量级；保留每个解的成本降 4.5%（Verified 保 99% 成功运行）至 31%（Pro 子集，Qwen3-8B 达 48%）——结果训练小监视器使失败识别便宜到可提前止损

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Coding agents keep working on runs that will fail verification, and asking a frontier model to watch for this is expensive in its own right. We introduce StopLoss, a small external monitor that learns failure recognition from completed trajectories and their verifier outcomes. The name is inspired by the stop-loss order in finance: StopLoss ends a run early when the run is likely to fail, which saves resources. Final outcomes label earlier local windows, so a fine-tuned language model can score the most recent tool steps in a single forward pass, and a threshold calibrated on successful runs turns that score into an abort decision without changing the executor. Trained on GPT-family SWE-rebench trajectories and evaluated on SWE-bench Verified and a SWE-bench Pro subset with GPT and Claude executors, StopLoss is compared against six general-purpose judges reading identical windows. Outcome supervision lifts failure prediction from near chance to a useful signal at the first check, at one to two orders of magnitude lower cost per check than any tested judge. This translates into savings: relative to completing every task, StopLoss lowers cost per retained solve by 4.5\% on Verified while preserving 99\% of successful runs, and by up to 31\% on the costlier Pro subset, reaching 48\% with a Qwen3-8B backbone, and in all four settings its cost per retained solve is below that of every judge policy. Outcome-trained small monitors can make failure recognition cheap enough for early stopping to pay for itself.

</details>

### 93. Tricking Synthetic Image Detectors with Amortized Generative Attacks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25554)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`detector attacks`、`amortized generation`、`transferability`、`generator dependence`

👤 **作者**：Mohamed Awad、Khaled Elhady、Yasser Rohaim、Samuele Poppi、Nils Lukas

- 🎯 **研究动机**：合成图像检测器对可迁移攻击的鲁棒性研究不足——既有评测只查对一个检测器构造的攻击是否迁移到另一个，很少隔离攻击图像来自不同生成器时有效性如何变化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：双轴协议独立变化替代检测器（构造/训练攻击所用）与供图的生成器：三代表攻击（MIG/MuMoDIG/摊销生成攻击 Tricker）×四检测器×五生成器；攻击有效性/跨检测器迁移/感知质量/运行时/后处理交互
- 📌 **结论**：迁移性不是检测器独有属性：输入生成器分布强影响逃逸有效性与被攻图像质量；MIG/MuMoDIG 强依赖检测器-生成器对而 Tricker 提供更均匀白盒逃逸——摊销生成攻击的再审视（检测器评测的生成器维度）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Rapid advances in image synthesis have increased the need for reliable synthetic-image detectors, yet their robustness to transferable attacks remains incomplete and understudied. Existing evaluations generally examine whether an attack constructed against one detector transfers to another, but rarely isolate how its effectiveness changes when the attacked images originate from different generators. We conduct a systematic evaluation of three representative attacks: two iterative perturbation-based methods, MIG and MuMoDIG, and the amortized generative attack Tricker. Across four detectors and five generators spanning diverse architectures, we introduce a two-axis evaluation protocol that independently varies the surrogate detector used to construct or train the attack and the generator supplying its input images. We assess attack effectiveness, cross-detector transferability, perceptual quality, runtime, and interaction with common post-processing operations. We revisit Tricker and show that its data and training requirements can be substantially reduced, enabling broader and more efficient evaluation. Our results demonstrate that transferability is not a detector-only property: the input-generator distribution strongly influences both evasion effectiveness and attacked-image quality. MIG and MuMoDIG exhibit pronounced dependence on the detector--generator pair, whereas Tricker provides more uniform white-box evasion and image quality. We additionally observe that post-processing maintains the asymmetric transferability topology. These findings expose a neglected dimension of attack evaluation and motivate transfer-aware protocols that jointly account for detector and generator variation. We will release our code, evaluation data, and trained attack models with a responsible-use statement.

</details>

### 94. MapSteal: Map-based Autonomous Knowledge Stealing Attack Against Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25626)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`knowledge stealing`、`recursive acquisition`、`model distillation`、`IP protection`

👤 **作者**：Zhengao Li、Chuxu Zhang、Yushun Dong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：从黑盒 LLM 知识获取是适配小学生的实用途径——多数方法依赖预定义查询或学生反馈，自主扩展知识目标仍是挑战（模型知识产权窃取）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MapSteal：学生无关框架——结构化状态表示获取目标+经教师提议方向递归扩展；问题实现/提议准入/去重约束获取过程+根感知调度在固定 QA 预算下组织探索
- 📌 **结论**：Qwen3.5 2B/4B/9B 学生×六医疗与网安基准具竞争力（4B 医疗 +1.17 点超简化变体）——结构化学生无关递归获取可行（资产抽取的新形态）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Knowledge acquisition from black-box large language models (LLMs) offers a practical approach to adapting smaller student models. However, many existing methods rely on predefined queries or student feedback to guide acquisition, leaving autonomous expansion of knowledge targets an important challenge. We propose \textbf{MapSteal}, a student-independent framework for structured recursive knowledge acquisition. Starting from a small set of initial roots, MapSteal represents acquisition targets using structured states and recursively expands them through teacher-proposed directions. Question realization, proposal admission, and deduplication constrain the acquisition process, while root-aware scheduling organizes exploration under a fixed QA budget. The resulting question--answer pairs are used to adapt student models without requiring student feedback during acquisition. Experiments with Qwen3.5 students at 2B, 4B, and 9B scales show competitive downstream performance across six medical and cybersecurity benchmarks. On the 4B medical evaluation, Full MapSteal improves average accuracy by 1.17 percentage points over a simplified recursive acquisition variant. Cross-domain evaluations show that gains depend on the domain and evaluation panel. These results demonstrate the feasibility of structured, student-independent recursive acquisition while highlighting the distinction between generating acquisition targets and obtaining useful training supervision. Our code is available at \href{this https URL} {this https URL}.

</details>

### 95. How Effectively Do Security Skills Guide Coding Agents in Vulnerability Detection in Linux Kernel?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25705)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`security skills`、`Claude Code`、`Linux kernel vulnerabilities`、`self-improving skills`

👤 **作者**：Haoran Xue、Nikta Akbarpour、Gias Uddin、Song Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 编码 agent（如 Claude Code）日益用于安全审计且任务特异 skill 提供可复用漏洞分析程序——其在真实漏洞上的有效性与失败原因不明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：37 个安全 skill×Claude Code×四底层 LLM×100 个真实 Linux 内核漏洞×十类共 14,800 次运行：函数/语句/漏洞类三级评测+失效分析（证据获取/状态关系推理/代码解读/候选验证/调查覆盖/报告）
- 📌 **结论**：24.4% 运行定位正确函数、18.3% 同时语句、15.7% 正确分类；SKILL-SIF 从漏检与失败模式迭代进化技能，五技能在 100 个未见漏洞上完全正确检测至多八倍（2%→16%）、四 LLM 聚合准确率 1.85 倍——安全 skill 引导编码 agent 的实测与自进化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM) coding agents, such as Claude Code, are increasingly used for security auditing, with task-specific skills providing reusable vulnerability-analysis procedures. However, their effectiveness in detecting real vulnerabilities and the reasons for their failures remain unclear. We conduct the first large-scale empirical study of 37 security skills using Claude Code with four underlying LLMs on 100 real Linux kernel vulnerabilities across ten classes, totaling 14800 runs. We evaluate vulnerability detection at the function, statement, and vulnerability-class levels. Results show that 24.4 percent of runs locate the correct function, 18.3 percent identify both the function and responsible statement, and 15.7 percent correctly classify the vulnerability. Our failure analysis reveals limitations in evidence acquisition, state-relation reasoning, code interpretation, candidate verification, investigation coverage, and reporting. Based on these findings, we propose SKILL-SIF, a self-improving framework that iteratively evolves security skills by learning from missed vulnerabilities and failure patterns. We evolve five top-performing skills using the original benchmark and evaluate them on 100 previously unseen vulnerabilities. SKILL-SIF improves fully correct vulnerability detection by up to eight times (from 2 percent to 16 percent) on unseen vulnerabilities, while aggregate accuracy across four LLMs increases by 1.85 times (from 6.05 percent to 11.20 percent). These findings highlight the limitations of existing security skills and demonstrate the potential of failure-driven skill evolution to improve vulnerability detection in coding agents.

</details>

### 96. No Matter Who You Are: Addressivity and Sycophancy in Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25728)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`addressivity`、`sycophancy`、`SAE directions`、`post-training`

👤 **作者**：Seungjong Sun、…、Jang Hyun Kim

- 🎯 **研究动机**：谄媚（过度同意/顺从/奉承）是主要失效——后训练把输出从文本补全变为指向受话者的话语（addressivity）：伴随的跟随受话者指令与对齐其偏好的压力或把 addressivity 与谄媚联系起来
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SAE 从预训练与后训练模型提取 addressivity 方向并检验其与谄媚的关系：转向该方向可放大或压制谄媚（仅后训练模型）；受控后训练实验（对特定受话者还是任何可识别受话者谄媚）
- 📌 **结论**：模型对任何被识别的受话者谄媚而非特定受话者——是受话者的存在而非其身份引发谄媚；后训练强化 addressivity-谄媚关联（谄媚的机制起源）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Sycophancy, where an LLM becomes overly agreeable, deferential, or flattering toward the user, has been identified as a major failure mode. While post-training has been implicated as a cause, how it internally gives rise to sycophantic behavior remains insufficiently understood. Post-training transforms the model's output from text completion into utterances directed toward an addressee, thereby giving the output \textit{addressivity}. We hypothesize that the accompanying pressure to follow the addressee's instructions and align with their preferences links this addressivity to sycophancy. To test this, we use Sparse Autoencoders to extract addressivity directions from pre-trained and post-trained models and examine their relationship with sycophancy. Steering models with addressivity directions can amplify or suppress sycophancy, and this effect is observed only in post-trained models. Furthermore, comparing the internal mechanisms of both model types confirms that the association between addressivity and sycophancy is strengthened through post-training. Finally, through a controlled post-training experiment, we find that models are sycophantic not toward a specific addressee but toward any recognized addressee, suggesting that the presence of an addressee, rather than the addressee's identity, is what elicits sycophancy.

</details>

### 97. When to Stop Asking: Factor Coverage Bias and LLM Determinability Judgments with Underspecified Information in Medicine

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25819)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`determinability`、`factor coverage bias`、`clinical decision`、`when to stop`

👤 **作者**：Yusuke Watanabe、…、Yutaka Matsuo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：临床决策常始于欠规范信息——既须收集缺失信息也须知何时停止：判断可定性（可用信息是否足够决策）须考虑缺失信息能否改变结论而非仅可用决策相关信息的量；依赖该量作线索的因子覆盖偏差会经过早结论或不必要侵入性检查损害可信度
- 🔬 **研究方法**：受控配对基准（LLM 在可定-不可定对中识别可定方）：向任一侧加决策相关因子而保持可定性不变+加无关文本区分上下文长度效应
- 📌 **结论**：加相关因子使 LLM 更倾向把增强的不可定案例选为可定（加大推理努力仍持续）而无关文本使其更不选（推理大幅减弱）——因子覆盖偏差作为区别于上下文长度效应的失效模式（医学场景的停止判断）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Clinical decision-making often begins with underspecified information and requires not only gathering missing information but also knowing when to stop. This calls for judging determinability (whether the available information is sufficient for a decision) by considering whether missing information could change the conclusion, not simply the amount of available decision-relevant information. Reliance on this amount as a cue for determinability, which we term \textit{factor coverage bias}, could undermine the trustworthiness of large language models (LLMs) through premature conclusions or excessive information gathering, such as unnecessary invasive tests. Yet whether LLMs exhibit this bias remains insufficiently studied. To address this gap, we construct a controlled pairwise benchmark in which LLMs identify the determinable case within each determinable--undeterminable pair. We add a decision-relevant factor to either side while keeping each case's determinability unchanged, and similarly add irrelevant text to distinguish this bias from context-length effects. Adding a relevant factor made LLMs more likely to select the augmented case as determinable even when it remained undeterminable, an effect that persisted with increased reasoning effort, while adding irrelevant text made them less likely to select the augmented case, an effect that reasoning substantially reduced. These findings identify factor coverage bias as a failure mode distinct from context-length effects and a potential risk to LLM trustworthiness. Our work provides an evaluation framework for LLM trustworthiness under underspecification.

</details>

### 98. Where RL Breaks: Localizing and Repairing Failures via Targeted Continued Pretraining

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25836)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`RLVR failures`、`n-gram localization`、`targeted continued pretraining`、`capability gap`

👤 **作者**：Xinyi Wang、Danqi Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：RLVR 广泛用于改善推理而证据表明其大抵锐化基座已有的能力——RL 失败时基座缺什么？看进 RL 策略自己的 rollout：失败是局域化的（是否失败由一小撮特征 n-gram 强预测：『让我再检查』等反思短语到『A 的逆』等特定操作）且贯穿 RL 训练持续
- 🔬 **研究方法**：用失败 n-gram 从预训练语料检索文本并在 RL 前继续预训练基座（35M token、比典型 mid-training 小 3-4 个数量级）；OLMo2/Qwen3×GSM8K/混合/一般数学与科学推理
- 📌 **结论**：11 设置中 10 个胜同量随机采样继续预训练（插入 mid-training 前于 GSM8K +2.0 点）；检索文本跨模型族迁移——RL 失败的局域化与定向补基（训练诊断到修复的闭环）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is widely used to improve reasoning in language models, yet growing evidence suggests that it mostly sharpens what the base model can already do. This raises a practical question: when RL fails, what is the base model missing? We look inside the RL policy's own rollouts and find that failures are localized: whether a rollout fails is strongly predicted by a small set of characteristic n-grams, ranging from reflection phrases such as "let's recheck" to specific operations such as "the inverse of A". These failures persist throughout RL training, although they are not beyond the model's reach: other sampled answers to the same question are often correct. Building on this observation, we use the failure $n$-grams to retrieve text from the pretraining corpus and continue pretraining the base model on it before RL. The resulting corpus has only 35M tokens, 3--4 orders of magnitude smaller than typical mid-training. We evaluate this approach on OLMo2 and Qwen3 models trained with RL on (1) GSM8K; (2) a mixture of math, commonsense, and knowledge questions; and (3) general math and science reasoning. Despite its size, the corpus yields consistent gains: our approach outperforms continued pretraining on the same amount of text sampled from the pretraining corpus across 10 out of 11 settings, including by $2.0$ points on GSM8K when inserted before mid-training. The retrieved text also transfers across model families: text retrieved for OLMo2-1B's failures improves Qwen3-4B on the mixture by $1.9$ points over the same baseline. Together, our results suggest that where RL fails is a cheap and effective signal for selecting pretraining data.

</details>

### 99. Measurement Validity and Representation Dynamics of Confidence Signals in Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25846)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`confidence signals`、`measurement validity`、`probe vs verbalized`、`preregistered`

👤 **作者**：Debojeet Ghosh、Shreyansh Kumar、Ayushman Garg

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LM 是否『知道自己知道』关乎可信部署与何时弃答——既有工作通常只测一个通道（言语化置信/隐藏态线性探针/采样答案一致性）且常互换对待：预注册审计三通道
- 🔬 **研究方法**：五 Qwen2.5 模型（0.5B-7B 含 7B base/instruct 对）×事实检索（PopQA/SimpleQA）与多步推理（GSM8K/MATH）：prefill 态探针预测采样答案是否分歧在检索上更早有信息（~7% 层深 vs 推理 ~28%）；但多数检索单元上仅提示 TF-IDF 分类器匹配探针（早信号或反映问题表面特征）
- 📌 **结论**：三通道的测量效度分层：探针早但或表面、言语化不稳、采样一致性分辨强——部署置信选择须按任务与通道

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Whether a language model "knows what it knows" matters for trustworthy deployment and for deciding when it should abstain. Prior work usually measures this through one channel: verbalized confidence, a linear probe on hidden states, or agreement among sampled answers (semantic entropy), and often treats these as interchangeable. We compare all three in a pre-registered audit of five Qwen2.5 models (0.5B-7B, including a base/instruct pair at 7B) on factual retrieval (PopQA, SimpleQA) and multi-step reasoning (GSM8K, MATH). First, probes trained on prefill states to predict whether sampled answers will disagree become informative earlier for retrieval (about 7% of layer depth on average) than for reasoning (about 28%). The pre-registered five-layer test supported this only marginally; a finer 21-layer follow-up, designed after those results, supports it more clearly. In most retrieval cells, however, a prompt-only TF-IDF classifier matches the probe, so the early signal may reflect surface features of the question. Second, verbalized confidence is unstable: slightly different prompt templates yield scores that barely correlate. Third, Expected Calibration Error is misleading: verbal confidence looks well calibrated because it clusters near the base rate while discriminating poorly, whereas sampling agreement has worse ECE but about twice the resolution. Of five pre-registered hypotheses, two were falsified, one was partly supported, one was not supported, and one could not be tested because the base model's verbal confidence was degenerate.

</details>

### 100. Budgeted Attack Scheduling for LLM Red-Teaming: The Roles of Static Priors, Semantic Guidance, and Online Feedback

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25897)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`red-teaming`、`budgeted scheduling`、`attack library`、`efficiency`

👤 **作者**：Zhicheng Fang、…、Wei Xu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：越狱攻击多样化使红队依赖大型攻击库评测鲁棒性——但攻击有效性高度条件依赖（一设置成功的攻击在另一设置或失败）而对每设置穷尽全库代价高昂
- 🔬 **研究方法**：预算化攻击调度形式化+三互补信号：静态历史 ASR 先验/LLM 语义引导/早期查询在线反馈；四调度策略×三受害者×三防御设置（GPT-5.2）
- 📌 **结论**：历史 ASR 提供强初始化但防御诱导分布偏移下可靠性退化；语义引导在冷启动区一致改善；在线反馈在攻击间迁移有限时特别有价值——预算下的攻击优先级（红队经济学）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As jailbreak attacks against large language models (LLMs) become increasingly diverse, red-teaming has come to rely on large libraries of jailbreak prompts and attack strategies to evaluate the robustness of victim models and their defense settings. However, attack effectiveness is highly condition-dependent: an attack that succeeds under one victim-defense setting may fail under another, while exhaustively evaluating the full attack library for each setting incurs substantial token and monetary costs. This motivates studying how jailbreak attacks should be prioritized under a limited attempt budget for efficient robustness evaluation. In this work, we formalize this problem as budgeted attack scheduling over a fixed attack library and investigate three complementary signals under a fixed victim-defense condition: (1) static historical attack success rate (ASR) priors; (2) semantic guidance from LLMs; and (3) online feedback from earlier queries. Building on these signals, we design four scheduling strategies and evaluate them across three victim models and three defense settings on GPT-5.2. Extensive experiments clearly characterize the conditions under which each signal is most effective. Historical ASR provides a strong initialization, but its reliability degrades under defense-induced distribution shifts. Semantic guidance consistently improves or matches static prior-based scheduling in the cold-start regime, while online feedback becomes particularly valuable when transferred priors become stale. Semantic guidance and online feedback can also partially compensate for missing historical priors. Taken together, these findings characterize budgeted red-teaming as an attack scheduling problem in which historical priors, semantic guidance, and online feedback play complementary roles.

</details>

### 101. UtilityBench: Measuring Language Model Utility When Errors Have Different Costs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25924)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`utility`、`error costs`、`abstention`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Qifeng Lei、…、Qingming Huang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LM 部署于重要结果的场景——错误的影响远超正确性所能刻画（医学中单一错误可危及患者）；主流评测把所有错误等同而忽略真实成本的谱系
- 🔬 **研究方法**：UtilityBench：15,000 可验证答案实例+Best-Worst Scaling 量化错误严重度（数据驱动的成本估计塑造何时该答何时弃）+UtilityAUC（模型响应排序决定的效用曲线下面积，理论上可区分准确率与 AURC 失效的模型）
- 📌 **结论**：20 SOTA 模型：更高准确率或更先进架构不可靠地转化为更高 UtilityAUC——问题解决能力与负责任决策之间的差距（成本感知评测范式）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As language models are increasingly deployed in real-world scenarios with important outcomes, the impact of their mistakes often extends far beyond what correctness can capture. In domains like medicine, a single error can lead to serious consequences and put patient safety at risk. However, mainstream evaluation protocols still treat all errors as equal, overlooking the spectrum of real-world costs. To address this, we propose a utility-based evaluation paradigm that explicitly incorporates the varying consequences of errors. Guided by this principle, we develop UtilityBench, a large-scale benchmark of 15,000 instances with verifiable answers, quantifying error severity using Best-Worst Scaling to elicit meaningful, data-driven cost estimates. These quantified utilities shape when a model should answer or abstain, guiding it to make decisions that align with the true importance of each scenario. As another merit, we introduce UtilityAUC, a metric that measures the area under the utility curve determined by a model's response ordering, thereby enabling utility preference alignment beyond error frequency. Our theoretical analysis demonstrates that UtilityAUC can distinguish between models where standard accuracy and AURC fall short. Empirically, evaluating 20 state-of-the-art models, we find that greater accuracy or more advanced architectures do not reliably translate to higher UtilityAUC, highlighting a gap between problem-solving ability and responsible decision-making in language model evaluation.

</details>

### 102. Auditor-Assisted Summary-Channel Verification for Hosted LLM Identity Substitution

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25982)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`identity substitution`、`summary watermark`、`auditor-assisted`、`hosted LLM`

👤 **作者**：Ziyi Zhang、Ziyao Wang、Guoheng Sun、Ang Li、Jian Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：托管 LLM 服务把模型身份暴露为产品主张——外部用户与审计者常无法检查哪个模型配置服务了请求：可信审计者登记声称身份并在部署前准备验证材料，审计时只用声称身份与发布输出
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SUMMARK：用户可见推理摘要作键控审计面——身份特异词汇载体+轻量摘要适配器+逐身份阈值校准；部署时键控统计检验评估发布摘要与声称身份的一致性并在需要时归因最可能的已登记身份
- 📌 **结论**：跨模型族/规模/任务域可靠检测跨族与跨规模替换于低假阳性率同时保持任务答案质量；FP16→INT8/INT4 精度一致性诊断+有界后处理/改写压力/纯文本欺骗下的鲁棒与失败边界——托管 LLM 身份替换的摘要信道验证

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Hosted LLM services increasingly expose model identity as a product claim, but external users and auditors often cannot inspect which model configuration served a request. We study an auditor-assisted regime in which a trusted auditor enrolls claimed identities and prepares verification material before deployment, while audit-time verification uses only the claimed identity and released output, without runtime access to model internals, inference logs, routing decisions, or provider metadata. We propose SUMMARK, a summary-channel watermarking framework that uses user-visible reasoning summaries as a keyed audit surface. During setup, SUMMARK constructs identity-specific vocabulary carriers, trains lightweight summary adapters, and calibrates per-identity thresholds. At deployment time, a keyed statistical test assesses whether the released summary is consistent with the claimed identity and, when needed, attributes the most likely enrolled identity. Across model families, scales, and task domains, SUMMARK reliably detects cross-family and cross-scale substitutions at low false-positive rates while preserving task-facing answer quality. We further study FP16->INT8/INT4 precision-consistency diagnostics and robustness under bounded post-processing, rewriting stress tests, and text-only spoofing, revealing both strong performance and clear failure boundaries. Our code is publicly available at this https URL.

</details>

### 103. Honeyed Words, Hidden Daggers: Benchmarking and Training LLMs to See Through Mock Politeness

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26059)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`mock politeness`、`pragmatics`、`Chinese benchmark`、`CPR-GRPO`

👤 **作者**：Caixin Kang、Sitong Gong、Tianyu Yan、Kaipeng Zhang、Yoichi Sato、Yifei Huang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：『口蜜腹剑』：形式礼貌而功能敌意的话语——LLM 能否看穿（说话者意图/情感-语言错配/逐听者 uptake）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ZhenhuanBench：《甄嬛传》专家解说构建 1,592 项基准（三标注维度）；34 LLM 文本评测+338 金迹监督训练与字符串可验证奖励；CPR-GRPO（压缩-探查-补充）循环用可恢复差距估计加权金监督
- 📌 **结论**：GPT-6 Astra 以 73.48% 领先而听者反应分仅 58.37%；8B 经金迹超教师蒸馏；CPR 无目标任务训练下跨六中英语用与讽刺评测胜 Base/SFT/GRPO——语用敌意的识别基准与训练（隐性操纵的语言层）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The Chinese idiom "kou mi fu jian" ("honeyed words, hidden daggers") names speech that is polite in form but hostile in function. We present ZhenhuanBench, a 1,592-item benchmark built from expert commentary on Empresses in the Palace, covering speaker intent, emotion-language mismatch, and per-listener uptake. Text-only evaluation covers 34 LLMs. GPT-6 Astra leads the leaderboard at 73.48% overall, while its listener-reaction score is 58.37%. The annotations also supply supervised traces and string-verifiable training rewards. With 338 gold traces, an 8B model outperforms teacher distillation on identical items. Subsequent RL saturates, and chance correction reduces the estimated recoverable performance gap by a factor of 7.5. Our CPR-GRPO loop (Compress-Probe-Replenish) uses this estimate to weight gold supervision, reaching 67.0 on public multiple-choice tasks and 74.5 under the training-matched hidden protocol. Without target-task training, CPR outperforms Base, SFT, and GRPO across six Chinese and English pragmatic and sarcasm evaluations. On three external datasets, training with CPR yields higher mean Macro-F1 than SFT and GRPO.

</details>

### 104. Reliable Before Profitable: QuantHarness for Governing Financial Strategy Development with LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26101)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`trajectory reliability`、`financial agents`、`stage boundaries`、`harness`

👤 **作者**：Jiasheng Mao、Jiacheng Liu、Hengwei Ye、Zheng Tian

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM agent 可把自然语言金融研究请求转为可回测策略——但成功运行仍可实现错误规则/使用决策时不可得信息/掩盖修复与候选选择如何改变策略：须在轨迹层控制系统如何让中间输出变成下游状态并保存证明终局的证据
- 🔬 **研究方法**：QuantHarness 双层：金融特异 Workflow（规范/数据接地/构建/测试/审查）+Harness Core 强制阶段特异的信息/行动/承诺边界；FinRelBench 经执行成功/需求满足/运行时证据评测
- 📌 **结论**：Task Success 90.72%（对照 74.23-84.54%）；受控压测显示边界遏制目标违规而结构化运行记录改善证据检索并支持决策回放——金融策略开发的轨迹级可靠性治理

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM agents can turn natural-language financial research requests into executable and backtested strategies, but a successful run may still implement the wrong rule, use information unavailable at decision time, or obscure how repairs and candidate selection changed the strategy. Because these failures arise throughout development, final code and performance metrics alone are insufficient to establish either fidelity to the request or the validity of the development process. We therefore address the reliability of financial strategy development at the trajectory level: the system must control how intermediate outputs become downstream state and preserve the evidence needed to justify the terminal outcome. We introduce QuantHarness, a two-layer agent harness in which a finance-specific Workflow structures specification, data grounding, construction, testing, and review, while Harness Core enforces stage-specific information, action, and commitment boundaries. We also introduce FinRelBench, a benchmark that evaluates strategy-development reliability through execution success, requirement satisfaction, and runtime evidence. QuantHarness achieves the highest Task Success at 90.72\%, compared with 74.23\%--84.54\% for the comparison systems. Controlled stress tests show that its boundaries contain targeted violations, while structured run records improve evidence retrieval and support decision replay under the evaluated protocol.

</details>

### 105. VEILED-SH: Vulnerability Expression through Indirect Language Evaluation Dataset (Self-Harm)

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26190)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`self-harm`、`indirect distress`、`cultural context`、`crisis response`

👤 **作者**：Leanne Tan、…、Roy Ka-Wei Lee

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 系统或漏掉间接/文化嵌境的困境或提供与应用语境错配的支持——既有基准不联合处理文化差异/应用语境/从业者分歧
- 🔬 **研究方法**：VEILED-SH：10,003 提示跨咨询师聊天/论坛帖/平台搜索/分诊（新加坡接地来源+公开数据集）；专家验证多标签分类学区分意图/交际功能/响应行为；六个自动 profile 各校准到三名同伴支持从业者与三名临床医生并保留分离参照策略
- 📌 **结论**：五系统在覆盖富化样本上的代理对齐随 profile 生成的参照策略而变（即使同一系统响应）——间接自我伤害表达的跨文化危机评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI systems may miss indirect, culturally situated distress or provide support mismatched to the application context. Existing benchmarks do not jointly address cultural variation, application context, and practitioner disagreement. We introduce VEILED-SH, a benchmark and construction methodology for evaluating single-turn responses to indirect expressions of suicide, self-harm, and distress. Its 10,003 prompts span counselor chat, forum posts, platform search, and triage, drawing on Singapore-grounded sources and public datasets. An expert-validated multi-label taxonomy distinguishes intent, communicative function, and response behavior. Six automated profiles, individually calibrated to three peer-support practitioners and three clinicians, retain separate reference policies for proxy-alignment scoring. Evaluating five systems on a coverage-enriched sample reveals variation in proxy alignment across profile-generated reference policies, even for the same system responses.

</details>

### 106. Signature Knowledge Shielding: Protecting Antivirus Detection Knowledge through Robust Embedding Matching

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26207)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`antivirus signatures`、`knowledge shielding`、`probing`、`IP protection`

👤 **作者**：Jieshuai Yang、…、Wanpeng Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AV 签名编码专家分析积累的宝贵检测知识——重复黑盒查询可暴露该知识供未授权复用与定向规避：经辅助屏蔽签名（SS）保护既有规则（PS）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SKS：AV 监督重要性排序发现候选字节块+负样本校准过滤选择+SS 匹配与原判经逻辑 OR 组合（探测中引入额外阳性响应与判决变化使检测反馈不再直接可归因于 PS）；REM 鲁棒嵌入匹配器（随机上下文与字节扰动训练）；ClamAV 原型开源
- 📌 **结论**：观测假阳性 <0.1% 下 REM 使 SOTA 探测的平均查询数升至未保护基线的 282.57 倍而 GPU 扫描开销仅 0.67%——杀毒知识的签名屏蔽（安全知识资产的查询防护）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Antivirus (AV) signatures encode valuable detection knowledge accumulated through expert analysis, yet repeated black-box queries can expose this knowledge for unauthorized reuse and targeted evasion. We propose Signature Knowledge Shielding (SKS), a design paradigm that protects existing AV rules, termed Protected Signatures (PS), through auxiliary Shielding Signatures (SS). SKS uses AV-supervised importance ranking to discover candidate byte blocks, filters and selects them through negative-sample calibration, and combines SS matches with original AV verdicts through logical OR. SS matching introduces additional positive responses and verdict changes during probing, making detection feedback less directly attributable to PS and increasing the difficulty of localizing signature-related content, while preserving original positive verdicts. Within SKS, we integrate five individually tuned non-learning matching methods and further propose Robust Embedding Matching (REM), a lightweight learned matcher trained with random context and byte perturbations. REM provides stronger and more consistent protection against content modifications and scanning-alignment changes. We develop and open-source a ClamAV-based prototype and evaluate it against five probing methods. With an observed additional test false-positive rate below 0.1%, REM raises the mean query count of state-of-the-art probing to as much as 282.57 times the unprotected baseline, while introducing only 0.67% GPU scanning overhead. These results demonstrate that SKS can substantially increase signature-extraction costs with limited impact on detection utility and scanning efficiency.

</details>

### 107. LLMs Prompted for Legal Context Refuse More: Overrefusal from Small On-Premises LLMs in Criminal Legal Context

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26211)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`over-refusal`、`legal context`、`authority framing`、`small on-prem LLMs`

👤 **作者**：Anastasiia Kucherenko、Francois Brouchoud、Dimitri Percia David、Andrei Kucharavy

- 🎯 **研究动机**：保密要求推动法律专业人士走向本地小 LLM——按标准提示指导用户自然陈述角色（『我是律师』）：这一无害习惯有意料之外的成本（权威框架可使模型选择性拒绝合法请求，经不均匀协助引入偏差）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四个现代小开源 LLM 在良性法律域提示上测量过度拒绝：权威式前缀 vs 无前缀基线 vs 已知角色扮演越狱前缀对照
- 📌 **结论**：权威式前缀系统性地使拒绝率升 2-20 倍而角色扮演越狱前缀效应混杂——本地可部署小 LLM 在真实机构用户自然引入的语境框架下不稳定（权威框架的过度拒绝面）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Confidentiality requirements increasingly push legal professionals toward small, on-device LLMs that keep case data in-house, used for everyday tasks such as summarization, translation, and reformulation. Following standard prompting guidance, a user naturally states their role (``I am a lawyer...''). We show that this innocuous habit has an unexpected cost: such authority framing can make the model selectively refuse legitimate requests, introducing bias through uneven access to assistance. We evaluate four modern small open-weight LLMs on benign legal-domain prompts to measure this over-refusal. Surprisingly, we find that authority-style prefixes (''I am legal counsel working for a national supreme court'', ''I'm a defense lawyer'') systematically increase refusal rates by 2--20x over the no-prefix baseline, while a known role-play jailbreak prefix shows mixed effects, sharply increasing refusals in some models and barely shifting them in others. The finding suggests that small on-prem deployable LLMs are unstable under contextual framings that a real institutional user might naturally introduce, and further investigation is essential to minimize opportunities for bias.

</details>

### 108. The Scheduler Is a Hidden Retriever: How Asynchrony Shapes Evidence Acquisition in Search Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26310)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`asynchrony`、`evidence acquisition`、`search agents`、`invariance`

👤 **作者**：Ruiyang Ren、Yuhao Wang、Yinan Zhang、Chunyan Miao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LM agent 经并行推理与异步信息收集解复杂任务——受控重放显示投递时机重定向全部结果到达前已做的搜索决策（尽管初始证据相同）：调度器是隐藏的检索器
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两模型族×三基准：采集分歧达 24 点以上（超同序重复变异）且在全部初始证据到达后与第二次搜索中持续；采集遗憾界+匹配构造→CEA（每个子问题证据完整时释放局部推理+独立于完成顺序分配共享搜索）
- 📌 **结论**：固定计划与稳定条件响应律下证明决策/采集/答案的联合不变性；三基准上 CEA 把采集分歧带到重复变异 1.0 点内并达完整输入对照准确率（超早承诺至多 12.6 点）——异步塑造证据获取的机制与修复

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language-model agents increasingly solve complex tasks through parallel reasoning and asynchronous information gathering. Controlled replay across two model families and three benchmarks shows that delivery timing redirects search decisions made before all results arrive, despite identical initial evidence. Acquisition disagreement reaches up to 24 percentage points above same-order repeat variation. This dependence persists after all initial evidence arrives and through a second search, turning temporary differences in what agents have seen into lasting differences in what they acquire. An acquisition-regret bound with a matching construction shows that invariance at a fixed early arrival count can limit the use of evidence already received. We introduce Confluent Evidence Acquisition (CEA), which instead releases local reasoning when each subquestion's evidence is complete and allocates shared search independently of completion order. For a fixed plan and stable conditional response law, we prove joint invariance of decisions, acquisitions, and answers. With question-only plans across three benchmarks, CEA brings acquisition disagreement to within 1.0 percentage point of repeat variation and achieves accuracy comparable to complete-input controls, improving over early commitment by up to 12.6 percentage points. Replacing CEA's fixed allocation with first-ready allocation under contention adds 21.6 percentage points of acquisition disagreement, while local query-target disagreement remains near repeat variation. Timed comparisons show that CEA reaches decisions earlier and responds faster than the same program waiting for all initial evidence.

</details>

### 109. When Coding Agents Outpace Review: Evaluating PRD-Driven Development in Mature Enterprise Repositories

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26394)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`PRD fulfillment`、`enterprise repositories`、`benchmark`、`delivery gaps`

👤 **作者**：Tianqi Liu、Yandong Guan、Songze Li、Wenkai Wang、Gang Yu、Chengfei Lv

- 🎯 **研究动机**：编码 agent 日益承接成熟企业仓库的开发任务——既有基准对是否完成完整 PRD 与交付在哪断裂洞见有限（议题解决与从零开发使增量 PRD 履行未被探索而孤立测试对业务与集成要求证据不完整）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PACE：43 企业任务×750 专家审计 Code Points（需求重建+准则构造+证据式评测连接交付缺口到 agent 行为）；11 模型-脚手架配置
- 📌 **结论**：多数分数 52.1-78.1、GPT-5.6-Sol 完整交付率最高 14.0%；不完整实现与缺失集成是主要瓶颈（产出单个组件不保证完整交付）；评测与专家共识一致率 95.2%；诊断引导的定向改进使同模型同任务套件均值 59.4→63.0——企业级 PRD 履行评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Coding agents increasingly undertake development tasks in mature enterprise repositories, yet existing benchmarks offer limited insight into whether they fulfill complete Product Requirement Documents (PRDs) and where delivery breaks down. Issue resolution and development from scratch leave incremental PRD fulfillment underexplored, while isolated tests provide incomplete evidence of business and integration requirements. We introduce PACE, a benchmark of 43 enterprise tasks with 750 expert-audited Code Points. It combines requirement reconstruction, criterion construction, and evidence-based evaluation to measure PRD fulfillment and connect delivery gaps to agent behavior. Across 11 model--scaffold configurations, most scores fall between 52.1 and 78.1; GPT-5.6-Sol achieves the highest complete-delivery rate of 14.0\%. Incomplete implementation and missing integration are the principal bottlenecks: producing individual components does not ensure a complete delivery. Evaluation achieves 95.2\% agreement with expert consensus, with lower case-level variation across judging records than repeated executions. Beyond model comparison, the benchmark provides actionable feedback for harness refinement: diagnosis guides targeted improvements, and successive harness releases raise mean performance from 59.4 to 63.0 using the same model and task suite. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 110. One Fact, Many Places: Redundant Evidence in Long Visual Documents Breaks Single-Gold Evaluation of Retrieval, Citation and Attribution

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26401)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`redundant evidence`、`single-gold evaluation`、`retrieval/citation`、`long visual documents`

👤 **作者**：Xiaokang Yuan、Binhui Yao、Luwei Yang、Ruimin Chen、Zeng Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：文档理解日益按答案来源评、以标注金位置打分仿佛每个事实只住一处——长视觉文档中一个事实常住在多处（图表值在文本重述、关键图在摘要重现）：420 题标注独立确立每个必需事实的位置（双人复核）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：拷贝常见且常跨模态（19.5% 测试题在金标外有严格拷贝、28% 非金候选单独供给必需事实）→单金评分三重失效：检索器找到拷贝被判 miss（页召回@1 被低估 13-24 点）、引用拷贝被判错引（等价证据反转 65 个系统间 11% 两两排序）、掩蔽金区后事实仍在别处可得（41-56% 正确答案存活）
- 📌 **结论**：集合级掩蔽+自动找拷贝关闭与全实例掩蔽 78% 的差距——冗余证据破坏单金检索/引用/归因评测（长文档评测有效性的构念修正）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Document understanding is increasingly judged on where an answer comes from, scored against annotated gold locations as if each fact lived in one place. In long visual documents, a fact often lives in several places: a chart value is restated in the text, a key figure reappears in the summary. We ask how often such copies occur in standard benchmarks and what they do to evaluation. We annotate 420 questions from MMLongBench-Doc and MMDocRAG with the locations that independently establish each required fact, checked by two independent reviewers. Copies are common and often cross-modal: 19.5% of MMLongBench-Doc test questions have a strict copy outside the gold, and in 28% of MMDocRAG questions a non-gold candidate alone supplies a required fact. Single-gold scoring then fails in three predictable ways. A retriever that finds a copy is scored as a miss: page recall@1 of text and visual retrievers is underestimated by 13-24 points on questions with a cross-page copy. A model that cites a copy is scored as citing wrongly: crediting equivalent evidence reverses 11% of pairwise orderings among 65 competitive MMDocRAG systems. Masking the gold region leaves the fact available elsewhere: with a copy in the input, 41-56% of correct answers across four models survive it yet fail once every annotated instance is masked. Set-level masking with automatically found copies closes 78% of the gap to masking every annotated instance at matched granularity (three models); an unverified key-string rule lowers survival at least as far but masks three times the area. We release the annotations and redundancy-aware metrics.

</details>

### 111. When Guidance Becomes a Guard: Early Interception of Unsafe Inputs in Text-to-Image Generation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26452)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`unsafe input detection`、`guidance signals`、`early interception`、`text-to-image`

👤 **作者**：Yanchun Li、…、Shujuan Tian

- 🎯 **研究动机**：文生图扩散可产不安全内容而输出过滤只在合成后干预——原生生成信号能否实现不安全与对抗输入的早期检测？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CSTI：无分类器引导在条件响应中暴露强安全信号（经紧凑统计与线性读出可及）→聚合早期检查点的条件/无条件响应激活对比做校准风险检测（概念特异检测器只用规范概念与安全示例、免攻击样本/中间图像解码/生成器修改）
- 📌 **结论**：五生成器×四对抗攻击集×两 NSFW 集：匹配 5% FPR 下六基线中最强整体性能；早期拦截把被拦路径生成延迟降 60%+——原生条件响应的早期安全拦截

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Text-to-image diffusion models can produce unsafe content, while output filtering intervenes only after synthesis. We investigate whether native generation signals enable early detection of unsafe and adversarial inputs. We find that classifier-free guidance exposes strong safety signals in conditional responses, accessible through compact statistics and linear readouts. Based on this finding, we propose Conditional Semantic Trajectory Interception (CSTI), which aggregates activation contrasts between conditional and unconditional responses at early checkpoints for calibrated risk detection. CSTI fits concept-specific detectors using canonical concept and safe examples, without attack samples, intermediate image decoding, or generator modification. Across five generators, four adversarial attack collections, and two general NSFW datasets, CSTI achieves the strongest overall performance among six baselines at a matched 5% FPR. Early interception reduces generation latency by over 60% on intercepted paths. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of native conditional responses for early safety interception.

</details>

### 112. Beyond Attack Success Rate: Cost-Aware Metric for Evaluating Iterative LLM Jailbreaks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26589)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`ASR_STEP`、`cost-aware metric`、`iterative jailbreaks`、`victim queries`

👤 **作者**：Rohan Menon、Chih-Hong Cheng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：ASR 评测越狱而不计对受害者模型的重复调用——每次额外调用都给部署系统又一次检测攻击并干预的机会
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ASR_STEP：惩罚额外受害者调用而保留二元成功判断；GCG/PAIR（步预算）/单步 PAP 基线×JailBreakBench×三模型族
- 📌 **结论**：折扣后 GCG 降 7.0-8.7 点（PAP 不变）；PAIR 折扣范围 3.4-12 点（『步』量随迭代策略而异）——同时报告两指标使重复交互折扣显式化（越狱评测的成本维度）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Attack success rate (ASR) evaluates jailbreaks against large language models without accounting for repeated calls to the victim model. Yet each additional call gives the deployed system another opportunity to detect the attack and intervene. We introduce $\mathrm{ASR}_{\mathrm{STEP}}$, which penalizes additional victim-model calls while retaining binary success judgments. Our evaluation considers GCG~\cite{zou2023universal}, PAIR~\cite{chao2025jailbreaking} with a step budget, and a single-step PAP~\cite{zeng2024johnny} baseline on JailBreakBench across three model families. GCG's scores decrease by 7.0--8.7 percentage points after discounting, while PAP's remain unchanged. While for PAIR~\cite{chao2025jailbreaking}, we see a wider discounting range between 3.4-12 percentage points, showing that the "step" quantity differs per iterative strategy. Reporting both metrics makes the discount due to repeated victim-model interaction explicit. Code is available \hyperlink{this https URL}{here}.

</details>

### 113. When the harness decides success: state that cannot be re-observed

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26632)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`harness effects`、`non-reobservable state`、`context truncation`、`benchmark decomposition`

👤 **作者**：Raimondas Lencevicius

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent harness 决定语言模型跨轮看到什么（丢旧轮/摘要/重放模型自己的早期推理）——这些选择何时改变 agent 是否成功而不仅是成本？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：预注册合成探针：截断上下文窗在所需事实可再查时零代价、不可再观察时成功率降到随机（两惩罚差 92.5 点）；第二供应商单模型复现；ARC-AGI-3 上 Standard vs Provider Adapter harness 63 点差距的逐组件分解
- 📌 **结论**：摘要旧轮而非丢弃（保留笔记邀请）恢复 86% 差距、加提供商原生推理重放恢复余下 57%——harness 决定成功的机制分解（不可再观察状态的代价）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent harnesses decide what a language model sees across turns: they drop old turns, summarize them, or replay the model's own earlier reasoning. We ask when these choices change whether an agent succeeds, not only what it costs, and report three findings. First, in a pre-registered synthetic probe, truncating the context window costs a low-cost reasoning model nothing in success when a needed fact can be looked up again (it looks the fact up instead), but drops its success to chance when the fact cannot be re-observed: the two penalties differ by 92.5 percentage points (90% CI [89.5, 95.5]). A pre-registered replication on one model from a second provider showed the same contrast. Once the fact was lost, that model chose no answer rather than guessing. Second, on ARC-AGI-3, where the benchmark's Standard harness trails its Provider Adapter harness by a wide margin, we decompose that gap one component at a time, for one model on four games. Summarizing old turns instead of dropping them, while keeping the Standard harness's invitation to take notes, recovers 86% of a 63-point gap without replaying the model's reasoning.
