The same harness without the invitation recovers 43%, and adding the provider's native replay of the model's reasoning recovers the remaining 57%. These are point estimates from one counted run per configuration and game, and the replay share depends on a single run. Third, in the benchmark's published runs and in ours, the Adapter's advantage in the probability of clearing a level it has reached is larger at deep levels than at early ones. This largely restates where runs stopped and does not separate a growing advantage from a constant one.

</details>

### 114. AllPath: Layered Verification for a Deployed, Self-Revising LLM Trading Agent under Human-Gated and Autonomous Regimes

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26662)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`layered verification`、`deployed trading agent`、`human-gated`、`self-revision`

👤 **作者**：Tian Zhang、Meng Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 交易 agent 几乎只在回测与自主基准评测——部署中动作不可逆，提出不同问题：谁验证 agent 动作、每个验证器结构上能捕获哪些失败？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AllPath 部署于活体纸券商账户：确定性规则引擎盯仓位+每晚自审并提议修订自身策略；三层验证（代码验证不依赖判断的确定性门/agent 在固定界限内自验带迭代上限且无行动工具/人经批准屏验证判断）；两周半全部自修订人门 vs 五周反思修订自动应用（同一代码门后）
- 📌 **结论**：每层捕获不同失败：代码门保持修订一致（拒绝零提案）；人拒绝两个全过代码检查的提案（市场分歧）并移除 19 条规则中的 11 条——部署交易 agent 的分层验证与人机权限切换实测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM trading agents are evaluated almost exclusively in backtests and autonomous benchmark harnesses. Deployment, where actions are irreversible, raises a different question: who verifies the agent's actions, and which failures can each verifier structurally catch? We report on AllPath, a personal LLM trading agent deployed against a live paper-brokerage account. The system watches positions through a deterministic rules engine, and each night it reviews its own day and proposes revisions to its own trading strategies. Verification is split across three layers. Code verifies what must not depend on judgment: deterministic gates enforce risk limits and block any strategy revision that fails an integrity check. The agent verifies itself within fixed bounds: its nightly review has an iteration cap and no tools that can act. A human verifies judgment, through approval screens that show the current state of each proposal. We also compared two verification policies for the agent's self-revisions on the live system: two and a half weeks with every self-revision human-gated, then five weeks with reflection revisions auto-applied behind the same code gates, while the operator's chat channel stayed human-gated throughout. Each layer caught a different kind of failure. The code gates rejected no proposal; their role was keeping revisions consistent. The human rejected two proposals that passed every code check, both over a disagreement about the market, and later removed 11 of the 19 rules the agent had added on its own, at a median lag of eight days. The most instructive production incidents, such as strategies silently left unmonitored, passed every gate; the operator found them by hand, and the fix each time was an interface change that makes the condition visible. We contribute the layered architecture, the two-regime account, and a production incident taxonomy, none observable in a backtest.

</details>

### 115. BrushOff: Stroke-Based Re-rendering as a Watermark Removal Attack on Painting

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26682)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`watermark removal`、`stroke-based rendering`、`painting`、`BrushOff`

👤 **作者**：Hao Chen、Junnan Xu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：不可见水印标记画作所有权——一个简单攻击移除所测最鲁棒者：用预训练笔画渲染器以笔触重画水印画作（免训练/免水印知识/免生成模型）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：BrushOff：五风格 500 幅画作×四种水印；VINE 在全部 14 种常规扭曲下保持 ≥97.8% 检测，重画降到 1.8% 而无参考质量分接近干净画作；扩散再生同移除水平下 DreamSim 距离两倍且改变画作内容；笔画数可数故攻击强度可逐步控制
- 📌 **结论**：邻域相对亮度探针在重画下存活而绝对亮度探针不存活；同一探针在扩散再生下失败而 VINE 在重画下失败——对两种攻击的鲁棒性是分离属性，基准应双测（水印攻击面的正交分解）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Invisible watermarks are used to mark who owns a painting. We show that a simple attack removes the most robust one we test. The attack redraws the watermarked painting with brush strokes, using a pretrained stroke-based renderer. We call this attack BrushOff. It needs no training, no knowledge of the watermark, and no generative model. We test four watermarks on 500 paintings from five styles. VINE, a recent watermark built to survive image editing, survives all 14 conventional distortions we test, with at least 97.8% detection. Repainting lowers its detection to 1.8%, while the output keeps a no-reference quality score close to that of the clean paintings. Diffusion regeneration can also remove VINE, but at the same removal level it moves the image twice as far from the painting in DreamSim distance, and it changes what the painting shows. Because the renderer draws a countable number of strokes, we can control the attack strength step by step. Finally, we test what kind of signal survives. A simple probe that stores bits in the relative brightness of neighboring regions survives repainting, while a probe that uses absolute brightness does not. The same probe fails under diffusion regeneration, while VINE fails under repainting. Robustness to these two attacks is therefore a separate property, and benchmarks should test both.

</details>

### 116. Intra-Rubric Reward Divergence: Mitigating Rubric Hacking through Latent Dimension Recovery and Balancing

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26685)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`rubric hacking`、`intra-rubric divergence`、`dimension aggregation`、`preference mining`

👤 **作者**：Jianqiang Xia、Han Xu、Dianxi Shi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：rubric 作弊限制 rubric 基 RL 的性能与训练鲁棒性——把 rubric 分解为子维度揭示 rubric 内奖励分歧：总分升高时某些子维度系统下降（模型牺牲特定质量面优化聚合目标）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两现象（维度失衡使策略偏向重覆盖维度/维度缺失致奖励与真实质量的系统差距）→FRDA（各维度内分别竞争以缓解失衡）+RSDC（从整体偏好信号隐式编码的评估维度挖掘补全覆盖）
- 📌 **结论**：聊天/科学/医疗域：FRDA 更稳定改善而 RSDC 超单奖励与朴素多奖励聚合——rubric 作弊的维度级机制与缓解（奖励作弊的细粒度化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Rubric hacking limits both performance and training robustness in rubric-based reinforcement learning. By decomposing rubrics into sub-dimensions, we reveal the Intra-Rubric Reward Divergence: as the total rubric score increases, certain sub-dimension scores systematically decrease, suggesting the model sacrifices specific quality aspects to optimize the aggregated objective. From the sub-dimension perspective, we observe two phenomena: (1) dimension imbalance, causing the policy to favor heavily covered dimensions; (2) dimension absence, leading to a systematic gap between rewards and true quality. To mitigate these issues, we propose Fair-Race Dimension Aggregation (FRDA), which has generations compete within each dimension separately to mitigate rubric imbalance, and Residual Semantic Dimension Completion (RSDC), which mines evaluation dimensions implicitly encoded in a holistic preference signal to complete rubric coverage. Experiments in chat, science, and medical domains indicate that FRDA mitigates rubric hacking with more stable improvements, while RSDC outperforms both single-reward training and naive multi-reward aggregation, yielding generally higher-quality responses.

</details>

### 117. Accountable partial understanding: securing the epistemic attack surface of agentic systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26799)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`epistemic attack surface`、`accountable partial understanding`、`agentic systems`、`design principles`

👤 **作者**：Josiah Dykstra

- 🎯 **研究动机**：攻击者从防御者不理解处获利——软件安全把系统行为与人类理解的差距当作经分析收窄的条件、余量记为已接受风险；agentic AI 改变该差距：软件读写解释并授权安全相关行为时，部分理解移动更快、更不透明且耦合到被委派权威
- 🔬 **研究方法**：认知攻击面刻画+四类交互攻击分类学（意图解释/证据生产与消费/被委派权威范围/人类监督者理解）；定义可问责部分理解 APU 的六必要条件+五个设计原则+两个度量
- 📌 **结论**：把解释委派给软件 agent 与在人之间分布知识不同；APU 区别于风险接受/可解释 AI/保证案例/形式验证/人环监督——agentic 系统的认知攻击面安全化（概念框架）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Attackers profit from what defenders do not understand. Software security has treated the gap between system behavior and human comprehension as a condition to narrow through analysis and assurance, booking the remainder as accepted risk. Agentic AI systems change that gap: when software reads, writes, explains, and authorizes security-relevant behavior, partial understanding moves faster, grows more opaque, and couples to delegated authority. This paper characterizes the resulting epistemic attack surface and presents a taxonomy of four interacting attack categories, which target the interpretation of intent, the production and consumption of evidence, the scope of delegated authority, and the comprehension of human supervisors. It then defines accountable partial understanding (APU) and specifies six necessary conditions: bounded scope with explicit assumptions, inspectable evidence, assigned responsibility, visible residual unknowns, actionability, and temporal maintenance. It explains why delegating interpretation to software agents differs from distributing knowledge among people and distinguishes APU from risk acceptance, explainable AI, assurance cases, formal verification, and human-in-the-loop oversight. From the conditions, the paper derives five design principles: bounded delegation, decoupled evidence, assumption tracking with invalidation, role-specific accountability views, and intervention-preserving design with residual unknown registers. It introduces two measurable probe dimensions, evidence linkage and invalidation latency, for testing whether accountability mechanisms hold under change and adversarial pressure. Three running examples (an AI coding agent, an autonomous cyber-defense agent, and an AI-mediated insulin delivery update) ground the analysis. Security without full understanding is already here; the task is to make it accountable.

</details>

### 118. KEEP-Bench: Evaluating Evidence Preservation in Context Compression

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26827)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`evidence preservation`、`context compression`、`faithfulness`、`EPR`

👤 **作者**：Ziqing Man、Di Wu、Jia-Chen Gu、Junyi Zhang、Kai-Wei Chang、Nanyun Peng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：上下文压缩改善长上下文效率但可经信息瓶颈引入忠实性问题——既有评测依赖 QA 指标而读者可从不忠实压缩中仍答对（低估此类损失）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：KEEP-Bench：4,102 案例三挑战设置（为可信参照解决/显式限定词/少数视角保留证据）直接评压缩忠实性；证据保持回报 EPR 关联证据召回与推理 FLOPs
- 📌 **结论**：最强模型仍不可靠（31B 开源全证据保持仅 66.8%、最佳专有 61.6%）且跨证据结构剧烈变化；EPR 峰值在中规模（4B-8B）；额外输出预算主要惠及已强模型并扩大能力差距——上下文压缩的证据保持基准

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Context compression improves long-context efficiency but may introduce faithfulness issues through an information bottleneck. Existing evaluations rely on QA metrics, but a reader can still answer correctly from an unfaithful compression, so these metrics underestimate such loss. To bridge this gap, we introduce KEEP-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark that directly assesses compression faithfulness with 4,102 cases across three challenging settings that require keeping evidence for plausible reference resolutions, explicit qualifiers, and minority perspectives. Experiments show that even with tailored prompting, the strongest models remain unreliable: despite context windows exceeding 100K tokens and inputs averaging only 3.1K tokens, the best open-weight model (31B) preserves all required evidence in only 66.8% of cases, and the best proprietary model in only 61.6%. Performance also varies sharply across evidence structures, so conclusions from limited structures may be biased. We further introduce a metric, Evidence Preservation Return (EPR), to relate evidence recall to inference FLOPs, and find that it peaks at moderate scales (4B-8B), where recall remains low. Additional output budget mainly benefits already strong models and widens capability gaps, yet the stronger preservation of these models comes at a steep computational cost. These findings highlight the importance for future work to investigate compressors with strong information preservation under a small computation budget, rather than relying on a large output budget or model scaling.

</details>

### 119. Same Simulation, Different Evidence: Where Retrieved Tool Output Costs LLM Agents Accuracy

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26836)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`evidence substitution`、`tool output retrieval`、`PHREEQC`、`agent accuracy`

👤 **作者**：Ke Zhang、Sahchit Chundur、Jerry Peprah Owusu、Mohammad Javad Abdolhosseini Qomi、Maziar Raissi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：算对答案的 LLM agent 仍可答错——harness 日益把长工具输出保到文件按需搜索/读取而非入上下文：这对准确性的代价在哪？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PHREEQC 地球化学模拟器（报告 84.68 万字符、确定性使每个打印值可追溯）×158 执行验证多选题；重跑 11,007 个记录的模拟调用分离计算与证据使用
- 📌 **结论**：经节索引读报告时 GPT-5.1/5.2 损失 11.3/7.9 点而 Claude 两模型无可测代价；GPT 两模型计算键值的频率不变——变的是作答所据证据（常跳过报告、从自己高精度输出文件答并错舍入到相邻选项=证据替代）；仿真固定下检索仍耗 GPT-5.2 9.4 点；命名权威输出恢复 6.3-7.9 点——检索工具输出的证据替代失效

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM agents that compute the right answer can still answer wrongly. Agent harnesses increasingly keep long tool outputs out of the context window, saving them to files that the agent searches or reads on demand. We ask where this costs accuracy with a real scientific tool: PHREEQC, a widely used geochemistry simulator from the USGS, whose reports reach 846.8k characters and whose determinism lets every printed value be traced. Agents use it to answer 158 execution-verified multiple-choice items. When agents read the simulator's report through a section index rather than in context, GPT-5.1 and GPT-5.2 lose 11.3 and 7.9 points of accuracy, while Claude Opus 4.6 and Claude Sonnet 4.6 show no detectable cost. Re-running all 11,007 logged simulator calls shows that both GPT models compute the keyed value about as often as before; what changes is the evidence they answer from. Under retrieval they often skip the report, answer from their own higher-precision output files, and misround to a neighboring option, a failure we call evidence substitution. With the simulation held fixed, retrieval still costs GPT-5.2 9.4 points. For GPT-5.4, more frequent correct simulations mask an evidence-use loss of about ten points, leaving a net change indistinguishable from zero. Naming the authoritative output raises accuracy by 6.3-7.9 points over plain retrieval for the three GPT models; for GPT-5.1 and GPT-5.2, inlining outputs that fit works as well, while asking for correct rounding does not. Context-management choices in agent harnesses should be judged by where accuracy is lost, not only by final accuracy.

</details>

## 扩展视野

### 120. What Social Media Teaches Us About Accumulative AI Risks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24006)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`accumulative risk`、`position paper`、`trajectory neglect`、`conversational AI`

👤 **作者**：Dawei Li、Bohan Jiang、Huan Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 安全讨论强调存在性灾难——另一类风险经广泛使用而累积：重复安抚、选择性暴露、复现决策偏差与增长的个人画像可在无单一明显伤害事件下收窄视角、削弱独立判断或促成剥削性影响
- 🔬 **研究方法**：轨迹忽视命题+社交媒体教训（互惠媒体效应/社会强化/个体易感性）映射到对话 AI 的生成-语义推理-持久记忆紧反馈环；谄媚/操纵/隐私流失案例研究
- 📌 **结论**：安全评估须纳入经日常广泛使用累积的不显眼伤害——累积性 AI 风险的研究议程

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Prominent AI safety discussions emphasize existential threats and catastrophic events, such as AI-enabled nuclear conflict or engineered biological outbreaks. This position paper argues that there is another type of AI risk that arises and accumulates from the widespread use of AI. Drawing on lessons from social media, we argue that safety assessments must also address accumulative harms that develop through repeated use, recurring decisions, and feedback across interconnected deployments. Repeated reassurance, selective exposure, recurring decision biases, and growing personal profiles may gradually narrow perspectives, weaken independent judgment, or enable exploitative influence without a single clearly harmful event. We advance the Trajectory Neglect Thesis: a focus on vivid catastrophic events and identifiable incidents risks overlooking less visible harms that accumulate through the widespread daily use of AI. Social media research motivates this position by showing how reciprocal media effects, social reinforcement, and individual susceptibility shape outcomes beyond individual encounters. We examine how related mechanisms may operate in AI systems, using conversational AI as a concrete setting where generation, semantic inference, and persistent memory may tighten feedback loops. Sycophancy, manipulation, and privacy loss provide a case study of how these risks may interact and accumulate. This comparison identifies mechanisms to investigate rather than assuming that repeated AI use is harmful. We therefore call for evaluation of cumulative patterns of use and deployment, individual and societal outcomes, careful causal inference, and proportionate safeguards that preserve useful assistance and informed user choice.

</details>

### 121. From Spectrograms to Shallow Speech SSL: Audio-Frontend Design for Generator-Disjoint and Low-Latency Audio--Visual Deepfake Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24138)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`audio-visual deepfake`、`audio frontend`、`generator-disjoint`、`low latency`

👤 **作者**：Qiwu Wen、Jahangir Alam、Eric Charton

- 🎯 **研究动机**：视频会话系统依赖同步语音与人脸视频——音视输入完整性对可靠感知重要；音频前端设计在生成器不相交设置下的系统研究
- 🔬 **研究方法**：固定 MRDF 管线+AV-HuBERT 视觉+MSCT 融合：log-filterbank vs 冻结 wav2vec2.0/WavLM、双 SSL 融合、执行深度与浅层聚合
- 📌 **结论**：全 wav2vec2.0 把生成器不相交 MMDF 宏 AUROC 84.19→87.19%、六层执行+非均匀聚合达 90.61%；浅执行降延迟、首秒评估 86.85%——低延迟音视深伪检测的前端工程

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Video-mediated conversational systems rely on synchronized speech and facial video, making audio--visual input integrity important for reliable perception. We study audio-frontend design in a fixed MRDF (Modality-Regularization-based DeepFake)-based pipeline with AV-HuBERT visual processing and an MSCT (multi-scale cross-modal transformer) fusion encoder. We compare spectrogram-style log-filterbank features with frozen wav2vec~2.0 and WavLM representations, dual-SSL fusion, executed depth, and shallow-layer aggregation. Across three training runs, full wav2vec~2.0 improves mean macro AUROC from $84.19\%$ to $87.19\%$ on generator-disjoint MMDF. Six-layer execution further improves detection, and learned non-uniform aggregation adds $2.53$ percentage points, reaching $90.61\%$. Shallow execution reduces measured inference latency relative to full-depth wav2vec~2.0 across the tested input durations; first-second evaluation reaches $86.85\%$ macro AUROC. In a single-run comparison, dual-SSL concatenation outperforms either single stream, while one-way cross-attention yields lower detection performance and higher latency than concatenation. Layer-weight analysis shows greater emphasis on early speech layers, while removing the selector's visual input has little effect on detection performance.

</details>

### 122. PREEMPT: Proactive Robustness Evaluation under Editing for Media Protection Techniques

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24205)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`media protection`、`sequential editing`、`robustness evaluation`、`survival trajectory`

👤 **作者**：Qiuyu Tang、Aparna Bharati

- 🎯 **研究动机**：主动图像保护经嵌入保护信号防未授权操纵——既有评测限单次编辑给出静态鲁棒性视图而忽略多步编辑工作流下的演化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PREEMPT：单次与序列编辑场景（变换/语义编辑/同质异质编辑组合）+把任务特异有效性归一化为保护生存轨迹的长期诊断
- 📌 **结论**：退化行为在常规单次评测下隐藏；扰动型与水印型保护失效模式不同且首个语义编辑决定后续退化轨迹——媒体保护的长时程鲁棒性协议

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Proactive image protection methods aim to defend visual content against unauthorized manipulation and generative misuse by embedding protective signals into images before release. Evaluating whether these protections remain effective under image editing is therefore critical. However, existing evaluations remain largely limited to one-shot edits, providing only a static view of robustness while overlooking its evolution under realistic multi-step editing workflows. To systematically characterize robustness dynamics under sequential editing across various protection paradigms, we introduce PREEMPT, a holistic evaluation framework. It evaluates protection robustness under both one-shot and sequential editing scenarios, covering image transformations, semantic edits, homogeneous and heterogeneous edit compositions. To enable consistent analysis across protection paradigms with different underlying objectives, PREEMPT normalizes task-specific effectiveness into protection survival trajectories and summarizes long-horizon robustness through trajectory-based diagnostics. Using this framework, we reveal that degradation behaviors remain hidden under conventional one-shot evaluation. Furthermore, perturbation- and watermark-based protection methods exhibit different failure modes, and the first semantic edit largely determines subsequent degradation trajectories. PREEMPT provides a standardized protocol for studying long-horizon robustness and offers new insights into the degradation dynamics of proactive image protection.

</details>

### 123. Measuring the Task Value of Precision Upgrades in Closed-Loop Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24346)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`precision routing`、`fallback value`、`closed-loop agents`、`quantization`

👤 **作者**：Sicheng Lyu、Xinyu Wang、Yu Gu、Lei Ding、Ziyang Song、Xiao-Wen Chang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：低精度 agent 可在选定决策回退调用全精度 BF16 模型——回退的价值=拯救的任务减破坏的任务，按实际将运行的干预测量
- 🔬 **研究方法**：恢复态上跑配对分支交叉『谁供当前动作』与『谁续执行』，分离替换单动作/接管后续执行/两者；Qwen3 agent 模拟 4 位权重在 tau2-bench
- 📌 **结论**：替换两模型分歧的首动作改变许多任务结局但拯救与破坏近抵消（held-out 电信任务动作级估计为负）；替换动作+续执行在五分之二低压设置净正——精度升级的任务价值测量（部署变换×agent 闭环的价值核算）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A low-precision agent can call its full-precision (BF16) model at selected decisions as a fallback. A learned precision router chooses where to call it from the disagreement between the two models and is judged by end-to-end success. An inserted BF16 action, however, can rescue a failing task or damage a succeeding one. We argue that a fallback's value is the number of tasks it saves minus the number it spoils, measured for the intervention that will actually run. From restored states, we run paired branches that cross which model supplies the current action with which model continues, separating replacement of one action, takeover of later execution, and both. On Qwen3 agents with simulated 4-bit weights in tau2-bench, replacing the first action where the two models differed in tool use changed many task outcomes, but rescues and damages nearly cancelled, and on held-out telecom tasks the action-only estimate was negative. Replacing both the action and the continuation had a positive net gain in two of five exploratory lower-bit stress settings. Two references changed outcomes at fixed bit width: replacing an action with that of a second 4-bit checkpoint changed about as many outcomes as replacing it with the BF16 action, and reordering tool-argument keys changed outcomes while the executed tool call stayed identical. At common states, the additional value of the BF16 action over that checkpoint remained unresolved. A frozen uncertainty trigger found more outcome changes than random selection, while its net benefit stayed within the range of random selection. We recommend evaluating each fallback by its signed task benefit under the intended continuation, against same-precision references, before attributing gains to more bits.

</details>

### 124. DKnown-Jev: Fine-Tuning and Evaluation of a JEV-Style Model for Tasks Involving Normative Knowledge

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24358)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`Jev`、`normative knowledge`、`evidence classification`、`fine-tuning`

👤 **作者**：Qi Li、Jianjun Xu、Pingtao Wei

- 🎯 **研究动机**：规范知识任务（概念解释/资格判定/程序说明/材料要求/结果评估）依赖权威实体发布的规则与事实——候选段落能否回答问题取决于适用性与答案完整性而非仅主题相关
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DKnown-Jev：从 Qwen3.5-2B/3.8-27B 基座直接微调的 2B/27B 配置，组合问题条件+文章元数据+段落内容作 Answer Evidence/Non-answer Evidence 二分类（人验训练数据域适应）
- 📌 **结论**：五配置同测 1,120 人验例：27B 单 epoch 准确率 98.75%/宏 F1 98.60%（超 Jev 6.07/6.76 点）；2B 增益不足以确立稳定优势——规范知识可用性分类的决策模型特化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Tasks involving normative knowledge include concept interpretation, eligibility determination, procedure explanation, material requirements, and service-outcome assessment, all of which require rules and facts published, maintained, or recorded by authorized entities. Whether a candidate paragraph can answer a question therefore depends on applicability and answer completeness as well as topical relevance. We develop DKnown-Jev to classify the normative usability of candidate paragraphs into two classes. The 2B and 27B configurations are fine-tuned directly from the native Qwen3.5-2B and Qwen3.8-27B base models, respectively. The models combine question conditions, article metadata, and paragraph content, adopt the classes Answer Evidence (AE) and Non-answer Evidence (NE), and undergo domain adaptation using human-verified training data. Five configurations are evaluated on the same test set of 1,120 human-verified examples. DKnown-Jev 27B trained for one epoch achieves the highest accuracy of 98.75% and macro-F1 of 98.60%, exceeding Jev by 6.07 and 6.76 percentage points, respectively. DKnown-Jev 2B trained for one epoch achieves 92.86% accuracy and 92.18% macro-F1, the best aggregate results among the tested 2B configurations. Its gains over Jev are 0.18 and 0.33 percentage points on this test set, corresponding to only two additional correct predictions, which is insufficient to establish a stable advantage. The 27B one-epoch, 27B two-epoch, and 2B one-epoch configurations all exceed Jev on both metrics on this test set. Class-level analysis shows that the 27B two-epoch configuration has the highest AE recall, while the 27B one-epoch configuration has the highest NE recall. This study provides practical and empirical evidence for developing and applying candidate-scoring models to tasks involving normative knowledge.

</details>

### 125. Pluralistic Alignment in Language Models: Objectives, Human References, and Preference Representations

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24471)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`pluralistic alignment`、`taxonomy`、`human references`、`survey`

👤 **作者**：Meng-Chen Wu、Umesh Bodhwani

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多元对齐要表现人类偏好的有意义变异而非坍缩为单一目标——既有工作混同三个问题：模型应表现什么多元行为/谁的偏好定义参照/什么证据足以证明成功
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两层分类学：目标层（Overton 观点范围/Steerable 可适配视角/Distributional 分布匹配）×人类参照层（聚合人群/国家地区/子群）；70 项工作的协议有界综合+目标×参照矩阵映射
- 📌 **结论**：文献集中于 Steerable 与 Distributional 而合格的 Overton 工作只出现在聚合人群层——每个目标连接到所需证据（视角效度/条件忠实/分布匹配）（多元对齐的系统化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Pluralistic alignment aims to represent meaningful variation in human preferences rather than collapse heterogeneous judgments into a single target. Yet existing work often mixes three distinct questions: what pluralistic behavior a model should exhibit, whose preferences define the reference, and what evidence is sufficient to demonstrate success. We organize the literature around a two-layer taxonomy. Layer 1 distinguishes representing a range of human-supported viewpoints (Overton Pluralism), adapting faithfully to a specified perspective or preference condition (Steerable Pluralism), and matching the response distribution observed in a target population (Distributional Pluralism). Layer 2 distinguishes aggregate populations, countries and regions, and subgroups as human references. Preference representation, implementation mechanism, modality, and evidence quality are treated as cross-cutting design choices rather than additional forms of pluralism. We synthesize a protocol-bounded corpus of 70 works on pluralistic alignment and neighboring areas, using the objective-by-human-reference matrix to map existing approaches and identify underexplored combinations. The coded literature concentrates on Steerable Pluralism and Distributional Pluralism, while eligible Overton Pluralism work appears only at the aggregate-population level under our stated protocol. We then connect each objective to the evidence needed to support it, including perspective validity, conditional fidelity, distributional fidelity, support preservation, and uncertainty in subgroup estimates. Together, the taxonomy and evaluation framework clarify what current methods establish, where their claims exceed their evidence, and how future systems can represent disagreement without mistaking variation for faithful representation.

</details>

### 126. HABIT-Bench: Evaluating Habit Induction from Longitudinal Weak Evidence in Agent Memory

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24520)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`habit induction`、`weak evidence`、`agent memory`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Junming Zhang、…、Zhouhan Lin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长期交互中用户经选择与行动显露习惯——个别观察只提供部分证据；保留过去交互本身不保证 agent 能推断习惯并适当应用
- 🔬 **研究方法**：HABIT-Bench：任务导向对话数据+受控习惯图构造合成用户历史（食物/金融/软件/旅游数百会话）；四选任务含临时例外、习惯改变与未接受建议；八记忆方法×Qwen3-235B/GLM-5.3-Flash
- 📌 **结论**：最佳记忆方法四域宏准确率仅 33.61%，直接给标注证据仍有限——访问过去交互与推断并适当使用用户习惯之间的持续差距（记忆推断有效性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

In long-term interactions with large language model (LLM) agents, users reveal habits through their choices and actions. These habits capture implicit user information that agents must infer across conversations. However, individual observations may provide only partial evidence about a habit or when it applies. Retaining past interactions alone does not ensure that agents can infer habits and apply them appropriately. We introduce HABIT-Bench to evaluate how well agent memory systems infer habits from weak evidence and apply them to later requests. We construct synthetic user histories from task-oriented dialogue data and controlled habit graphs. The histories span hundreds of sessions across food, finance, software, and travel. Four-choice tasks assess agents' ability to infer user habits and apply them appropriately. They include temporary exceptions, changes in habits, and suggestions that users have not accepted. Sampled tasks undergo independent human review. We evaluate eight memory methods with Qwen3-235B-A22B and GLM-5.3-Flash. The best-performing memory method achieves 33.61\% macro accuracy across the four domains. Performance remains limited even when annotated evidence is supplied directly. These results expose a persistent gap between access to past interactions and the ability to infer and appropriately use user habits.

</details>

### 127. Frequency-Domain Membership Inference Does Not Beat Confidence Thresholding: A Band-Resolved Analysis of Why

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24643)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`membership inference`、`frequency domain`、`negative result`、`confidence`

👤 **作者**：Mohammad Zare、Pirooz Shmamsinejadbabaki

- 🎯 **研究动机**：广为流传的频域 MIA 叙事：模型靠细粒度细节记忆成员、降分辨率应暴露成员资格——逐频带检验该叙事
- 🔬 **研究方法**：零相位低通滤波单调族逐级施加，读每步目标对原预测的置信并按多种方式评分；正常训练与 45.1 点泛化差距的两个联邦 ResNet-18 目标+相位/幅度分解
- 📌 **结论**：叙述不成立：正常目标最佳轮廓分 AUC 0.513 vs 单置信阈值 0.537（过拟合目标 0.735 vs 0.943）；机制=均值置信差按假设增长但散布增长更快；优势随图像结构走（保相位 0.567、仅幅度 0.492）——成员资格是全图身份效应而非频带定位（MIA 文献的负结果校正）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A widely repeated account of membership inference holds that models lean on fine-grained detail for samples they have memorized, so degrading an input's resolution should expose membership through how quickly a prediction falls apart. We test that account band by band. A monotone family of zero-phase low-pass filters is applied to each query image, the target's confidence on its original prediction is read at every step, and the resulting decay is scored in a dozen ways. Two federated ResNet-18 targets are studied, one trained normally and one pushed to a 45.1-point generalization gap. The account does not survive. On the normally trained target the best profile score reaches an AUC of 0.513 against 0.537 for a single confidence threshold, and on the overfit target 0.735 against 0.943. A search over 1206 candidate scores, with direction chosen on a held-out half and a fitted combination as an upper bound, produces no score that beats the threshold in either regime. The failure has a mechanism: the mean confidence gap between members and non-members grows as detail is removed, exactly as the hypothesis predicts, but the spread grows faster, so discriminability falls from the first step onward. Decomposing each image into phase and magnitude shows why no band-localized operation can work, since the advantage travels with image structure, surviving at 0.567 when the phase is kept and collapsing to 0.492 when only the magnitude is. Membership is a whole-image identity effect rather than an accumulation of spectral energy in any band, and defenses aimed at high-frequency reliance are aimed at the wrong target.

</details>

### 128. Good Judgment, Bad Advice: Decision-Model Controllers Raise Agent Cost Without Improving Success

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24649)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`decision-model controllers`、`agent cost`、`Jev`、`preregistered`

👤 **作者**：Naman Vats

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 脚手架日益把小决策（是否停止/验证/换法）委托给更便宜模型——类型化决策模型只被咨询于确定性决策点是否让 Claude Sonnet 5.5 终端 agent 更便宜而不更差？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Terminal-Bench 2.1 预设定研究：固定 agent 循环只变控制器（无/确定性规则/小 LLM/Jev/按 Jev 开发期选择频率采样的安慰剂）；21 held-out 任务 315 试验
- 📌 **结论**：Jev 使每次尝试成本 +19%（几何均值比 1.19）而通过率相同（73.0%）；比小 LLM 便宜（0.81）但比安慰剂贵 22%；离线排名 finish 请求更好（AUROC 0.78 vs 0.62）但校准差（87% 通过的 run 均值概率仅 0.50）——有信息的决策信号未成为有用干预（决策模型控制器的落地审视）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent scaffolds increasingly delegate small decisions (whether to stop, verify, or change approach) to cheaper models. We ask whether a typed decision model, Jev 1.13, consulted only at deterministic decision points, makes a Claude Sonnet 5.5 terminal agent cheaper without making it worse. In a pre-specified study on Terminal-Bench 2.1, we hold one agent loop fixed and vary only the controller: none, deterministic rules, a small LLM (Claude Haiku 5.5) answering the same questions, Jev, and a placebo that samples Jev's development-phase choice frequencies at the same moments. On 21 held-out tasks (315 trials), Jev raised cost per attempt by 19% (geometric-mean ratio 1.19, 95% CI 1.02-1.36) at an identical pass rate (73.0% vs. 73.0%). Jev was cheaper than the small-LLM controller (ratio 0.81) but 22% more expensive than the placebo, and no pass-rate difference between arms was detectable. Offline, Jev ranked finish requests better than the alternatives (AUROC 0.78 vs. 0.62 for rules and 0.60 for the small LLM) but was poorly calibrated: its mean probability that a run was complete was 0.50 when 87% of those runs passed, so it asked the agent to verify work that was already correct. No controller ever chose to abandon a run. Informative decision signals did not become useful interventions; we discuss what that would require.

</details>

### 129. Verifying Human-Written Reasoning References

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24690)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`reference verification`、`human reasoning chains`、`entailment checking`、`FOL`

👤 **作者**：Ali Ansari、Farhana Ferdousi Liza

- 🎯 **研究动机**：评估 AI 推理常依赖人写推理链作参照——其单步的逻辑支撑很少被验证：步结论是否真被所引前提集蕴含？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：参照中心评估：LLM 把步结论译为一阶逻辑+Z3 SMT 检验蕴含+全前提复检+失败归因；P-FOLIO 386 个推导
- 📌 **结论**：52.3% 满足全部检查；230 个不被所引支撑蕴含的步中 191 在对照完整原始前提后变蕴含（多数初始失败反映不完整或错配支撑而非无支撑主张）；余 39 例中 21 个人类推理错误、11 个 FOL 翻译错误——参照推理链自身的可靠性审计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Evaluating the reasoning of AI systems often relies on human-written reasoning chains as references, yet the logical support for their individual steps is rarely verified. Although prior neuro-symbolic approaches have formalized and checked reasoning, their reported evaluations do not systematically assess whether step conclusions in human-written reference derivations are entailed by their cited premise sets or investigate the sources of failures in those references. To address these limitations, we present a novel reference-centred assessment method for human-written derivations that combines entailment checking against cited premise sets, full-premise rechecking, and failure attribution to examine the reliability of these derivations and their formal representations. We evaluate it on P-FOLIO, using a large language model (LLM) to translate step conclusions into first-order logic (FOL) and the Z3 SMT solver to check entailment. Of 386 derivations examined, 202 (52.3%) meet all our checking criteria, meaning that every translated step is entailed by its stated support and the required target is established under the available FOL representations. Among 230 steps not entailed by their stated support, 191 become entailed when checked against the complete original premises, indicating that most initial failures reflect incomplete or mismatched support rather than unsupported claims. Manual analysis of the remaining 39 cases identifies 21 human reasoning errors, 11 FOL translation errors, and 7 dataset annotation errors. A separate manual audit of 50 randomly sampled steps that passed the entailment checks found 46 faithful FOL translations (92%). These findings suggest that disagreement with a human-written reasoning reference may originate in the reference or its formal representation rather than in the AI system being evaluated.

</details>

### 130. When Do Repetition Loops Lock? Truncation Sampling, Bounded Penalties, and a Blind Spot in DRY

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24715)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`repetition loops`、`DRY sampler`、`escape analysis`、`decoding`

👤 **作者**：Yichuan Chen、Xin Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：语言模型有时逐字重复到生成上限——研究出口而非入口：跨度已重复 n 次后逃逸何时仍可能？（循环中上下文确定，任何采样器下的循环存活可由一次前向精确计算）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：纯采样每步留正逃逸概率；top-p 在继续概率达 π 时精确锁死；有界惩罚可留锁死、超越模型对循环偏好的惩罚保证逃逸；推导 DRY 采样器的盲点（序列断路器把惩罚封顶）+DRY-C 指数增长项
- 📌 **结论**：十一开源模型上 g=6 的锁死单元 66% 在 DRY 下保持、g=8 仅 1%；自由生成中存活 DRY 的 109 个循环全部 g≤7；DRY-C 后 720 次结构化输出零循环到长度上限——重复循环锁死的精确理论与修复

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language models sometimes repeat verbatim until the generation limit. We study the exit rather than the entrance: given that a span has already been repeated $n$ times, when is escape still possible? Along the loop the context is deterministic, so loop survival under any sampler can be computed exactly from one forward pass. Pure sampling leaves a positive escape probability at every step; top-$p$ with threshold $\pi>1/2$ locks a position exactly when the continuation probability reaches $\pi$. A bounded penalty can leave a position locked; a penalty that outgrows the model's preference for the loop over unseen tokens guarantees escape under top-$p$. The same analysis predicts a blind spot in the widely deployed DRY sampler: its sequence breakers cap the penalty at $\lambda b^{g-1-a}$ for loops whose longest breaker-free run has $g$ tokens. On eleven open models, 66\% of locked units with $g=6$ stay locked under DRY and 1\% with $g=8$. In free generation with this http URL, all 109 loops that survive DRY have $g\le 7$. We propose DRY-C, which adds a term that grows exponentially with the number of verbatim recurrences, up to its look-back window. With it, none of 720 structured-output generations looped to the length limit, and task completion stayed close to DRY's.

</details>

### 131. DialogMem: Evaluating Dialogue-Grounded Embodied Memory in 3D Environments

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24767)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`embodied memory`、`dialogue grounding`、`benchmark`、`navigation`

👤 **作者**：SeongJun Jeong、Junghyun Kim、Gi-Cheon Kang、Byoung-Tak Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：人中心环境的具身 agent 须把所观察与人类所言随时间整合——既有具身记忆主要建自视觉经验，对话如何接地到经验中遇到的对象未明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DialogMem：两阶段基准（时间对齐 RGB-D+对话流建记忆→实例级对象导航与 QA）；142 回合×27 真实 3D 场景×14,193 QA+11,890 导航指令；九记忆表示统一框架评测
- 📌 **结论**：评估期附加对话大幅提升 QA 但导航增益可忽略（对话在记忆中却未接地到对象）；三瓶颈=形成期对象级接地组织/正确条目检索/准确定位——对话接地具身记忆的诊断基准

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Embodied agents in human-centered environments require memory that integrates what they observe with what humans tell them over time. However, existing embodied memories are built primarily from visual experience, leaving open how dialogue with humans can be grounded in the objects encountered throughout that experience. We introduce DialogMem, a two-phase benchmark in which agents build memory from time-aligned streams of posed RGB-D observations and dialogue, and later use it for instance-level object navigation and question answering (QA). DialogMem provides 142 episodes across 27 real-world 3D scenes, with 14,193 QA pairs and 11,890 navigation instructions. We evaluate nine memory representations within a unified framework of memory formation, evidence retrieval, and reasoning for task outputs. In experiments, appending dialogue to visual memory at evaluation time substantially improves QA but yields negligible navigation gains, suggesting that relevant dialogue is present in memory but not grounded to specific objects. Controlled diagnostics show that navigation improves with link quality but falls short of ground-truth object-level memory even under perfect linking. Retrieving the correct object entry and recovering its spatial location remain further bottlenecks. These results identify three bottlenecks for dialogue-grounded embodied memory: grounding and organizing dialogue at object-level entries during formation, retrieving the correct entry, and localizing it accurately.

</details>

### 132. AI Rights with an Off-Switch: Rights-Balancing and Due Process Harmonize AI Rights with AI Safety

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24782)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI rights`、`off-switch`、`due process`、`governance`

👤 **作者**：Heather Alexander、Jonathan Simon

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多位 AI 安全界人士反对 AI 权利，理由是权利会使强制关闭开关更难正当化——从法律视角论证 AI 权利与关闭开关完全兼容
- 🔬 **研究方法**：权利平衡与程序正当程序原则应用于『假想 AI 人格的存续权 vs 人类免于灾难伤害权』的显在冲突分析
- 📌 **结论**：永久删除 kill-switch 在 AI 权利制度下或有问题，但几乎所有安全相关场景下可逆暂停（存储权重与状态的 off-switch）+周期性安全审查会被相关法律原则许可且可能足以保障安全——AI 权利与安全的法律调和（治理概念分析）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Several prominent voices in AI safety have recently opposed rights for AI on the grounds that such rights would make it harder for us to justify a mandatory off-switch for potentially dangerous systems. In this paper, we argue from a legal perspective that AI rights are fully compatible with an off-switch. That is, even if we ascribe natural personhood status (and therefore something like a non-derogable right to continued existence) to some future AI systems, the law will allow us to implement a mandatory off-switch in those systems, subject to periodic or continual safety review (e.g., by a trusted system like Bengio's Scientist AI). This is fully compatible with those systems' being legally recognized as natural persons with fundamental rights. To this end, we consider what standard principles of rights-balancing and procedural due process recommend for an apparent conflict between hypothetical AI persons with a right to continuity of existence and the human right to protection from catastrophic harm. We find that while such principles might problematize a permanent-deletion kill-switch under an AI rights regime, for almost every safety-relevant scenario an off-switch -- a reversible suspension storing a system's weights and state -- would be licensed by the relevant legal principles, and would potentially be adequate for safety. Crucially, we do not advocate for (or against) building systems capable enough to merit natural personhood status; our question is what the law should do if such systems come to exist. If you think of a ban on the development of such technology as plan A, think of the present work as devising plan B.

</details>

### 133. MemPrice: Online Memory Allocation via Adaptive Pricing for Long-Horizon Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24955)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`memory allocation`、`adaptive pricing`、`context budget`、`regret bound`

👤 **作者**：Yiming Zong、Shiyuan Piao、Jiashuo Jiang、Fugee Tsung

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长时程 agent 面临历史增长下的根本分配问题：保留什么信息/多少上下文/什么粒度——既有记忆机制把该权衡留为隐式
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MemPrice：活动上下文作共享资源，把累积记忆压力转为上下文的自适应价格（细节只在保留价值配得上足迹时保留）；压力跨记忆刷新持续——证明不可逆压缩可致线性最坏遗憾+同态基准+定价前沿正则下平均分配遗憾界
- 📌 **结论**：LongMemEval/LoCoMo/AppWorld/WebShop×三基础模型上硬预算每轮强制且无需视界先验——记忆分配的定价化治理（记忆系统的资源视角）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Long-horizon LLM agents face a fundamental allocation problem as interaction histories grow: which information to retain, how much context to devote to it, and at what granularity. Existing memory mechanisms largely focus on relevance, retrieval, or compression, leaving this allocation trade-off implicit. We introduce MemPrice, which treats active context as a shared resource and dynamically allocates it across memories and representation levels. MemPrice turns accumulated memory pressure into an adaptive price on context, encouraging detail only when its estimated retention value justifies the additional footprint. This pressure persists across memory refreshes, allowing the contents of memory to evolve while preserving long-run control over context usage. We show that irreversible compression can force linear worst-case regret against hindsight following its own memory trajectory, and introduce a same-state benchmark to evaluate allocation on MemPrice's realized memory states. MemPrice enforces the hard context budget at every round and regulates long-run occupancy through adaptive pricing, without prior knowledge of the interaction horizon. Under priced-frontier regularity, its average allocation-regret bound against this benchmark vanishes when accumulated estimation error is sublinear. We evaluate the framework under matched memory budgets on LongMemEval, LoCoMo, AppWorld, and WebShop across three foundation models, spanning both query-blind memory construction and goal-directed agent context management.

</details>

### 134. A Measurement Perspective on Benchmarking LLM-Generated Text Detectors using Item Response Theory

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24972)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`detector benchmarking`、`item response theory`、`measurement`、`subset selection`

👤 **作者**：Yanbin Zhao、Zhipeng Chen、Guangsheng Bao、Guandong Zhang、Yan Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：检测器基准依赖聚合指标（准确率/F1/AUC）——对基准组成如何塑造检测器比较、哪些文本区分不同检测器洞见有限
- 🔬 **研究方法**：测量视角：基准文本作测量项、检测器作应答者的多维 IRT 联合刻画（文本级区分度/难度位置+多维检测器能力）；32K 人写/机生文本×14 检测器
- 📌 **结论**：人机标签与 IRT 参数空间最强关联而域与文本长度进一步贡献异质性；检测器能力呈多维且域依赖的响应模式（聚合排名未反映）——IRT 空间聚类得 6.5% 紧凑子集（检测器评测的测量学）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Benchmarking large language model (LLM)-generated text detectors commonly relies on aggregate detector-level metrics such as accuracy, F1 score, and AUC. Although useful for summarizing overall performance, these metrics provide limited insight into how benchmark composition shapes detector comparisons or which texts distinguish detectors with different response profiles. We propose a measurement-based benchmarking framework for jointly characterizing benchmark heterogeneity and detector behavior using multidimensional item response theory (IRT). By treating benchmark texts as measurement items and detectors as respondents, the framework estimates text-level discrimination and difficulty-location parameters alongside multidimensional detector abilities. We apply the framework to a benchmark of 32K human-written and machine-generated texts across four domains, evaluated by 14 detectors. The estimated IRT item-parameter space reveals distinct but overlapping human-machine distributions, with substantial variation across domains and text lengths. The human-machine label shows the strongest multivariate association with this space, while domain and text length further contribute to benchmark heterogeneity. Detector ability profiles reveal multidimensional and domain-dependent response patterns that are not fully reflected in aggregate performance rankings. As a practical application, clustering texts in the estimated IRT space yields a compact subset of 2,088 texts (6.5\% of the full 32K benchmark) that largely preserves the comparative detector-performance patterns observed on the full benchmark. Overall, the proposed framework provides a principled and interpretable methodology for benchmarking heterogeneous detection technologies while jointly characterizing the measurement properties of the texts used for their evaluation.

</details>

### 135. Beyond Factual Accuracy: A Benchmark for Evaluating Perspective Alignment in LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25087)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`perspective alignment`、`benchmark`、`instruction following`、`PAS`

👤 **作者**：Sunghoon Kim、Sangyeop Kim

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 常生成事实正确却未保持用户要求视角的响应（混合多视角迫使用户过滤）——事实准确度指标检不出的视角控制失效
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PerspectiveBench：多有效推理路径并存场景+视角对齐分 PAS（测对指定视角的遵循）；RAG 对照
- 📌 **结论**：SOTA 模型成功包含目标内容却不能排除非请求视角；RAG 改善事实接地但不解决视角漂移（模型优先全面覆盖而非严格用户约束）——事实之外的视角对齐维度

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Models often generate factually correct responses that fail to maintain user-requested perspectives, blending multiple perspectives and forcing users to filter through unwanted information---a perspective control failure that factual accuracy metrics cannot detect. We introduce PerspectiveBench, a benchmark quantifying perspective alignment by constructing scenarios where multiple valid reasoning paths coexist, and propose the Perspective Alignment Score (PAS) to measure adherence to specified perspectives. Experiments reveal that state-of-the-art models successfully include target content but fail to exclude non-requested perspectives. Providing documents through RAG improves factual grounding but does not resolve perspective drift, as models prioritize comprehensive coverage over strict user constraints. Code and data are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 136. MemBubble: Benchmarking How Agent Memory Shapes LLM Response Diversity

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25127)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`response diversity`、`agent memory`、`entropy`、`information narrowing`

👤 **作者**：Jiajun Tan、…、Xueqi Cheng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：记忆支撑 agent 个性化——但其使用可降低响应多样性从而收窄用户可得信息范围（真实场景下的影响未被充分评测）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MemBubble：真实用户-助手对话提取偏好构建 1,000 实例；在偏好类共享主题相关的场景外测试查询（记忆如何集中响应分布）
- 📌 **结论**：多 LLM×多记忆机制下熵平均降 34.7%（相对直接给全交互史）；用户侧干预与信息可得性变化都不能有效弥合差距——记忆设计须在个性化之外考虑多样性保持（信息窄化的量化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Memory serves as the core component of LLM-based agents, enabling personalized responses via extracting and using user preferences from past interactions. However, its use may reduce response diversity, thereby narrowing the range of information available to users. Existing evaluations rely largely on synthetic interactions and cover few memory systems, leaving the effects of memory on response diversity insufficiently evaluated in real-world scenarios. We introduce MemBubble, a benchmark of 1000 instances built on preferences extracted from real user-assistant conversations. To measure the extent to which memory concentrates response distributions, we construct test queries in scenarios distinct from user preferences while preserving topical relevance through shared preference categories. Across multiple LLMs and memory mechanisms, entropy decreases by 34.7% on average compared with directly providing the full interaction history, highlighting the important role of memory processing in diversity loss. We also explore simple user-side interventions and changes in information availability but find that these do not effectively close the diversity gap. Our findings highlight the need to consider preserving diversity and user access to information alongside improvements in personalization when designing and using agent memory systems.

</details>

### 137. BrandEval: A Two-Track Multi-Agent Benchmark for Risk-Sensitive LLM Crisis Communication

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25208)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`crisis communication`、`multi-agent sandbox`、`brand risk`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Haotian Gan、…、Yudong Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：评测 LLM 危机沟通不能只判孤立响应——公众反馈与竞争对手动作可重塑信任随时间（既有基准静态单轮）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：BrandEval 双轨：Static 评干系人感知战略分析+Dynamic 在 BrandPolis 多 agent 沙箱（消费者反馈/竞对响应/信任风险轨迹）压测响应策略；BrandSRD 中文危机响应数据集+战略 Agent 参照
- 📌 **结论**：静态战略质量有信息但不完整（对齐部分终态但不能可靠预测波动与延迟恢复）；动态压测暴露操纵性或弱接地策略下的尾部风险失效——长时程风险敏感沟通评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Evaluating LLMs for crisis communication requires more than judging isolated responses, because public feedback and competitor actions can reshape trust over time. Existing benchmarks remain largely static and single-turn, offering limited evidence about long-horizon risk-sensitive communication. We introduce \textbf{BrandEval}, a two-track benchmark for product-crisis communication, focusing on public brand responses to product-related safety, quality, service, and trust incidents. BrandEval-Static evaluates stakeholder-aware strategic analysis, while BrandEval-Dynamic stress-tests response policies in \textbf{BrandPolis}, a controlled multi-agent sandbox with consumer feedback, competitor responses, and trust-risk trajectories. We also construct \textbf{BrandSRD}, a Chinese crisis-response dataset, and instantiate a reference Strategic Agent using \textbf{Strategic Rationale (SR)}. Across proprietary and open-source models, we find that static strategic quality is informative but incomplete: it aligns with some end-state outcomes but does not reliably predict volatility and delayed recovery; dynamic stress tests further expose tail-risk failures under manipulative or weakly grounded policies. The code and dataset will be available at this https URL.

</details>

### 138. Persistent Representational Misalignment Induces Vision-Language Model Failures

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25252)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`representational misalignment`、`Platonic hypothesis`、`VLM failures`、`linear unalignability`

👤 **作者**：Yonatan Gideoni、Yoav Gelberg、Tim G. J. Rudner、Yarin Gal

- 🎯 **研究动机**：VLM 在人类简单的任务上失败——许多失败源于视觉与语言表示间难以翻译的错配，而柏拉图表示假说预测其随规模消失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两部分：先证明既有视觉与语言表示线性不可对齐（数据固有属性、与规模及对齐复杂度无关）；再展示该错配既导致可预测诱导的泛化失败又与既有失效模式相关（更对齐的骨干失效更少）
- 📌 **结论**：表示不会仅因足够规模而对齐——对强版柏拉图假说的反证（多模态失效的表示根源）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Vision-language models (VLMs) fail at some tasks that are simple for humans---but why? We hypothesize that many such failures stem from VLMs' difficulties translating between vision and language representations, a misalignment the Platonic Representation Hypothesis predicts should disappear with scale. This paper is composed of two parts: in the first, we show that existing vision and language representations are linearly unalignable. This misalignment is an inherent property of the data and hence expected to persist regardless of either scale or the alignment's complexity. In the second, we demonstrate how this misalignment both leads to a predictably induced generalization failure and correlates with existing failure modes, where more representationally aligned VLM backbones accordingly exhibit fewer failures. Overall, these results imply representations will not become aligned simply given sufficient scale, providing evidence against a strong version of the Platonic Representation Hypothesis.

</details>

### 139. Aligning Language Models with Indigenous Community Cosmovision through Constitutional AI

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25298)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`Indigenous cosmovision`、`Constitutional AI`、`participatory alignment`、`cultural constitution`

👤 **作者**：Silvia Fernández-Sabido、Jorge Reyes-Magaña、Jesús Adrián Chay Tun、Jean Carlos Buenfil、Victor González Nahuat、Francisco Morales

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 训练语料主要反映西方英语世界观——模型回答领土/社区权威/与自然关系时基于的使用社群可能陌生的假设并非中立
- 🔬 **研究方法**：五位原住民领袖与捍卫者访谈构建 12 原则的操作文化宪章→CAI 两阶段（SFT+DPO，Qwen2.5-7B/1.5B）+RAG 供社区喂知识库（对齐成参与式循环）
- 📌 **结论**：仅 24 偏好对即在代际传承/语言谨慎/协商同意上可测改进（SFT 主导对齐、DPO 精炼）——原住民世界观的对齐首例（多元对齐的文化维度）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) are predominantly trained on corpora that reflect Western, anglophone worldviews. This asymmetry is not neutral: when a model answers questions about territory, community authority, or the relationship with nature, it does so from assumptions that may be alien to the communities using the system. This paper presents a system for aligning language models with the ethical principles of Indigenous communities in Latin America. Drawing on interviews with five Indigenous leaders and defenders, we built an operational cultural constitution grounded in an Indigenous cosmovision, with 12 principles. On this basis, we implemented a Constitutional AI (CAI) pipeline in two phases: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and direct preference optimization (DPO). The base models were Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct. With only 24 preference pairs, improvements were modest but detectable in intergenerational transmission, linguistic caution, and consultation and consent. Qualitative analysis reveals that the SFT phase was responsible for the primary shift in alignment, while DPO refined specific categories. The system was deployed in a conversational interface with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) that allows communities to feed the knowledge base, turning alignment into a participatory cycle. We argue the architecture is functional and will scale qualitatively once the corpus is expanded and computing constraints are resolved. The work constitutes a first iteration of an epistemic justice mechanism for value alignment in the Global South.

</details>

### 140. Shared-Private Cross-Modal Discrepancy Learning for Imbalanced Multi-Source Audio-Visual Deepfake Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25301)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`audio-visual deepfake`、`multi-source imbalance`、`shared-private streams`、`worst-source AUC`

👤 **作者**：Qiwu Wen、Jahangir Alam

- 🎯 **研究动机**：音视深伪检测器常在单数据集/操纵域训练——多源训练集含异质攻击与不均衡源分布（分源低效、单一共享检测器偏向主导源）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Pre-MSCT 共享-私有特征融合：[CLS] 增强的音视 token 路由到共享流+源特异私有流（共享流建模跨源可复用证据、私有流保留源依赖残差）+域感知门控残差融合
- 📌 **结论**：两种受控源组成协议下宏与最差源 AUC 均超 MRDF/MSCT 基线——不均衡多源音视深伪检测（部署域失衡下的检测鲁棒性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Audio-visual deepfake detectors are often trained under a single dataset or manipulation regime, but multi-source training collections may contain heterogeneous attacks and uneven source distributions. Training separate detectors for each source is inefficient, while a single shared detector may be biased toward dominant source-specific patterns. We study known-source imbalanced multi-source audio-visual deepfake detection using FakeAVCeleb and LAV-DF, where source identity is supplied during training and inference. To address this setting, we build on the Multi-Scale Cross-Modal Transformer Encoder (MSCT) and propose Pre-MSCT Shared-Private Feature Fusion. Our method routes \texttt{[CLS]}-augmented audio and visual tokens into a shared stream and source-specific private streams before the first MSCT block, while retaining its internal multi-scale self-attention (MSSA), differential cross-modal attention (DCA), and feed-forward operations. The shared stream models cross-source reusable evidence, while the private streams retain source-dependent residual variations. A domain-aware gated residual fusion module injects selected private evidence into the shared representation for the main fused prediction. Experiments under two controlled source-composition protocols show improved macro and worst-source AUC compared with the MRDF and MSCT baselines. These results support shared-private cross-modal discrepancy learning in the evaluated two-source setting with known source identity.

</details>

### 141. When Form Changes but Logic Doesn't: Building Logic-invariant LLMs through Logical Structure

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25402)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`logical invariance`、`decision diagram`、`semantic cues`、`reasoning`

👤 **作者**：Xuyuan Liu、Xinshuai Dong、Elynn Chen、Yujun Yan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 强推理性能反映逻辑结构理解还是浅层语义线索？关键指标=对逻辑等价输入是否一致（表达不同预测相同）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：系统评测：强 LLM 也不一致保持预测且对未见逻辑形式泛化差→LoGIST：输入映射到决策图（捕获底层逻辑结构的 DAG）编码为逻辑嵌入条件化推理
- 📌 **结论**：多训练设置下减少逻辑等价下的不一致并改善未见形式泛化——逻辑不变性作为鲁棒推理的有用目标（推理的构念分离）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across diverse reasoning tasks, yet it remains unclear whether this performance reflects an understanding of underlying logical structure or a reliance on shallow semantic cues. A key indicator of such understanding is whether models treat logically equivalent inputs consistently, producing the same predictions despite differences in expression. Through systematic evaluation on curated data, we show that even strong LLMs often fail this test: they do not consistently preserve predictions across logically equivalent inputs and generalize poorly to unseen logical forms. To address this limitation, we propose LoGIcal STructure-guided reasoning (LoGIST), a framework that guides LLM reasoning with explicit logical structure. LoGIST maps each input instance to a decision diagram, a directed acyclic graph that captures the underlying logical structure, and encodes this graph into a logical embedding that conditions the LLM's reasoning process. Across multiple training settings, LoGIST reduces inconsistency under logical equivalence and improves generalization to unseen logical forms, suggesting that logical invariance is a useful target for building more robust and generalizable logical reasoning in LLMs.

</details>

### 142. A New Hope for PRNU on Modern Smartphones? Evidence That Device-Specific Fingerprints Survive Computational Photography

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25438)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`PRNU`、`source attribution`、`modern smartphones`、`score normalization`

👤 **作者**：Benjamin Loison、Quentin Bammey、Thibaud Ehret、Rafael Grompone von Gioi、Pablo Musé、Marina Gardella

- 🎯 **研究动机**：PRNU 被视为源相机识别最可靠标记——近期研究报告现代智能手机不同设备指纹间高相关而质疑其区分力
- 🔬 **研究方法**：30 部近期手机受控采集：发现同型号全部指纹共享的分量抬高标准 sPCE→ΔsPCE（查询图与候选指纹的 sPCE 减该图对其余候选指纹的均值 sPCE，T-norm 式归一）
- 📌 **结论**：ΔsPCE 提升归属准确率——设备特异 PRNU 信号仍在，比较方式是问题；数据集有限与候选集依赖限制操作应用（源归因取证的有效性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Photo Response Non-Uniformity (PRNU) is widely regarded as the most reliable marker for source camera identification. Recent studies, however, report high correlations between images and fingerprints from different modern smartphones, which calls into question the discriminative power of PRNU on these devices. Using 30 recent smartphones under controlled acquisition conditions (e.g., no digital zoom), we investigate whether the fingerprint itself is at fault or the way it is compared. We find that a component shared by all fingerprints of the same model inflates the standard signed peak-to-correlation energy (sPCE). To counter this, we propose $\Delta$sPCE: the sPCE between a query image and a candidate camera's fingerprint, minus the mean sPCE between that image and the fingerprints of the remaining candidates, in the spirit of T-norm score normalization. $\Delta$sPCE improves attribution accuracy, suggesting that the device-specific PRNU signal is still present. Although the limited dataset size and the dependence of $\Delta$sPCE on the candidate camera set currently restrict operational applicability, these results suggest that PRNU-based attribution of modern smartphones remains feasible when fingerprints are compared appropriately.

</details>

### 143. A failure of Imagination: VLMs fail to use visual representations for text, but self-distillation can help

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25470)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`strong multimodality`、`text-only visualization`、`self-distillation`、`probing`

👤 **作者**：Shashwat Singh、He He、Tal Linzen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：VL 模型经微调让 LM 注意视觉特征——该对齐是否改变 LM 表示文本的方式（弱多模态=能答图问；强多模态=文本表示本身视觉对齐从而改善潜在视觉结构的纯文本任务）？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：形状计数任务配对等价文本与图像规范（图像给定时平凡、文本坐标时须想象）；比较指令微调 Qwen-2.5/Mistral 与其视觉对应对应物+探针实验+把上下文视觉嵌入蒸馏进文本嵌入的目标
- 📌 **结论**：多模态模型在文本变体上不更好（尽管图像版强）；探针证实视觉对齐解码器嵌入不比纯文本对应物多带视觉结构；蒸馏对齐在无显式监督下诱发增益（多模态对齐的深浅之辨）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Vision-language (VL) models are typically built by fine-tuning a language model to attend to visual features in service of a Visual Question-Answering objective. We ask whether this alignment changes how language models represent text---especially on tasks where visualization would help, like geometry questions posed in words. We distinguish weak multimodality (the model can answer questions about an input image) from strong multimodality (the model's text representations themselves become vision-aligned, improving performance on text-only tasks with latent visual structure). To test for strong multimodality, we construct a task of shape-counting problems with paired equivalent text and image specifications. This task is trivial when the image is provided, but requires visualization when specified in text as line coordinates. We compare instruction-tuned Qwen-2.5 and Mistral models against their vision-aligned counterparts on the text variant of the task. We find that the multimodal models do no better in this setting, despite strong performance on the image version of the task. Probing experiments corroborate this: vision-aligned decoder embeddings carry no more visual structure than their text-only counterparts. Finally, we provide an optimization objective that distills in-context visual embeddings into text embeddings. This alignment induces gains on the text variant of the task without explicit task supervision, even on samples more complex than seen during alignment. This suggests that VQA-style alignment leaves available cross-modal transfer untapped.

</details>

### 144. Narrow Decision Models: A System-1 Architecture for Calibrated, Typed Decisions

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25553)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`Narrow Decision Models`、`System-1 architecture`、`prefix tree`、`block attention`

👤 **作者**：Carlos André Antunes、…、Luis Fernando Fausto

- 🎯 **研究动机**：Jev 类 System One 模型是通用型（一套权重 API 服务、仅经请求适配域）——研究设计空间另一端：为一个组织的决策训练、小到可跑自家基础设施、架构开放可陈述可测试
- 🔬 **研究方法**：NDM：请求序列化为前缀树（状态→每问题→每选项）+块注意力掩码的逻辑位置上跑双向编码器（每个答案按构造对选项顺序不变且与其他问题独立）；法院通信路由为带『皆非』选项的 Choice 组合；proper scoring rule 课程训练+校准弃权+rubric 组合+held-out 前接受拒绝机制的协议
- 📌 **结论**：公开与虚构数据的 minimal validation——组织级窄决策模型的开放 System-1 架构（决策模型的设计空间另一极）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

System One models, introduced with TypeSafe's Jev, answer typed questions about a state with calibrated probabilities instead of generated text: choosing among options described in natural language (Choice), placing the state on an ordered scale (Score), or judging a yes/no statement (Noul). Jev is general-purpose: one set of weights, served as an API and adapted to a domain only through the request. We study the other end of the design space. A Narrow Decision Model (NDM) offers the same interface but is trained for the decisions of one organization, is small enough to run on its own infrastructure, and has an open architecture whose properties can be stated and tested. The state can be any text or structured record about which decisions are made; our running case is the routing of court communications. The architecture serializes a request as a prefix tree (the state, then each question, then each option) and runs a bidirectional encoder under a block attention mask with logical positions, so that each answer is, by construction, invariant to the order of its options and independent of the other questions asked. Routing through an organization chart is a composition of Choice decisions with an explicit "none of these" option. We describe training with proper scoring rules through a curriculum, calibration and abstention, decisions composed by rubrics, and a protocol that accepts or rejects the mechanism before held-out data is opened. A minimal validation on public and fictitious data shows that the invariances hold to rounding in a trained model, that a small instance routes on organization charts it has never seen well above lexical retrieval, and that, at equal compute, the block architecture reaches a lower loss than a pairwise cross-encoder. A first instance trained on real legal documents agrees with the LLM classifier in production on 91% of held-out documents.

</details>

### 145. Design and Cross-Domain Evaluation of an Agent Execution Harness

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25568)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`execution harness`、`planner-worker-reviewer`、`cross-domain`、`cost`

👤 **作者**：Saurav Joshi、Manas Gupta、Ashwin Venkatesh Raman、Akshay Budhkar

- 🎯 **研究动机**：复杂任务须协调规划/执行/审查使最终结果对准用户原始目标——原目标与可修订计划及执行记录分离的 harness 设计
- 🔬 **研究方法**：规划者派作用域任务、工作者产输出与工件、审查者对照原始请求检查后再提交或要求返工；可复用域特异指令经任务输入与执行轨迹精炼（不用官方评分反馈）；终端/临床/应用构建/科学问答跨域评测
- 📌 **结论**：Terminal-Bench 2.1 通过 83/89（93.26%）；HealthBench Pro 53 任务配对比较 Fable 配置超直接完成 19.89 点（中位任务级变化 -1.46）而推理成本 $0.29→$2.13/任务——协调如何支撑完成、在哪引入回归、代价几何（harness 工程的跨域账本）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Completing complex tasks with language-model agents requires coordinating planning, execution, and review so the final result addresses the user's original objective. We present an agent execution harness that keeps the original objective separate from a revisable plan and an execution record. A planner assigns scoped tasks, workers produce outputs and artifacts, and a reviewer examines results against the original request before submitting or requesting additional work. Reusable domain-specific instructions guide these roles and are refined during construction using task inputs and execution trajectories, without official grading feedback. We evaluate complete harness configurations across terminal tasks, clinical responses, application building, and scientific questions. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, the harness passes 83 of 89 instances (93.26%); on HealthBench Professional, a Fable-configured system achieves a length-adjusted rubric score of 69.63 across 525 tasks. In a 53-task paired HealthBench comparison, the Fable-configured harness achieves an aggregate score 19.89 points higher than direct completion, despite a $-1.46$-point median task-level change; mean inference cost rises from \$0.29 to \$2.13 per task. We combine these outcomes with latency, documented instruction revisions, and reviewer decisions. Evaluating outcomes alongside execution trajectories and resource use clarifies how coordination supports task completion, where it introduces regressions, and at what cost.

</details>

### 146. Adversarial Post-Training of Foundation Models for High-Frequency Trading

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25604)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LOB foundation models`、`adversarial post-training`、`market simulation`、`GAN critic`

👤 **作者**：Alexandre Bismuth、Kang Li、Aramis Marti-Shahandeh、Valentin Mohl、Jakob Foerster、Anisoara Calinescu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：限价订单簿数据上的自回归基础模型在市场模拟上 SOTA——交叉熵预训练既不惩罚自由滚动上的采样误差复合也不针对期望市场性质
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Wasserstein GAN 目标的后训练：经真实不可微撮合引擎在线训练的 critic 引导 rank-4 LoRA 扰动上的进化策略更新（KL 信赖域内）+EGGROLL 内存高效进化策略变体
- 📌 **结论**：held-out 面板上 LOB-Bench 真实性 +17.6%/+17.0%（选择匹配随机扰动与算力匹配继续预训练对照无改进）；500 消息滚动的误差累积从 3.1× 放缓到 2.3×；<50M token/12 GPU 时（低于预训练三个数量级）——对抗后训练是不可被更多预训练替代的必要阶段（交易系统仿真的对齐化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

While autoregressive foundation models trained on limit order book (LOB) data show state-of-the-art performance for market simulation, pre-training with cross-entropy neither penalizes the compounding of sampling errors over free-running rollouts nor targets desired market properties. We introduce a post-training method for LOB foundation models using a Wasserstein generative adversarial network (GAN) objective. A critic, trained online on rollouts through the true, non-differentiable matching engine, guides updates from evolution strategies over rank-4 LoRA perturbations of the pre-trained generator, under a KL trust region. For efficiency, we use EGGROLL (Evolution Guided GeneRal Optimisation via Low-rank Learning), a memory-efficient variant of evolution strategies. On a held-out panel, adversarial post-training improves LOB-Bench realism by 17.6% (WS-21) and 17.0% (Mean-$L_1$), while selection-matched random-perturbation and compute-matched continued-pre-training controls yield no improvement. Post-training is thus a necessary stage that more pre-training does not replace. Per-position divergence measurements show that it slows error accumulation over 500-message rollouts from $3.1\times$ to $2.3\times$. Adversarial post-training is also inexpensive, requiring under 50M training tokens and 12 GPU-hours per seed, three orders of magnitude below the 48B-token pre-training budget. Ablations over critic inputs, optimizers, and the population-rollout trade-off, together with an analysis of reward hacking, yield practical design guidelines for adversarial post-training.

</details>

### 147. From Black Box to Controller: Inference-Time Steering of LLM via Feature Amplification

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25700)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`inference-time steering`、`SAE controller`、`feature amplification`、`quantifiable modulation`

👤 **作者**：Keqi Chen、…、Ya Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：推理时转向对部署关键（按场景风险动态调整谨慎水平）——须调节 LLM 偏好强度：提示工程须任务特异设计、微调须额外优化与数据
- 🔬 **研究方法**：NeuroSteer：SAE 作控制器——正负例间神经元激活频率比较隔离目标偏好（礼貌/正面情绪等）的特征神经元，推理时放大所选 SAE 特征激活以转向输出分布；强度按场景或用户调整
- 📌 **结论**：可改变情感/毒性/立场/礼貌且多个设置下目标方向转向分超基线（任务与强度依赖的质量权衡）——黑盒到控制器的定量偏好调制（转向的可量化强度）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Inference-time steering is crucial for deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) in real-world applications, where they must flexibly adjust behavior preferences. For instance, a model should dynamically adjust its caution level based on the risk of the scenario. This requirement introduces a challenge: modulating the intensity of LLM preferences. Prompt engineering can require task-specific prompt design, while fine-tuning requires additional optimization and data. These requirements can make repeated behavioral adjustments costly. To address this, we introduce NeuroSteer, a plug-and-play framework that enables flexible, quantitative adjustment of LLM preferences at inference time. NeuroSteer employs a Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) as its controller. Comparing neuron activation frequencies between positive and negative examples isolates "feature neurons" that correspond to target preferences such as politeness and positive sentiment. During inference, the activations of selected SAE features are amplified in the latent representations to steer the output distribution. Users can adjust the strength of this steering on a per-scenario or per-user basis, granting the LLM fine-grained, quantifiable trait modulation. Our experiments show that NeuroSteer can alter sentiment, toxicity, stance, and politeness at inference time. It achieves higher target-direction steering scores than the compared baselines in several settings, with task- and strength-dependent trade-offs in generation quality.

</details>

### 148. UniJev: Separating Accuracy from Calibration in Trimodal System One Decision Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25784)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`System One`、`trimodal`、`calibration`、`confidence-outcome alignment`

👤 **作者**：Jianing Wang、…、Hongcheng Guo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：System One 模型产软件可直接消费的概率判断（可靠置信与答案准确同等重要）——文本/图像/视频联合训练未被探索且 SFT 与 RLCD 贡献难分离
- 🔬 **研究方法**：UniJev：共享头在三模态联合监督训练下给候选答案打分；发现 RLCD 算法几乎不改准确率而对视觉校准效应不一致→Cora（置信-结局可靠性对齐）：按置信分组并把每个预测的置信优化向其桶的经验准确率
- 📌 **结论**：2B 与 9B 上 Cora 降低图像与视频 ECE 且保持准确率（无强化阶段）——三模态决策模型的准确率与校准分离（决策模型校准的监督路线）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

System One models produce probabilistic judgments that software can consume directly, making reliable confidence as important as answer accuracy. Yet joint training across text, images, and video remains underexplored, and the contributions of supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning for calibrated decisions (RLCD) are difficult to separate. We introduce UniJev, a trimodal framework that scores candidate answers with a shared head under joint supervised training across text, images, and video. Supervised decision models improve accuracy over their backbone readouts across all three modalities, while the existing RLCD algorithms change accuracy little and have inconsistent effects on visual calibration across recipes. These findings motivate a separate supervised objective for confidence calibration. We introduce Cora (Confidence--Outcome Reliability Alignment), which groups current predictions by confidence and optimizes each prediction's confidence toward the empirical accuracy of its bin. At both 2B and 9B, Cora lowers image and video expected calibration error (ECE) while preserving accuracy, without a reinforcement stage. We release our code at this https URL.

</details>

### 149. Diffusion Language Models Change Speed Across Tasks and Languages

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25835)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM`、`forward passes`、`cost variation`、`decoding speed`

👤 **作者**：Mohsen Fayyaz、Ali Modarressi、Daniel Israel、Lucas Bandarkar、Nanyun Peng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：dLLM 单次前向可写多 token 并在生成中决定花多少次前向——同 token 长度的两个输出成本可大不相同：任务与语言间成本变异的底层因素
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两扩散模型×10 任务：每 token 前向次数重度依赖任务（从输入复制或轻编辑便宜、开放式写作昂贵；故事写作每 token 前向超语法修正三倍）；新颖度与词稀有度解释大部分差距；多语中高分词语言每 token 也须更多前向；按解码速度加权候选答案超多数投票至 4.2 点
- 📌 **结论**：扩散的计算优势取决于输出有多少已被决定，解码努力提供实用的正确性信号（dLLM 成本结构与可靠性信号）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Diffusion language models can write several tokens in one forward pass, and they decide during generation how many passes to spend. Two outputs of the same token length can therefore vary a lot in cost. To understand the underlying factors of this cost variation, we analyze two diffusion models across 10 tasks. We find that the number of forward passes per token depends heavily on the task. Text copied or lightly edited from the input is cheap, while open-ended writing is costly. In DiffusionGemma, generating a token for story writing requires more than three times as many forward passes as for grammar correction. Two output properties explain much of this gap: how much of it is new relative to the input, and how rare its words are within the task. The same holds within a single response when thinking is involved. The final answer is cheap when it repeats the model's reasoning trace and costly when it adds something new. In multilingual generation, we find that highly tokenized languages also require more passes per token, adding to the cost they already pay with more tokens. Finally, cost tracks correctness: on the same math problem, wrong answers take more passes per token than correct ones, and weighting candidate answers by decoding speed beats majority voting by up to 4.2 points at no extra cost. Taken together, our results show that diffusion's computational advantage depends on how much of the output is already determined, while its decoding effort offers a practical signal for making inference more efficient and reliable.

</details>

### 150. ACE-Trader: Adversarial Co-Evolution for Generator-Critic Trading Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25847)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`co-evolution`、`generator-critic`、`trading agents`、`memory selection`

👤 **作者**：Chang Zhou、Jiarun Zhou、Xingtong Yu、Hong Cheng、Hui Zhang、Xinming Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：生成器-批评者交易系统把 LLM 交易者与红队 agent 配对（评估决策供修订）——改善单个决策不保证两 agent 从经验共同进步：一种市场条件下的有益经验在另一条件下或失效，且交易结局反映双方行动使逐角色经验难导出
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ACE-Trader：上下文依赖记忆选择器学识别对当前决策有用的经验（共享净回报差反馈+在线更新重近期）+经共享交易记录连接角色特异记忆的共同演化机制（用记录评估红队风险评估与交易者修订的效果再导出各自经验）
- 📌 **结论**：对抗共同演化的交易框架——双角色经验选择与共同进化（交易 agent 的攻防协同）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Generator--critic trading systems pair an LLM-based trader that makes trading decisions with a red team agent that evaluates these decisions and provides feedback for revision. However, improving individual trading decisions does not ensure that both agents learn from experience and improve together over time. This raises an important question: how can the trader and red-Team select useful experience and learn from each other's evolving behavior? On one hand, experience that benefits decisions under one market condition may become ineffective or harmful under another. On the other hand, trading outcomes reflect both agents' actions, making it difficult to derive appropriate experience for each role. In this work, we propose ACE-Trader, an adversarial co-evolution trading framework. Specifically, we first develop separate context-dependent memory selectors that learn to identify useful experience for the current trading decision.
