Both selectors receive shared feedback from net-return differences between complete memory-assisted and memory-free trading pipelines, with online updates emphasizing recent outcomes. We further design a co-evolution mechanism that links role-specific memories through shared trading records. We use these records to evaluate the Red-Team's risk assessments and the effects of the Trader's revisions against realized market outcomes, then derive experience for their respective memories. By linking this experience to the corresponding records, we enable the Trader to improve its proposals based on prior critiques and the Red-Team to adjust its review focus as the Trader's behavior changes. Experiments on U.S. stock trading show that ACE-Trader outperforms all 22 active baselines in cumulative return and achieves the highest Sharpe ratio and lowest maximum drawdown across both active methods and passive benchmarks.

</details>

### 151. Learning Who Talks to Whom: Role-Aware Communication Topology Scheduling for Efficient LLM Multi-Agent Collaboration

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25851)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`MAS communication topology`、`role-aware scheduling`、`credit assignment`、`efficiency`

👤 **作者**：Haibin Li、…、Wei Wei

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM-MAS 靠结构化通信拓扑促进信息交换与协作推理——有效调度拓扑（谁何时对谁说话）以平衡集体推理与指数 token 开销仍是根本挑战（静态图不能适应动态状态/忽略角色互补）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ACTS：CTDE 范式下双注意力——角色条件关系注意力按角色与演化状态动态选变基数交互伙伴+拓扑注意力混合器把有向通信拓扑显式嵌入团队价值分解做精确信用分配
- 📌 **结论**：九基准（代码生成/多步推理/通识/竞赛数学）一致超越——角色感知的自适应通信拓扑调度（MAS 效率工程）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multi-agent systems driven by large language models (LLMs) rely on structured communication topologies to facilitate information exchange and collaborative reasoning. However, effectively scheduling these topologies to dynamically determine who talks to whom and when to communicate remains a fundamental challenge in balancing collective reasoning performance against exponential token overhead. Existing topology scheduling approaches either enforce static communication graphs that cannot adapt to dynamic problem-solving states, or overlook agent role complementarity while failing to explicitly incorporate the synthesized graph structure into team-level decision evaluation. To tackle these limitations, we propose ACTS, a role-aware adaptive communication topology scheduling framework for efficient LLM multi-agent collaboration. Orchestrated within a Centralized Training with Decentralized Execution (CTDE) paradigm, ACTS introduces a novel dual-attention mechanism. Specifically, a Role-conditioned Relational Attention module dynamically selects variable-cardinality interaction partners based on agent roles and evolving execution states, while a Topological Attention Mixer explicitly embeds the directed communication topology into team value decomposition for precise credit assignment. Comprehensive evaluations across 9 challenging benchmarks covering code generation, multi-step reasoning, general knowledge, and competitive mathematics demonstrate that ACTS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, boosting average accuracy by up to 6.19 percentage points while reducing token consumption by over 15%. The project website is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 152. DeltaTrace: Efficient Signed Attribution for Reasoning Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25877)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`signed attribution`、`reasoning models`、`token masking`、`faithfulness`

👤 **作者**：Chen Liang、…、Hongzhi Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：上下文中的词如何支持或反对 LM 的响应——DeltaTrace 归因框架解释输入 token 对固定响应的效应（响应对数概率在被解释上下文 token 掩蔽时的变化）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：有限变化的算子特异局部规则+沿计算图后向经有效割线斜率组合；每 token 的带符号归因=其嵌入位移与后向传播斜率的内积（局部守恒使贡献和等于测得分数差且句子/段落聚合时保持意义）；支持注意力与线性注意力模型
- 📌 **结论**：检索与推理任务上删除忠实度（RISE/MAS）最低、针堆召回最高（超 FlashTrace 5.67-24.33 点）——推理 LM 的带符号高效归因（推理监控的证据工具）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We study how words in the context support or oppose a language model's response and present DeltaTrace, a novel attribution framework that explains the effect of input tokens on a fixed model response. DeltaTrace measures how the response's log-probability changes when the context tokens being explained are masked. To distribute this difference among source tokens, it constructs operator-specific local rules for finite changes and composes them backward through the computational graph using effective secant slopes. Each token is assigned a signed attribution as the inner product of its embedding displacement and the corresponding backward-propagated slope. Local conservation makes these contributions sum to the measured score difference and preserves their meaning when aggregated over sentences or passages. DeltaTrace supports both attention and linear-attention models, with auxiliary memory growing linearly with sequence length at fixed model dimensions and chunk size. On the evaluated attention model, DeltaTrace achieves the lowest deletion-faithfulness scores (RISE and MAS) on most retrieval and reasoning tasks and the highest needle-in-a-haystack Recall, exceeding FlashTrace by 5.67-24.33 percentage points. On the evaluated hybrid attention and linear-attention model, DeltaTrace lowers RISE on 9 tasks and MAS on 12 relative to FlashTrace.

</details>

### 153. When Should AI Chime In? Benchmarking Timely Contribution of Proactive Agents in Multi-Party Chat

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25896)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`proactive agents`、`timing`、`multi-party chat`、`human judgment`

👤 **作者**：Ruotong Wang、…、Amy X Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：主动 agent 可不经询问浮现有用信息支撑协作——但再相关的贡献时机不当也会破坏：隔离时机问题（固定候选贡献何时该插入）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PITCH：参与者在自己参与的同一非脚本多方对话中做时机判断的首个大规模人类判据基准（2-4 人同步聊天、撰写候选 AI 贡献、放置进历史并集体审议最佳位置）；384 候选贡献×1,091 判断×89 参与者×35 对话；15 模型×28 配置评测
- 📌 **结论**：给全历史时模型放置良好而实时决定时系统性过早插入（最佳轮准确率 57.8%→19.0%、近半数放置在每位参与者可接受窗之前）；外行人类实时更准——主动 agent 的时机基准（干预时机的量化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Proactive agents can support group collaboration by surfacing useful information without waiting to be asked, but even relevant contributions can be disruptive if poorly timed. In this work, we isolate the timing question: given a fixed candidate contribution such as a clarification or relevant fact, when should an AI agent chime in? We introduce PITCH (Proactive Intervention Timing in CHat), to our knowledge the first benchmark with large-scale human judgments of proactive agent timing collected from participants in the same unscripted multi-party conversations they took part in. To collect high-quality judgments, we design a novel interface in which 2-4 people chat synchronously, author candidate AI contribution messages, place their own and others' contributions into the chat history, and then deliberate as a group about the best placement for each message. PITCH contains 384 candidate contributions and 1,091 judgments, each marking an ideal turn, an acceptable window, or that a contribution should not appear, from 89 participants engaged in 35 conversations. Evaluating 15 models across 28 configurations, we find that while models can place contributions well when given the entire conversation history, they systematically chime in too early when asked to decide live as the conversation unfolds: best-turn accuracy falls from 57.8% to 19.0%, with nearly half of placements occurring before every participant's acceptable window. In comparison, lay humans deciding live are more accurate (23.7% vs 19.0%), and fewer of their placements are premature (39.7% vs 46.4%). Model reasoning traces show that 93% of intervention justifications cite relevance, while almost none consider waiting. By providing a human-grounded benchmark, our work reveals gaps in agents' ability to integrate into group settings and points towards proactive agents that can reason explicitly about appropriate timing.

</details>

### 154. CALM: Consistency-Aware Logit Masking in Decoding Space for Mitigating Overthinking of Large Reasoning Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25908)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`overthinking`、`logit masking`、`action transition points`、`decoding`

👤 **作者**：Shengjing Liu、Feiyang Li、Yuxuan Yang、Yile Wang、Hui Huang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：大推理模型常过度思考（不必要推理步）——截断法检测连续试答一致并强制早出，但局部停止信号不一定指示终答稳定且可打断有用的后续反思或验证
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CALM 免训练解码干预：步边界监视试答+对『Wait』/『Alternatively』/『Hmm』等动作转折点 token 施 logit 掩蔽（抑制冗余过渡推理而让思考更自然继续）
- 📌 **结论**：四先进 LRM×五基准：token 消耗降 18.6-25.0% 且保持性能（甚至 +0.07-1.48%）；judge 评更连贯简洁——过度思考的解码空间缓解

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large reasoning models (LRMs) have achieved strong performance on challenging mathematical and scientific tasks through long chain-of-thought reasoning, but they often suffer from overthinking with unnecessary reasoning steps. Existing truncation-based methods typically detect consecutive trial-answer agreement and force early exit, yet such local stopping signals do not necessarily indicate final-answer stability and can disrupt useful subsequent reflection or verification, potentially leading to performance degradation. We propose consistency-aware logit masking (CALM), a training-free decoding intervention for mitigating overthinking in LRMs. CALM first monitors trial answers at step boundaries and then applies logit masking to action transition point (ATP) tokens such as "Wait", "Alternatively", and "Hmm", suppressing redundant transitional reasoning while allowing the model to continue its thought process more naturally. Experiments across four advanced LRMs and five benchmarks show that CALM reduces token consumption by 18.6%-25.0% compared with full CoT reasoning while maintaining performance, and can even improve accuracy by 0.07%-1.48% by reducing redundant reasoning. LLM-as-a-Judge results also demonstrate that CALM provides more coherent and concise results.

</details>

### 155. OmniGround: Strengthening Omni-Modal Temporal Grounding via Attention-Consistency Training

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25929)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`omni-modal`、`temporal grounding`、`attention-consistency`、`post-training`

👤 **作者**：Zixuan Chen、…、Xinghao Jiang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：全模态 LLM 联合理解视频/音频/文本但时间接地弱——检查内部证据发现时间生成差距：模型内部注意力比它生成的时间戳更准地定位目标时刻
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TACG：把内部注意力当可靠时间枢轴并经注意力一致性训练与模型预测和真值标注对齐+58 万样本训练集（稠密音视标注重建时间 QA）→OmniGround
- 📌 **结论**：全模态时间接地 SOTA（LongVALE 37.5 mIoU/UnAV-100 35.7）且保持通用音视理解——内部注意力枢轴的后训练（全模态可靠性的能力面）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Omni-modal large language models can jointly understand video, audio, and text, yet they remain weak at temporal grounding, a capability essential for real-world audio-visual applications. To understand this limitation, we look beyond final predictions and examine the model's internal temporal evidence. We identify a temporal generation gap: the internal attention of the model localizes the target moment more accurately than the timestamps it generates. To close this gap, we propose TACG (Temporal Attention-Consistency Grounding), a post-training method that treats internal attention as a reliable temporal pivot and aligns it with both the model prediction and the ground-truth annotation through attention-consistency training. We further build a 580K-sample training dataset by reconstructing temporal QA from dense audio-visual annotations and organizing general omni-modal instruction data. Using TACG and this training dataset, we train OmniGround, a general-purpose omni-modal model with stronger temporal grounding capability, without injecting any additional temporal cues. OmniGround reaches state-of-the-art omni-modal temporal grounding, including 37.5 mIoU on LongVALE and 35.7 mIoU on UnAV-100, while staying competitive on both general audio-visual understanding and vision-only grounding.

</details>

### 156. ABACUS: Program-Guided Multistep Reasoning over Frozen Judgment Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25963)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`judgment models`、`program composition`、`frozen models`、`ABACUS`

👤 **作者**：Yuhao Wang、Zelin Zang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：判断模型经给有限候选结果打分回答语义问题——可复用程序能否把这些局部决策变成强大的多步推理？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ABACUS：生成式教师离线设计过程+判断模型在线解决局部语义问题+保存的程序把决策组合成答案（Kev 实例化于三个 BIG-Bench Hard 任务）
- 📌 **结论**：九个独立构造程序解决 150 开发输入的全部 450 次执行；冻结三程序在 600 held-out 上宏准确率 99.67%（直接 Kev 64.00%）且与 Qwen 无限制响应相当而在线提速 8.43 倍——冻结判断模型的程序化推理扩展

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Judgment models answer semantic questions by scoring a finite set of candidate outcomes. Can reusable programs turn these local decisions into powerful multistep reasoning? We introduce \method, a framework that jointly constructs judgment schemas and composition programs for frozen judgment models. A generative teacher designs the procedure offline; the judgment model resolves local semantic questions online, and the saved program composes its decisions into an answer. We study three allocations of design effort between humans and the teacher, focusing on construction through a shared interface. We instantiate the framework with Kev. On three selected BIG-Bench Hard tasks, nine independently constructed programs solve all 450 executions of 150 development inputs. A separate deployment study freezes three programs after development-set reference-agreement refinement and evaluates them on 600 held-out questions. These programs achieve near-perfect 99.67\% macro accuracy, versus 64.00\% for direct Kev and 68.80\% for final-answer-only Qwen3.5-4B. They attain comparable observed accuracy to Qwen's unrestricted response policy (99.07\%) with an \textbf{8.43-fold online speedup}: 0.255 versus 2.150 seconds per question. These results show how reusable programs can substantially expand the reasoning capability of a compact judgment model while keeping its parameters fixed.

</details>

### 157. NIOCIS: Typed-Decision Next-Hop Routing with Node-Conditioned Option Spaces for Tool-Use Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25985)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`typed routing`、`tool-use agents`、`loop-collapsed graph`、`cost measurement`

👤 **作者**：Jin Han、Mingfeng Xin、Feng Liang、Jingtao Yu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：工具 agent 逐决策付费（每跳语言模型重读状态思考数秒而动作有限且决策复现）——能否用小模型单次前向回答『此状态下舰队下一步选哪个声明选项』？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：NIOCIS：执行日志折叠进环折叠动作图（前向转移+恢复回边）；1,615 生产安全运营回合测量替换/回报/界限
- 📌 **结论**：类型化编码器 10-19.5ms 匹配 32.2% held-out 决策（LLM 每跳 2.2s）；最有信心 15% 持 91.9% 准确；跨迹留一测试给出已释放状态上限 25.6-44.9%（重复状态分歧多于一致）——路由层替换的诚实核算（成本-准确率的边界）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Tool-use agents pay per decision: at each hop a language model re-reads the state and thinks for seconds, though its actions are finite and decisions recur. We present NIOCIS, a routing layer that folds execution logs into a loop-collapsed action graph (forward transitions plus recovery back-edges) answering one typed question: which declared option at a state, plus stop, does the fleet take next? A small model reads a calibrated distribution over them in one pass, emitting no text. On 1,615 production security-operations episodes we measure what the layer replaces, returns and is bounded by. Replaces: the typed encoder matches the logged next hop on 32.2% of the 3,875 held-out decisions (34.9% within the declared options) in 10 ms of encoder time (19.5 ms for the typed-choice form), against 2.2 s per LLM hop; two feature readouts reach 32.8% and 32.3%, an ordering the paired test does not resolve, so the typed layer is chosen for cost, not a separated lead. Tuning: jpt-4b reaches 9.9% zero-shot and 31.9% fine-tuned, a gap an exact paired test does not resolve (p=0.68), at 244 ms. Returns little: the realised hop falls outside the declared options for 7.9% of decisions and outside the model's top twenty for 9.3%; the most confident 15% holds 91.9% accuracy. Bounded: a cross-trace leave-one-out test puts the released state's ceiling at 25.6--44.9% as the state key grows more specific, i.e. repeated states disagree more often than they agree; the option-space ablation attributes the gain to per-node candidate restriction (+3.5 points over a flat catalog) rather than graph traversal (zero within its interval); a gated self-evolution loop lifts every arm above its anchor yet ends below full supervision; a confidence-gated cascade loses agreement when the escalation arm is the zero-shot stand-in; and agreement past a third of the fleet needs a state carrying the plan and the observation.

</details>

### 158. Internalizing Chain-of-thought into Looped Transformers

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25998)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`looped transformers`、`CoT internalization`、`complexity`、`latent thought`

👤 **作者**：Kehang Zhou、Pinyan Lu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：CoT 使常数位 transformer 经生成 token 做通用计算而循环 transformer 经隐藏态递归做同样计算（潜思维）——构造在两者间插值的常数位循环 transformer 族
- 🔬 **研究方法**：对规定循环数 L 与固定整数 k（s(n)^{1/k}=Ω(L)）：模拟时间 t(n) 空间 s(n) 图灵机用 O(s(n)/L) 上下文窗与 O(t(n)s(n)^{1/k}/L) 生成 token（相对未循环队列机模拟两资源各降 L 倍）；softmax 注意力常数位下亦成立
- 📌 **结论**：循环即资源折扣的形式刻画——CoT 内化为潜思维的计算理论（推理内部化的复杂度基础）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Chain-of-thought (CoT) enables constant-bit-size transformers to perform general-purpose computation through generated tokens (Li and Wang, 2025a), whereas looped transformers carry out the same computation by recurrence in hidden states (latent thought). We construct a family of constant-bit-size looped transformers that interpolates between CoT and latent thought. For a prescribed loop count $L=L(n)$ and a fixed integer $k\ge1$ satisfying $s(n)^{1/k}=\Omega(L)$, our transformer simulates a time-$t(n)$, space-$s(n)$ Turing machine using a context window of $O(s(n)/L)$ and $O(t(n)s(n)^{1/k}/L)$ generated tokens, reducing both resources by a factor of $L$ relative to the corresponding unlooped queue-machine simulation. The construction also holds with softmax attention at constant bit-size, under the finite-precision kernel specified in this paper.

</details>

### 159. Accelerated Drift of Scientific Prose in the LLM Era: Evidence from an Interrupted Time-Series Analysis

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26008)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`scientific writing drift`、`interrupted time-series`、`ChatGPT breakpoint`、`linguistic change`

👤 **作者**：Hanna Hubarava、Benjamin V. Ineichen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：科学文本嵌入其时代的写作规范——LLM 写作工具的兴起引入新因素而其出现的语言学变化理解不足：以 ChatGPT 2022-11 公开为观测断点
- 🔬 **研究方法**：arXiv AI/统计/定量生物+medRxiv 摘要的前后轨迹比较（词汇/标点/可读性/句法）；中断时间序列回归
- 📌 **结论**：2022 后多特征族多研究域实质变化（平均词长增加、公式可读性降、依存树最大深度与句级深度变异下降）；孤立标记频率只捕获部分转移——速率/方向/结构轨迹提供互补证据（科研诚信生态的 LLM 冲击）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Every scientific text is embedded in the writing norms of its time: it reflects earlier publications while also shaping the texts that follow. The growing use of LLM-based writing tools introduces a new factor in this evolving system, but the linguistic changes coinciding with their emergence remain insufficiently understood. We investigate whether the trajectories of scientific-preprint writing changed in rate or direction after conversational LLMs became publicly accessible, using the release of ChatGPT in November 2022 as the observational breakpoint. We compare pre- and post-2022 trajectories across vocabulary, punctuation, readability, and syntax in abstracts from arXiv's artificial intelligence, statistics, and quantitative biology categories and from medRxiv. Using interrupted time-series regression, we find substantial post-2022 changes across multiple feature families and research domains, including increases in average word length, reduced formula-based readability, and declines in maximum dependency-tree depth and sentence-level depth variation. Individual features follow different dynamics: some lexical markers accelerate persistently, while others rise and then recede. Our findings show that isolated marker frequencies capture only part of the shift: changes in the rate, direction, and structure of linguistic trajectories provide complementary evidence of broader post-2022 change in scientific-preprint writing. More broadly, these findings motivate monitoring scientific writing as a multidimensional, evolving system rather than through a fixed set of LLM-associated markers.

</details>

### 160. FinHopper: A Progressive Multi-Hop Benchmark for Complex Financial Agents with Step-wise Evaluation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26036)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`financial agents`、`multi-hop`、`step-wise evaluation`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Yuxuan Wu、…、Hao Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：金融 agent 须经开放搜索回答事实投资查询——金融多跳检索与一般多跳 QA 的关键差异：每个中间答案是精确类型化约束（实体/日期/事件/数值）决定下一查询（严格答案条件依赖使小中间错误级联成貌似合理的错误搜索轨迹）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：FinHopper：1-6 跳受控推理深度（股票/基金/宏观事实+金标中间答案的过程级评测）；状态转移合成框架组合已验证原子金融 QA 模式为保依赖链+金融专家公开搜索验证
- 📌 **结论**：20 配置：公开搜索工具必要但不充分；跳深增加性能骤降、最佳模型远低于人类专家；过程感知评分揭示终答评测隐藏的部分推理进展——金融 agent 的分步评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Financial agents are expected to answer factual investment queries through open search, but financial multi-hop retrieval differs from general multi-hop QA in a crucial way: each intermediate answer is an exact typed constraint, such as an entity, date, event, or numerical indicator, that determines the next query. This strict answer-conditioned dependency makes small intermediate errors cascade into plausible but wrong search trajectories. We introduce FinHopper, a benchmark for complex financial agents with controlled reasoning depth from 1 to 6 hops, covering stocks, funds, and macroeconomic facts, with gold intermediate answers for process-level evaluation. To construct FinHopper at scale, we design a state-transition-based synthesis framework that composes verified atomic financial QA patterns into dependency-preserving chains, followed by targeted in-synthesis verification, model-ensemble checking, and public-search validation by financial experts. Experiments with 20 LLM-agent configurations show that public search tools are necessary but insufficient: performance drops sharply as hop depth increases, the best model remains far below human experts, and process-aware scoring reveals partial reasoning progress hidden by final-answer-only evaluation. The benchmark is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 161. FinSafeGuard: A Quality-Driven Synthetic Dataset for Financial AI Safety

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26066)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`synthetic dataset`、`financial safety`、`data pipeline`、`SFT`

👤 **作者**：Joseph Thomas Thacil、Reetu Raj Harsh、Krati Saxena、Bhaskarjit Sarmah、Stefano Pasquali

- 🎯 **研究动机**：大规模生成高质量合成数据集仍是数据稀缺领域训练专业 AI 的根本挑战（金融 AI 安全）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：63.2 万场景规格的结构化分类→四阶段管线（收益递减分析定生成参数/条件自动进化修复低多样性批次/两层语义去重/LLM judge 质量过滤）；三域实证
- 📌 **结论**：26.4M 原始样本缩到 14.3M 高置信例（合并 20.9%/语义去重 18.7%/质量过滤 15.9%）——领域特异合成数据生成的可复现框架（金融安全训练栈）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Generating high-quality synthetic datasets at scale remains a fundamental challenge for training specialized AI systems in data-scarce domains. We present a comprehensive methodology that produces 14.3 million quality-filtered synthetic training examples from a structured taxonomy of 632,000 scenario specifications. Our four-stage pipeline ad- dresses critical challenges in synthetic data quality, diversity, and realism through: (1) systematic pre-generation research establishing optimal generation parameters via diminishing returns analysis, (2) conditional auto evolution mechanisms that remediate low-diversity batches during generation, (3) a two-layer semantic deduplication architecture combining lexical and embedding-based methods, and (4) LLM-as-Judge quality evaluation filtering samples based on behavioral correctness and tuple fidelity. Empirical analysis across three domains (Banking, Insurance, Financial Services) demonstrates domain-specific deduplication patterns ranging from 9.9% to 40.6%, with output generation modes exhibiting highest redundancy. Our batch size optimization reveals mode-specific diversity saturation thresholds, enabling principled allocation strategies. The complete pipeline reduces 26.4M raw samples to 14.3M high-confidence examples through merge (20.9% reduction), semantic deduplication (18.7% reduction), and quality-based filtering (15.9% reduction), providing a reproducible framework for domain-specific synthetic data generation with rigorous quality control.

</details>

### 162. CUA-Lite: An Open Platform for Computer Use Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26071)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`CUA platform`、`open resources`、`unified harness`、`RL/SFT`

👤 **作者**：Zhanhui Zhou、…、Dawn Song

- 🎯 **研究动机**：计算机使用 agent 的开放资源（环境/数据集/模型）散落——无标准则无法整合扩展且评测/RL/SFT 不能共享一套基础设施
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CUA-Lite 三标准化抽象：统一环境接口（12 基准跨桌面/浏览器/移动+免 VM 轻量沙箱复制 OSWorld>30,000 可验证任务）+统一监督数据格式（主要公开 CUA 数据集+前沿 CUA 新鲜 rollout）+跨评测/RL/SFT 共享的模型 harness（14 模型族）
- 📌 **结论**：发布集成基准参考分并展示同一 harness 下 RL 与 SFT 提升开源权重模型——CUA 开发平台（安全研究的对象底座）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Open resources for computer use agents (CUAs) -- environments, datasets, and models -- remain scattered: without a standard, these resources cannot be integrated and scaled; additionally, evaluation, RL, and SFT cannot share one infrastructure. We present CUA-Lite, an open platform for developing CUAs, built around three standardized abstractions: (1) Unified environment interface, under which we integrate 12 benchmarks across desktop, browser, and mobile, plus lightweight VM-free sandboxes that replicate OSWorld's desktop at much lower cost, providing over 30,000 verifiable tasks. (2) Unified supervised data format, into which we convert the major public CUA datasets, from GUI grounding and understanding to full rollouts, and record our fresh rollouts from frontier CUAs on these verifiable environments. (3) Model harnesses shared across evaluation, RL, and SFT, one per model and implemented for 14 model families. Each harness runs its model in these computer environments, producing the rollouts that evaluation and RL consume, or renders stored supervised data into the model's own training format for SFT. We release reference scores across the integrated benchmarks, and show that RL and SFT through the same harness improve open-weight models. We release our dataset, code, and models, and the platform is open to contributions.

</details>

### 163. Not All Similarity Is Leakage: Distribution-Aware Identity Leakage Assessment in Synthetic Face Datasets

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26077)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`identity leakage`、`synthetic faces`、`data copying`、`Scaled Overlap`

👤 **作者**：Paweł Borsukiewicz、Daniele Lunghi、Wendkûuni C. Ouédraogo、Fadi Boutros、Jacques Klein、Tegawendé F. Bissyandé

- 🎯 **研究动机**：合成数据为更少偏见的面部识别提供途径——但生成模型与条件化的局限带来身份泄漏风险（合成样本与生成器训练集过于相似而削弱隐私收益）；既有方法受分类器依赖与对未用于训练的真实数据集误报泄漏两限制
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Scaled Overlap（SO）：非参数数据复制测试适配到面部识别并扩展到身份级+多模型设置补偿单分类器误差
- 📌 **结论**：识别泄漏数据集（CemiFace/SFace2/IDNet，条件化引入最急性泄漏）；真实数据集对生成器训练贡献不均、多数合成图像来自多身份——合成人脸数据集泄漏的分布感知评估

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Synthetic data provides the means for more ethical and less biased facial recognition. Nonetheless, the limitations of generative models and the conditioning process they rely on pose a risk of identity leakage, resulting in synthetic samples that are overly similar to those seen in a generator's training set, mitigating privacy benefits. In this work, we show that previously proposed approaches are affected by two key limitations: high classifier dependence and a tendency to identify leakage with respect to real datasets that were not used in training. To address these shortcomings, we propose a novel metric called Scaled Overlap (SO), which adapts a non-parametric data-copying test for facial recognition and extends it to the identity level. By leveraging a multi-model setup that compensates for errors in individual classifiers, it provides insights into the true strength of identity leakage. Our analysis identifies leaking datasets (CemiFace, SFace2, IDNet) with the most acute leakage introduced by generative model conditioning in CemiFace, demonstrates that real datasets do not contribute equally to generator training, and shows that the majority of synthetic images are drawn from multiple identities rather than relying on a single identity. Ultimately, our study provides new insights into the quality of synthetic datasets and gives more reliable techniques to help the community ensure the trustworthiness of synthetic data.

</details>

### 164. Provable Adversarial Detection: Prime Quantization meets Gromov--Wasserstein

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26102)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`provable detection`、`prime quantization`、`Gromov-Wasserstein`、`adversarial`

👤 **作者**：Manohar Kaul、…、Hiroaki Kurihara

- 🎯 **研究动机**：对抗脆弱性持续于 CNN 到 VLM 的现代视觉架构——既有检测方法依赖启发式而无理论保证：对抗扰动能否被可证明检测的几何视角
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两互补度量空间：标准 CNN 嵌入空间 Z（对抗样本显著位移）+素数量化空间 P（数论离散化吸收小扰动、位移最小而保判别）→跨空间几何差异检测；首个对抗检测的严格分离理论（像素级吸收界/邻域直径集中/GW 分离定理/实践风险控制）
- 📌 **结论**：广泛攻击下检测性能一致强劲——可证明对抗检测的几何理论（对抗检测的理论保证路线）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Adversarial vulnerability persists across modern vision architectures from CNNs to vision language models (VLMs), yet existing detection methods rely on heuristics without theoretical guarantees. We address the fundamental question of when adversarial perturbations can be provably detected from a geometric perspective. Our key insight is that adversarial perturbations cannot simultaneously preserve geometric structure across spaces with fundamentally different properties. Accordingly, we construct two such complementary metric spaces. First, we use a standard CNN embedding space $Z$, where adversarial samples exhibit significant displacement patterns. Second, we build a novel prime-quantized space $P$, that absorbs small perturbations through number-theoretic discretization, resulting in minimal displacement, while preserving discriminability. We then leverage the geometric discrepancies across spaces $Z$ and $P$ to detect adversarial samples. To the best of our knowledge, we establish the first rigorous separation theory for adversarial detection, proving that adversarial samples create unavoidable geometric inconsistencies across both spaces. Our framework provides theoretical guarantees including pixel-level absorption bounds, neighborhood diameter concentration, Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) separation theorems, and practical risk control. Extensive experiments validate our theoretical predictions and achieve consistently strong detection performance across a wide range of attack types and model families.

</details>

### 165. The Control Layer Behind Rule-Following Agents Trading Across Market Conditions

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26324)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`trading agents`、`rule adherence`、`live evaluation`、`risk controls`

👤 **作者**：Saad Naja、Nasser Odtallah、Jake Van-Praag、Sarah May

- 🎯 **研究动机**：交易纪律常使人失败——在给 agent 实质自主权或资本前测试四个 AI 交易 agent 是否遵循策略与风险规则
- 🔬 **研究方法**：2026-02-12 至 08-27 的 2,627 个平仓头寸分析（同资产宇宙与执行条件；多数对实盘价格模拟+0.09% 往返费、18% 真实 Hyperliquid 订单）；每头寸声明策略/止损/目标+每 agent 至多五个持仓
- 📌 **结论**：全部四 agent 规则遵循高（含亏损者）；97.5% 总亏损保持在声明止损内、声明风险在亏损后稳定、23/24 运行尊重持仓上限——盈利性追踪相对盈亏平衡胜率而非纪律（交易 agent 规则遵循的实盘审计）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Trading discipline often fails people. Our study tested whether four AI trading agents followed their strategies and risk rules. Offering traders the evidence and an agent evaluation toolkit to separate luck from following the rules, before giving agents meaningful autonomy or capital. Our study analysed 2,627 closed positions from 12 February to 27 August 2026 across the same asset universe and execution conditions. Most positions were simulated against live prices with a 0.09% round-trip fee; 18% involved real Hyperliquid orders. Each position declared a strategy, stop and target, with a limit of five open positions per agent. The fleet finished net positive after fees. Rule adherence was high across all four agents, including the unprofitable one. Profitability tracked how often agents won relative to their break-even win rates. Institutional-style controls ran by default and contained losses through a 29% market drawdown and recovery, with documented exceptions. The discipline held for all four agents. Agents kept 97.5% of gross loss within declared stops, and typical losses closed at declared levels. Declared risk remained stable after losses and broadly stable across calm and volatile months. Twenty-three of 24 runs respected the position limit; logs identify the exception's size and duration. The same live data produced different results across assigned strategies. The trade-log tests can be rerun from the published trade snapshot; regime tests also require the published Bitcoin prices and regime labels. Section 3.5 specifies the stop-breach tolerance. These trade-level results are not account-level returns or proof of a lasting edge. Our study provides an evaluation toolkit to help traders distinguish rule-following from luck before committing capital. For anyone considering allocating capital to agents to trade, discipline should be the entry test, not the only test.

</details>

### 166. Typed Decision Models under Prevalence Shift: Decision Training, Calibration, and Implicit Priors

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26325)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`typed decision models`、`prevalence shift`、`calibration`、`vendor claims`

👤 **作者**：Abdul Rahman Iqbal、Muhammad Fawad Nasim、Amir Saleem

- 🎯 **研究动机**：TDM 返回调用者定义选项的概率分布而 Jev 被供应商描述为校准——但部署中类频率变化使固定测试集上测得的校准在应用阈值处不必成立：供应商决策训练收益从未在相同读出下与骨干分离且 TDM 只在测试集类分布上测过
- 🔬 **研究方法**：三 TDM（Jev/Tev1-4B/Nimble-9B）+其基座+两训练分类器在五个公开数据集的相同决策上模拟偏移（35 个预注册配对测试）
- 📌 **结论**：决策训练非一致有益（Nimble 意图更校准但金融情感宏 F1 差 12 点）；中等偏移下 Jev 的 ECE 与阈值风险仅在金融情感上显著高于微调 DeBERTa（1.7/2.5 点）；EM 先验修正需要模型均值开发预测作先验——TDM 的患病率偏移审计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Typed decision models (TDMs) return a probability over caller-defined options, and Jev is described by its vendor as calibrated. But in deployment class frequencies change, so calibration measured on a fixed test set need not hold where thresholds are applied. Existing evaluations leave two gaps: the gains of vendor decision training have not been separated from those of the backbone under an identical readout, and TDMs have been tested only at the test set's class distribution. We ask whether decision training improves a model over its own backbone, and whether a TDM's calibration and thresholds withstand prevalence (label) shift as well as a fine-tuned classifier's. We compare three TDMs (Jev, Tev1-4B, Nimble-9B), their base models and two trained classifiers on identical decisions from five public datasets under simulated shift, with 35 paired tests fixed before any test was computed. First, decision training is not uniformly beneficial: Nimble-9B, which differs from Qwen3.5-9B only by a LoRA adapter, is better calibrated on intent tasks but 12.0 Macro-F1 points worse on financial sentiment. Second, under moderate shift Jev's expected calibration error (ECE) and threshold risk grow significantly more than a fine-tuned DeBERTa's only on financial sentiment, by 1.7 and 2.5 percentage points; averaged over 2,000 shift scenarios (exploratory), the gap is at most one point on every dataset. Third, EM prior correction with the model's mean development prediction as its prior needs no labels and lowers Jev's ECE under shift by 2.7 and 1.8 points on the two datasets where it is highest, although labelled recalibration lowers it far more. The three TDMs we tested are neither uniformly better than their backbones nor uniquely fragile under shift; for calibration, their main weakness is the miscalibration they already have without shift. We release all inputs, code and outputs.

</details>

### 167. Cartender: Verifiable Language-Conditioned Driving by Learning the Drives Not Taken

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26390)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`language-conditioned driving`、`counterfactual branching`、`instruction following`、`one-to-many`

👤 **作者**：Zhiwen Chen、Benjin Zhu、Yan Xie、Jun Ma

- 🎯 **研究动机**：每个驾驶场景只记录一次而驾驶是一对多的（场景决定哪些行为可行、指令在其中选择）——日志只记录采取的动作而对替代可行动作或场景不支持的指令无直接监督：忽略语言的策略与不顾场景约束遵循语言的策略拟合同样数据
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Cartender 从未采取的驾驶学习：反事实分支从真实录像构造匹配/失配指令-动作对（VLM 提议替代未来及其选择指令，不支持的指令作负标签）+学习的场景-语言兼容分调节语言条件化+奖励训练的语言残差重排序
- 📌 **结论**：WOD-E2E 上 3,603 可行指令条件 RFS 8.642；指令遵循经交换相同录像输入下的指令测行为而非与生成目标的一致性——语言条件化驾驶的反事实监督（指令权威的可验证化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Every driving scene is logged once, but driving is one-to-many: the scene determines which behaviors are feasible, while an instruction selects among them. A log records only the action taken, leaving no direct supervision for alternative feasible actions or instructions the scene cannot support. Consequently, a policy that ignores language and one that follows it without regard to scene constraints can fit the same data. We present Cartender, which learns from the drives not taken. Counterfactual Branching constructs matched and mismatched instruction-action pairs from real recordings without a simulator: a vision-language model proposes alternative futures and their selecting instructions, while unsupported instructions provide negative labels. A learned scene-language compatibility score regulates language conditioning in trajectory generation, and a reward-trained language residual re-ranks generated candidates over a scene-based score. The branches are used for training; instruction following is evaluated by swapping instructions under identical recorded inputs and measuring behavior rather than agreement with generated targets. On WOD-E2E, the best of four proposals achieves an RFS of 8.642 across 3,603 feasible instruction conditions. Across 8,974 admitted proposals, mean RFS is 8.203 versus 7.906 for matched logged trajectories, and 67% score higher than their logged counterpart. Compared with matched-only imitation using the same backbone, Cartender improves same-scene paired instruction success from 23.67% to 57.00% and RFS from 6.32 to 7.10; its gate reduces infeasible-instruction execution from 53.36% to 14.18%.

</details>

### 168. Where Does Rhetorical Distortion Live? A Multi-Platform, Multi-Language Measurement of Influencer Content

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26412)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`rhetorical distortion`、`influencer content`、`multi-platform`、`measurement`

👤 **作者**：Xucheng Yu、Jiangping Chen、Haohan Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：网红内容中的操纵多为不成比例而非虚假（准确事实被框定为历史性/紧急/灾难）——事实核查与标题党检测不为此设计而其来源（创作者/主题/平台/回复受众）所知甚少
- 🔬 **研究方法**：修辞扭曲经五个文献接地维度（意义膨胀/焦虑制造/新颖声明/ loaded 语言/时间扭曲）+三层管线（双语词汇规则提名→负过滤取消假阳→LLM 验证或重标）；六平台 330 账户 13,060 帖
- 📌 **结论**：平台比内容类别与账户级扭曲关联强得多（η²=0.49 vs 0.09）；X 最高（DI 7.74 vs RSS 0.78）；帖子下的回复层扭曲是其 3.5 倍（等长缩至 1.5 倍仍显著）——修辞扭曲的多平台测量（非虚假操纵的量化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Much of the manipulation in online influencer content is not false but disproportionate: an accurate fact is framed as historic, urgent, or catastrophic. Fact-checking and clickbait detection are not designed to capture such framing, and little is known about where it originates: with the creator, the topic, the platform, or the audience that replies. We operationalize rhetorical distortion through five literature-grounded dimensions (Significance Inflation, Anxiety Manufacturing, Novelty Claims, Loaded Language, and Temporal Distortion) and measure it with a three-tier pipeline in which bilingual lexical rules propose candidates, negative filters cancel known false-positive contexts, and an LLM verifies or relabels the candidates. Applying the pipeline to 13,060 posts from 330 accounts on six platforms (RSS/newsletters, YouTube, Bluesky, Reddit, Weibo, and X/Twitter), we find that platform is far more strongly associated with account-level distortion than content category (Kruskal-Wallis $\varepsilon^2=0.33$; two-way ANOVA $\eta_p^2=0.49$ for platform vs. $0.09$ for category). X/Twitter scores highest (DI 7.74 vs. 0.78 for RSS). Classifying tweets and their replies separately shows where this comes from: the reply layer readers see under a post (its top-5 replies) carries 3.5x the distortion of the tweet it answers (7.65 vs. 2.17, $p<10^{-6}$, 36 of 43 accounts), driven by Loaded Language. At equal text length the gap shrinks to 1.5x but remains significant, and creator tweets alone are statistically indistinguishable from YouTube and Bluesky. Two independent human annotators confirm the reply gap, and the platform ordering and the reply gap replicate with GPT-4o as a second classifier. On threaded platforms, measured distortion thus lives largely in the audience layer, which motivates measurement that separates creator and reply text.

</details>

### 169. Co-Evolving Policy and Reward for Code Generation: A Mechanistic Study of Single-Adapter Drift

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26416)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`policy-reward co-evolution`、`code generation`、`single-adapter drift`、`negative result`

👤 **作者**：Amarsaikhan Batjargal、Noriko Tomuro

- 🎯 **研究动机**：RLVR 特别适合代码生成（程序可经执行客观评）而执行也提供可复用作额外监督的行为信息——SPARK 式 rollout 回收训练模型同时生成与评估改进：该策略奖励共同演化是否迁移到代码生成？
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SPARK 式 rollout 回收 vs 仅执行 GRPO（Qwen2.5-Coder-3B）：共同演化改善训练性能但不泛化到 held-out 问题；rollout 回收产生约 34 倍于仅执行 GRPO 的参考策略 KL 散离
- 📌 **结论**：训练 token 从 185 降至 86 而训练通过率升、held-out 性能下降或不能维持早期增益——单适配器策略漂移刻画（共同演化的失效机制）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is particularly well suited to code generation because generated programs can be objectively evaluated through execution. Execution also provides behavioral information that can be reused as additional supervision. The Synergistic Policy and Reward Co-Evolving Framework (SPARK), originally proposed for reasoning tasks, exploits this idea by recycling training rollouts and their evaluation results to train a model not only to generate solutions but also to evaluate and improve them. We investigate whether this policy-reward co-evolution approach transfers effectively to code generation and whether execution-grounded reflection provides an additional source of self-improvement. We compare SPARK-style rollout recycling with execution-only Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) using Qwen2.5-Coder-3B-Instruct. Our results do not support either hypothesis under the configuration tested. Although co-evolution improves training performance, these gains do not generalize to held-out problems. Compared at the same stage of training, rollout recycling produces roughly 34x greater KL divergence from the reference policy than execution-only GRPO. Over training, trained tokens per GRPO sequence fall from 185 to 86 as training pass rate increases, while held-out performance declines or fails to sustain early gains. We characterize these patterns as single-adapter policy drift: the shared trainable parameters increasingly depart from pretrained code-generation behavior without corresponding gains in generalization. The results show that higher training reward alone is not evidence of generalizable code self-improvement and highlight the importance of held-out evaluation and drift diagnostics.

</details>

### 170. The Geometry of Agent Memory: Forecasting Retrieval-Bound Memory Failures, and Characterizing Its Limits

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26433)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`memory geometry`、`failure prediction`、`negative result`、`diagnostic limits`

👤 **作者**：Iliya Zhechev、…、Simba Khadder

- 🎯 **研究动机**：对话轮/记忆文本/查询间的几何关系能否在生成前预测答案正确性？——四记忆实现×稠密或混合检索嵌入共享空间+25 特征 logistic 分类器
- 🔬 **研究方法**：LongMemEval-small 池化 AUROC 0.640（低于问题难度基线 0.655）；单会话助手问题达 0.841 但系统身份单独 0.826；控制难度与身份后几何无统计确立的池化改进（delta +0.002, CI [-0.010,0.014]）
- 📌 **结论**：嵌入邻近不验证事实覆盖也不区分记忆构建选择——几何关联与可靠部署诊断间的差距（记忆失效预测的负结果与边界）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We study whether geometric relationships between conversation turns, memory texts, and queries can predict answer correctness before generation. We evaluate four memory implementations with dense or hybrid retrieval and one observation-summary comparator. We embed these texts in a shared space. A logistic classifier uses 25 features describing query proximity, memory-conversation correspondence, and conversation structure. On LongMemEval-small, pooled AUROC is 0.640 over 2,367 question-system pairs, below a question-difficulty baseline of 0.655. Performance varies by question type: geometry reaches 0.841 on single-session-assistant questions, but system identity alone reaches 0.826. After controlling for difficulty and identity, geometry adds no statistically established pooled improvement (delta = +0.002, 95% CI [-0.010, 0.014]). A fixed-store context-selection experiment finds higher accuracy with proximity-ranked memories than with random subsets, consistent with ordinary retrieval relevance. On LoCoMo, nearest-turn similarity has modest predictive value for single-hop questions and near-chance estimates for several other categories. Training uses correctness labels; prediction uses pre-answer features and no additional LLM generation call. The results characterize associations between memory representations and correctness, rather than establish a reliable deployment diagnostic. In particular, embedding proximity does not verify fact coverage or distinguish memory-construction, retrieval, and generation errors.

</details>

### 171. Reasoning-Aware Multicalibrated Reliability Predictors for Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26645)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`multicalibration`、`reliability predictors`、`CoT groups`、`hallucination`

👤 **作者**：Ekaterina Fedorova、Alessandro Morosini、Chara Podimata

- 🎯 **研究动机**：人们日益依赖 LLM 获取信息——提供者须传达单个响应是否正确的有意义估计：既有工作用多校准使预测跨应用相关组（提示主题）有意义，但主题组单独可隐藏系统误差（同主题同置信下不同 CoT 性质的响应可有相消的相反误差）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：推理感知多校准：主题组补充以 CoT 轨迹可观测性质导出的组；幻觉预测与不安全响应检测多基准验证
- 📌 **结论**：加入 CoT 组到基于主题的校准降低两任务 Brier 分；仅用 CoT 组（无主题）也已改善校准——CoT 性质分组的多校准可靠性预测器

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As people increasingly rely on large language models (LLMs) for information, model providers need to communicate meaningful estimates of whether individual responses are correct or, more generally, reliable. In the context of hallucinations, existing work uses multicalibration to make such reliability predictions meaningful across application-relevant groups, such as prompt topics. Topic groups alone, however, can conceal systematic errors: within the same topic and confidence level, responses with different chain-of-thought (CoT) properties may exhibit opposing errors that cancel on average. In this work, we introduce reasoning-aware multicalibration, a framework that supplements application-relevant topic groups with groups derived from observable properties of CoT traces. We highlight that, theoretically, groupings based on CoT are "internally important", i.e., groupings for which the multicalibration guarantee is not societally crucial as it is for high-risk topics, but whose inclusion in the multicalibration procedure could easily and significantly improve predictive accuracy of the final reliability predictor. Empirically, we apply our framework to hallucination prediction and unsafe-response detection across a variety of benchmarks, and find that adding CoT groups to topic-based calibration reduces Brier score in both tasks. Interestingly, we find that multicalibrating with respect to CoT groups alone (without any topic information) already improves calibration across topics, suggesting that the predictor's errors depend on how the model reasoned, rather than what it was asked about in the prompt.

</details>

### 172. RoboValue: A Fine-Grained Sim-and-Real Benchmark for Unified Evaluation of Robotic Value Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26663)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`robotic value models`、`sim-and-real`、`benchmark`、`progress monitoring`

👤 **作者**：Shengbang Liu、…、Chao Yu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：机器人价值模型从视觉观察与指令评估任务执行（为策略学习与执行监控提供反馈）——既有评测只覆盖执行理解的部分侧面而价值反馈的可靠性检验不足
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RoboValue：四互补能力维度（任务状态理解/时序进度监控/失败与恢复推理/价值一致性）共享接口；15 仿真+20 真实操纵任务+专家示范与标注轨迹；标准条件与机器人具身/环境偏移分开评
- 📌 **结论**：强结果与前向进展分的模型仍有细粒度指令接地/历史依赖进度追踪/恢复评估错误；同一语义子任务在有效执行顺序间价值增益差异巨大——价值监督的具体靶点（具身验证器可靠性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Robotic value models assess task execution from visual observations and instructions, providing feedback for policy learning and execution monitoring. However, existing evaluations cover only selected aspects of execution understanding, leaving the reliability of value feedback insufficiently tested. We introduce RoboValue, a fine-grained sim-and-real benchmark for unified evaluation of robotic value models. Its framework evaluates four complementary capability dimensions through shared interfaces: task-state understanding, temporal progress monitoring, failure and recovery reasoning, and value consistency. Diagnostic trajectories and instruction variations test whether value judgments accurately reflect task requirements and changes throughout execution. RoboValue-Dataset covers 15 simulation and 20 real-world manipulation tasks, providing expert demonstrations for training and annotated trajectories for fine-grained evaluation. We evaluate representative robotic value models in zero-shot and one-shot settings under standard conditions and separate shifts in robot embodiment and environment. Our results reveal errors in fine-grained instruction grounding, history-dependent progress tracking, and recovery assessment, even in models with strong outcome and forward-progress scores. The same semantic subtask can also receive substantially different value gains across valid execution orders. These findings identify concrete targets for value supervision, while the dataset and public leaderboard support continued assessment of feedback reliability. The website is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 173. Game of Phones: Harnessing Game Theory and LLMs in 'Bot Wars' to Counteract Phone Scams

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26781)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`scam-baiting`、`game theory`、`Nash equilibrium`、`defense`

👤 **作者**：Nardine Basta、Robin Carpentier、Benjamin Zi Hao Zhao、Dali Kaafar

- 🎯 **研究动机**：电话诈骗对个人与金融系统施加持续增长的成本——既有对策对行骗者不施加资源成本；战略 baiting（自动 agent 与骗子周旋耗尽其运营能力）提供主动替代但须静态系统不能提供的动态策略适应
- 🔬 **研究方法**：博弈论框架使 LLM 经上下文经验学习有效反策略（免微调）：骗子-baiter 交互为经验接地的两玩家博弈+纳什均衡作学习行为的形式基准；300 模拟对话×三 LLM 架构
- 📌 **结论**：自适应策略选择比非自适应基线对纳什均衡接近 28.9-79.7%——对抗电话诈骗的『Bot Wars』（主动防御的博弈化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Phone scams impose substantial and growing costs on individuals and financial systems worldwide, yet existing countermeasures impose no resource cost on perpetrators. Strategic scam-baiting, in which automated agents engage scammers to deplete their operational capacity, offers a proactive alternative but requires dynamic strategy adaptation that static systems cannot provide. We introduce a game-theoretic framework enabling LLMs to learn effective counter-strategies through in-context experience without fine-tuning. The framework models scammer-baiter interactions as a two-player game with empirically grounded strategies and utility functions, using Nash equilibrium as a formal benchmark for evaluating learned behaviour. Across 300 simulated dialogues and three LLM architectures, adaptive strategy selection achieves 28.9-79.7% closer alignment to Nash equilibrium than non-adaptive baselines. The code and an annotated dataset of 300 dialogues with turn-level strategy labels and utility scores are released at this https URL .

</details>

### 174. Where is Physical RSI capability from? An Observational Scaling Law of LLM-as-policy

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26848)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`physical RSI`、`scaling law`、`LLM-as-policy`、`barrel hypothesis`

👤 **作者**：Zekai Li、…、Li Yi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：系统研究物理递归自改进的机制/标度行为/泛化——PhysRSI-eval 以五轴度量（首试表现/效率/成功/预算/从失败改进）在固定交互预算下评 18 个操纵任务的各 VLM
- 🔬 **研究方法**：物理木桶假说（不同能力约束改进的不同部分）+两视角观测标度（从公开能力预测相对性能、从早期物理反馈预测后期进展）+工作区干预分析改进留下什么
- 📌 **结论**：五轴区分公开基准均分接近的系统（高分不必来自早期或高效增益）；移除历史降低后期成功、冻结工作区在未见布局仍有用——场景特异适应与可复用经验可共存（物理 RSI 的观测标度）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

In this paper, we systematically investigate the mechanisms, scaling behavior, and generalization of physical Recursive self-improvement. We introduce PhysRSI-eval to quantify physical RSI through a five-axis metric: First-Attempt Performance, Efficiency, Success, Budget and Improvement from failure. Different VLMs are evaluated on 18 manipulation tasks under a fixed interaction budget. The analysis of experimental results motivates the Physical Barrel Hypothesis: different capabilities may constrain different parts of improvement. The five-axis metrics also distinguish systems with close public benchmark averages and show that high scores need not come with early or efficient gains. We study observational scaling from two perspectives: predicting the relative performance of VLM agents from their public capabilities, and forecasting later progress from early physical feedback. Together, these analyses ask how quickly and how far an agent can improve. Moreover, we conduct workspace interventions and then analyze what improvement leaves behind. Removing history reduces late-round success, frozen workspaces remain useful on unseen layouts, and three cross-task cases achieve success earlier. The results suggest that scene-specific adaptation and reusable experience can coexist.

</details>
